nārāyaṇam namaskṛtya naram ca eva narottamam yaḥ śveta tvam upāgataḥ kṛta yuge tretā yuge rakta tām yugme yaḥ kapilaḥ kalau sa bhagavān kṛṣṇa tvam abhyāgataḥ kṛṣṇadvaipāyanam vyāsam viddhi nārāyaṇam svayam kaḥ anyaḥ hi puṇḍarīkākṣāt mahābhārata kṛt bhavet lakṣmīmaṭha nivāsaḥ aham gopāla ākhyaḥ dvijottamaḥ śrī kṛṣṇam tu namaskṛtya likhyāmi mukti kāmyayā śibirāt dhāstinapuram prāpya bhārata saṃjayaḥ praviveśa mahā prājñaḥ dhṛtarāṣṭra niveśanam śokena apahataḥ sūtaḥ vihvalan bhṛśa duḥkhitaḥ cintayan vaiśasam ghoram sūtaputrasya pāṇḍavaiḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ śara talpe mahātmānam śayānam aparājitam mad kṛte nidhanam prāptāḥ kim nu kaṣṭataram tatas idam tu dhārtarāṣṭrasya śreyaḥ manye janādhipa imām avasthām saṃprāptam yat asau tvām na paśyati nihataḥ kṣatra dharme asmin durātmā kula pāṃsanaḥ na sa paśyati duṣṭa ātmā tvām adya patitam kṣitau atas śreyaḥ mṛtam manye na iha jīvitam ātmanaḥ aham hi samare vīra gamitaḥ śatrubhiḥ kṣayam na tvām evam su duḥkha ārtam adrākṣam sāyaka arditam nūnam hi pāpa karmāṇaḥ dhātrā sṛṣṭāḥ sma he nṛpa hatvā ca enam na amṛtaḥ syāt ayam me na asya amṛta tvam bhavitā vai hate asmin tadvidhaḥ tad vaśe ca aham kālasya vaśagaḥ ciram rājā parama dhārmikaḥ sarva lokeṣu vikhyātaḥ su durjayaḥ iti khyātaḥ sarva śastrabhṛtām varaḥ ikṣvākoḥ tu śatam putrāḥ daśāśvaḥ daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ māhiṣmatyām abhūt rājā madirāśvaḥ tatas abhavat sā ca agni śaraṇe rājñaḥ śuśrūṣā kṛta niścayā niyuktā pitṛ saṃdeśāt ārirādhayiṣuḥ śikhim tasyāḥ manoramam rūpam dṛṣṭvā devaḥ hutāśanaḥ agniḥ manmathena samāviṣṭaḥ patnī tve yatate mithas rambhā ūru mṛgaśāvākṣi pūrṇa candra nibha ānane tava idam padma patra akṣam mukham dṛṣṭvā manoharam bhrū latā lalitam kāntam anaṅgaḥ bādhate hi mām lalāṭam candra lekhā ābham śiroruha vibhūṣitam dṛṣṭvā te patralekhā aṅkam anaṅgaḥ bādhate bhṛśam danta prākāra niyatā vāṇī tava su rakṣitā tāmra pallava saṃkāśā jihvā iyam te manoharā samāḥ snigdhāḥ sujātāḥ ca sahitāḥ ca dvijāḥ tava manojñam sukṛta apāṅgam mukham tava manoharam stanau te saṃhatau bhīru hāra ābharaṇa bhūṣitau bhīṣmaḥ īṣat ākampi hṛdayā vrīḍitā vākyam abravīt nanu nāma kulīnānām kanyakānām viśeṣataḥ mātā pitā prabhavataḥ pradāne bāndhavāḥ ca ye satām madhye niviṣṭāyāḥ kanyāyāḥ śaraṇam patiḥ sā aham na ātma vaśā deva pitaram varayasva me atha na ati cirāt kālāt rājā duryodhanaḥ kila yajña saṃbhāra nipuṇān mantrīn āhūya ca uktavān yajñam yakṣye aham iti vai saṃbhārāḥ saṃbhriyantu me vipra rūpī hutavahaḥ nṛpam kanyām ayācata atha dīkṣām upetasya yajñe tasya mahātmanaḥ āhitaḥ havana arthāya vedyām agniḥ praṇaśyata tatas sa bhītaḥ nṛpatiḥ bhṛśam pravyathita indriyaḥ mantriṇaḥ brāhmaṇān ca eva papraccha kim idam bhavet yajñe samiddhaḥ bhagavān naṣṭaḥ me havyavāhanaḥ stūyatām bhagavān vahniḥ brāhmaṇaiḥ vedapāragaiḥ ṛtvigbhiḥ mantra kuśalaiḥ gīyatām vai hutāśanaḥ atha ṛk sāma yajuṣām pāragaiḥ vedapāragaiḥ guhyeṣu nāmadheyeṣu kīrtyamāneṣu pāvakaḥ sudarśanaḥ tu rūpeṇa pūrṇa indu sadṛśa upamaḥ dṛṣṭvā kadā cit svām bhāryām ṛcīkaḥ tu abravīt vacaḥ vṛkṣayoḥ api vyatyāsam kuryāva varavarṇini atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam vāsavasya ca saṃvādam śukasya ca mahātmanaḥ jānan api ca tad prāptim pṛcchate pākaśāsanaḥ brahmā bījataḥ hi aṅkura utpattiḥ aṅkurāt parṇa saṃbhavaḥ parṇāt nālāḥ prasūyante nālāt skandhaḥ prasūyate phalāt nivartate bījam bījāt syāt saṃbhavaḥ punar hanta te kathayiṣyāmi yat mām pṛcchasi bhārata tad pāṭha dhāraṇāt svargam artha jñānāt parām gatim vitṛṣṇānām veda japāt svarga mokṣa phalam smṛtam yathā eva te namaskāryāḥ proktāḥ śakreṇa mānada tathā eva mayi ca ācakṣva yeṣām spṛhayase nṛpa jñāna vijñāna yuktānām teṣām ca spṛhayāmi aham brāhmaṇa anujñayā grāhyam rājyam ca sa purohitaiḥ dūratas mātṛ vat pūjyāḥ vipra dārāḥ su rakṣayā bhīṣmaḥ atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam ṛgvedaḥ caturṇām api vedānām saṃvādam putra tat śṛṇu gṛhān āśrayamāṇasya agnihotram ca juhvataḥ sarvam sukṛtam ādatte yaḥ sāye nudyate atithiḥ sāmavedaḥ na brāhmaṇam naḥ apavādam karoti bhīṣmaḥ bhūyas tu śṛṇu rāja indra dharmān dharma bhṛtām vara atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam indraḥ indra agnyoḥ sūrya śacyoḥ ca tat me nigadataḥ śṛṇu agniḥ yathā aham tatra na aśnāmi yatra na aśnanti vai dvijāḥ sūryaḥ yathā go brāhmaṇasya arthe na tapāmi yathābalam śacī karmaṇā manasā vācā na avamanye puraṃdaram bhīṣmaḥ atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam madra rājasya saṃvādam vyāsasya ca mahātmanaḥ madra rājaḥ uktaḥ bhagavān yajña arthe tathā tatra aśṛṇot bhavān kāni tīrthāni bhagavan phala arthāḥ ca iha ke kaḥ ijyate kaḥ ca yajñaḥ kaḥ yūpaḥ kramate ca kaḥ kaḥ ca adhvare śasyate gīti śabdaiḥ kaḥ ca adhvare gīyate valgu bhāṣaiḥ varṇa āśrame go phale kaḥ ca some kaḥ ca oṃkāraḥ kaḥ ca veda artha mārgaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ tat me brūhi sarvam mahā ṛṣe dvaipāyanaḥ loka jyeṣṭham yasya vijñānam āhuḥ loka jyeṣṭham yasya vijñānam āhuḥ pūta ātmānaḥ brāhmaṇāḥ veda mukhyā brāhmaṇaḥ asmin praśnaḥ dīyatām keśavāya bālaḥ jātaḥ devakyām śūra putreṇa vīra vāsudevaḥ dāśārhāṇām uttamaḥ puṣkarākṣaḥ pārāśarya brūhi yat brāhmaṇebhyaḥ prīta ātmā vai brahma kalpaḥ sumedhāḥ pṛṣṭaḥ yajña artham pāṇḍavasya ati tejāḥ vyāsaḥ uktam vākyam yat bhavān mām avocat chettum vispaṣṭam tiṣṭhati tvadvidhe vai vāsudevaḥ loka jyeṣṭhe viśva rūpe su nābhe tat tvam vākyam brūhi yat tvam mahā ṛṣe yasmin kṛṣṇaḥ procyate vai yathāvat prītaḥ te aham jñāna śaktyā yathāvat vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ tasmāt nirdeśe karmaṇām brūhi siddhim ukta vākye sattame yādavānām kṛṣṇaḥ vyāsaḥ prāñjaliḥ vāsudevam vipraiḥ sārdham pūjayan devadevam kṛṣṇam viṣṇum vāsudevam babhāṣe vyāsaḥ ānantyam te viśvakarman tava evam rūpam paurāṇam śāśvatam ca dhruvam ca bhīṣmaḥ dvārakāyām yathā prāha purā ayam muni sattamaḥ yūpam viṣṇum vāsudevam vijānan viṣṇum krāntam vāsudevam vijānan viṣṇuḥ yajñaḥ tu ijyate ca api viṣṇuḥ kṛṣṇaḥ viṣṇuḥ yaḥ ca kṛtsnaḥ prabhuḥ ca kṛṣṇaḥ vedāṅgam veda vādāḥ ca kṛṣṇa evam jānan brāhmaṇaḥ brahma eti sthānam sarvam vaiṣṇavam yajña mārge cāturhotram vaiṣṇavam tatra kṛṣṇaḥ puṇyān lokān brāhmaṇāḥ prāpnuvanti somam sadbhāvāt ye ca jātam pibanti dīptam karma ye vidānāḥ caranti te vai sthānam prapnuvanti vrata jñāḥ durgam panthānam pāpa karmā api vipraḥ sarvam kṛṣṇam vāsudevam hi viprāḥ kṛtvā dhyānam durgatim na prayānti ājyam yajñaḥ sruc sruvau yajña dātā icchā patnī patniśālā havīṃṣi idhmāḥ puroḍāśaḥ sarvadā hotṛ kartā kṛtsnam viṣṇum saṃvijānan tam eti yoge yoge karmaṇām ca abhihāre puṣṭi artheṣu prāpnuyāt karma siddhim dvaipāyanaḥ śānti artheṣu prāpnuyāt sarva śāntim śraddhām tyāgam nirvṛtim ca api pūjām satyam dharmam yaḥ kṛtam ca abhyupaiti śraddhā pūtaḥ sarva yajñeṣu yogyaḥ yasmin yajñe sarva bhūtāḥ prahṛṣṭāḥ sarve ca ārambhāḥ śāstra dṛṣṭāḥ pravṛttāḥ eka akṣaram dvi akṣaram ekam eva sadā yajante niyatāḥ pratītāḥ dṛṣṭvā manāk arcayitvā sma viprāḥ satām mārgam tam dhruvam saṃbhajante pāpa ātmānaḥ krodha rāga abhibhūtāḥ kṛṣṇe bhaktāḥ nāma saṃkīrtayantaḥ pūta ātmānaḥ yajña śīlāḥ sumedhā ekaḥ vedaḥ brāhmaṇānām babhūva catuṣpādaḥ traiguṇaḥ brahma śīrṣaḥ pādam pādam brāhmaṇāḥ vedam āhuḥ dvaipāyanaḥ tretā kāle tam ca tam viddhi śīrṣam sarve vedāḥ sarva vedyāḥ sa śāstrāḥ sarve yajñāḥ sarve ijyāḥ ca kṛṣṇaḥ viduḥ kṛṣṇam brāhmaṇāḥ tattvataḥ ye teṣām rājan sarva yajñāḥ samāptāḥ āraṇyakaiḥ sa eṣa kṛṣṇaḥ prabhu tvāt sarvān yajñān brāhmaṇān brahma ca eva saḥ eṣa devaḥ śakram īśam yajānam prītyā prāha kratu yaṣṭāram agryam bhaktaḥ bhaktyā śuddha bhāva pradhānaḥ mām jānante brahma śīrṣam variṣṭham sarvatra aham śāśvataḥ śāśvata īśaḥ tatas buddhyā mārgamāṇāḥ tanūnām sarvān kāmān prāpnuvante viśālān trailokye asmin kṛṣṇa nāma abhidhānāt kṛṣṇaḥ yajñaiḥ ijyate yāyajūkaiḥ kṛṣṇaḥ vīraiḥ ijyate vikramadbhiḥ kṛṣṇaḥ vākyaiḥ ijyate saṃmṛśānaiḥ kṛṣṇaḥ muktaiḥ ijyate vīta mohaiḥ vidyāvantaḥ soma pāḥ ye vipāpā iṣṭvā yajñaiḥ gocaram prārthayante sarvam krāntam deva lokam viśālam evam sarve tu āśramāḥ su vratāḥ ye mām jānantaḥ yānti lokān adīnān yām yām dīkṣām udvahante vipāpā pūjyante mām pūjayantaḥ prahṛṣṭā mām jānantaḥ śraddhayā vāsudevam dvaipāyanaḥ sattva spṛṣṭaḥ vīta mohaḥ ayam eti jyotīṃṣi śuklāni ca yāni loke trayaḥ lokāḥ lokapālāḥ trayī ca bhīṣmaḥ vyāsasya etat vacaḥ śrutvā madra rājaḥ saha ṛṣibhiḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ vyāsam kṛṣṇam ca vidhivat prīta ātmā pratyapūjayat jagāma pṛthvīm śirasā mahātmā namaḥ ca kṛṣṇāya cakāra bhīṣmaḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ garuḍaḥ pakṣiṇām śreṣṭhaḥ iti pūrvam pitāmaha uktaḥ tvayā mahā bāho śvetavāham praśaṃsatā atra kautūhalam me asti śravaṇe jāyate matiḥ katham garutmān pakṣīṇām śraiṣṭhyam prāptaḥ paraṃtapa kiṃvīryaḥ kiṃbalaḥ ca asau vaktum arhasi bhārata bhīṣmaḥ vāsudeva mahā bāho devakī su prajāḥ tvayā suparṇam śaṃse iti eva mām āha kuru nandanaḥ tvam hi śaure mahā bāho suparṇaḥ procyase purā an ādi nidhane kāle garuḍaḥ ca asi keśava tasmāt pūrvam prasādya tvām dharmaputrāya dhīmate vāsudevaḥ garuḍam patatām śreṣṭham vaktum icchāmi mādhava bhīṣmaḥ tathā ca garuḍaḥ rājan tathā asmai brūhi tattvataḥ yudhiṣṭhira mahā bāho śṛṇu rājan yathātatham garuḍam pakṣiṇām śreṣṭham vainateyam mahā balam tathā ca garuḍaḥ rājan suparṇaḥ ca yathā bhavat yathā ca bhujagān hanti tathā me bruvataḥ śṛṇu purā aham tāta rāmeṇa jāmadagnyena dhīmatā tam aham tāta dṛṣṭvā eva śastrāṇi utsṛjya sarvaśas abhivādya pūrvam rāmāya vinayena upatasthivān apṛccham sa ca mām prītaḥ pratyuvāca yudhiṣṭhira kadrūḥ ca vinatā ca āstām prajāpati sute ubhe te dharmeṇa upayeme atha mārīcaḥ kaśyapaḥ prabhuḥ prādāt tābhyām varam prītaḥ bhāryābhyām su mahā tapāḥ tatra kadrūḥ varam vavre putrāṇām daśataḥ śatam tulya tejaḥ prabhāvānām sarveṣām tulya janmanām sa tu tābhyām varam prādāt tathā iti uktvā mahā tapāḥ janayām āsa tān putrān tābhyām āsīt yathā purā kadrūḥ prajajñe tu aṇḍānām tathā eva daśataḥ śatam aṇḍe dve vinatā ca eva darśanīyatare śubhe tāni tu aṇḍāni tu tayoḥ kadrū vinatayoḥ dvayoḥ sa upasvedeṣu pātreṣu nidadhuḥ paricāriṇaḥ niḥsaranti tadā aṇḍebhyaḥ kadru putrāḥ bhujaṃgamāḥ pañca varṣa śate kāle dṛṣṭa śvāsa bala ojasaḥ vinatā teṣu jāteṣu pannageṣu mahātmasu viputrā putra saṃtāpāt aṇḍam ekam bibheda ha na hi pañcaśate kāle purā putrau dadarśa sā sā apaśyat aṇḍāt niṣkrāntam vinā patram manasvinam dṛṣṭvā tu tam tathārūpam asamagra śarīriṇam putra duḥkha anvitā aśocat sa ca pakṣī tathā gataḥ mātaram capalā asi iti hataḥ aham iti ca asakṛt na tvayā kāṅkṣitaḥ kālaḥ yāvān eva atyagāt purā āvām bhavāva putrau te śvasanāt balavattarau īrṣyā krodha abhibhūta tvāt yaḥ aham evam kṛtaḥ tvayā tasmāt tvam api me mātar dāsī bhāvam gamiṣyasi pañca varṣa śatāni tvam spardhase vai yayā saha dāsī tasyāḥ bhavitrī iti sa aśru pātam uvāca ha eṣa ca eva mahābhāge balī balavatām varaḥ bhīṣmaḥ mokṣayiṣyati te mātar dāsī bhāvāt mama anujaḥ vinatā putra śoka ārtā śāpāt bhītā ca bhārata pratīkṣate sma tam kālam yaḥ putra uktaḥ tadā abhavat tatas api atīte pañcaśate varṣāṇām kāla saṃyuge bandhura āsyaḥ śikhī patra kośaḥ kūrma nakhaḥ mahān rakta akṣaḥ saṃhata grīvaḥ hrasva pādaḥ mahā śirāḥ vinatā pūrvajaḥ putraḥ saḥ aruṇaḥ dṛśyate divi aruṇaḥ aruṇa saṃkāśaḥ nāmnā ca eva aruṇaḥ smṛtaḥ jāta mātraḥ tu vihagaḥ garuḍaḥ pannagāśanaḥ sa tathā ca ativavṛdhe sarva kāmaiḥ atha arcitaḥ pitāmaha visṛṣṭena bhojanena viśām pate kadrūḥ ca vinatā ca eva gacchetām sāgaram prati dadṛśāte tu te yāntam uccaiḥśravasam antikāt snātvā upavṛttam tvaritam pītavantam ca vājinam vinatā kadrūḥ varṇam vā kīdṛśam tasya manyase tvam manasvini vinatā brūhi kalyāṇi dīvyāvaḥ varṇa anya tvena bhāmini yadi ārye dīvyasi tvam me kaḥ paṇaḥ naḥ bhaviṣyati kadrūḥ sā tat jñātvā paṇeyam vai jñātvā tu vipaṇe tvayā jitā dāsī bhaveḥ me tvam aham ca api asita īkṣaṇe rocate me paṇaḥ rājñi dāsī tvena na saṃśayaḥ bhīṣmaḥ satyam ātiṣṭha bhadram te satye sthāsyāmi ca api aham vinatā tu tathā iti uktvā kṛta saṃśayanā paṇe mayā kṛtaḥ paṇaḥ putrāḥ mithas vinatayā saha uccaiḥśravasi gāndharve tat śṛṇudhvam bhujaṃgamāḥ abravam na eka varṇam tam sā eka varṇam atha abravīt jitā dāsī bhavet putrāḥ sā vā aham vā na saṃśayaḥ sā aham dāsī bhaviṣyāmi jitā putrā na saṃśayaḥ te yūyam aśva pravaram āviśadhvam atandritāḥ sarpāḥ sarva śvetam vāladhiṣu vālāḥ bhūtvā añjana prabhāḥ nikṛtyā na jayaḥ śreyān mātar satyāḥ giraḥ śṛṇu nikṛtyā vijayam mātar mā sma kārṣīḥ katham cana yadi adharmeṇa vijayam vayam kāṅkṣāmahe kva cit sā tvam asmān api sataḥ vipāpān ṛju buddhinaḥ kalmaṣeṇa abhisaṃyoktum kāṅkṣase lobha mohitā te vayam tvām parityajya draviṣyāma diśaḥ daśa yatra vākyam na te mātar punar śroṣyāmaḥ īdṛśam kadrūḥ śṛṇomi vividhāḥ vācaḥ hetumatyaḥ samīritāḥ na ca tat paṇitam mandāḥ śakyam jetum atas anyathā jite nikṛtyā śrutvā etat kṣamam kuruta putrakāḥ śvas aham prabhāta samaye jitāḥ dharmeṇa putrakāḥ śailūṣiṇī bhaviṣyāmi vinatāyāḥ na saṃśayaḥ iha ca amutra ca arthāya putrān icchanti mātaraḥ sā iyam īhā vipannā me yuṣmān āsādya saṃgatām iha vā tārayet putraḥ pretya vā tārayet pitṝn na atra citram bhavet kim cit punāti iti ca putra tā te yūyam tāraṇa arthāya mama putrāḥ manaḥ javāḥ āviśadhvam haya śreṣṭham vālāḥ bhūtvā añjana prabhāḥ jānāmi adharmam sakalam vijitā vinatā bhavet nikṛtyā dāsa bhāvaḥ tu yuṣmān api avapīḍayet nikṛtyā vijayaḥ vā iti dāsa tvam vā parājaye ubhayam niścayam kṛtvā jayaḥ vai dhārmikaḥ varaḥ bhīṣmaḥ śrutvā tu vacanam mātuḥ kruddhāyāḥ te bhujaṃgamāḥ kṛcchreṇa eva anvamodanta ke cit hitvā diśaḥ gatāḥ bhujaṃgamānām mātā asau kadrūḥ vaira karī tadā kadrūḥ utpatsyati hi rājanyaḥ pāṇḍavaḥ janamejayaḥ caturthaḥ dhanvinām śreṣṭhāt kuntī putrāt dhanaṃjayāt evam kruddhā aśapat mātā pannagān dharma cāriṇaḥ evam śaptāḥ diśaḥ prāptāḥ pannagāḥ dharma cāriṇaḥ vihāya mātaram kruddhā gatāḥ vaira karīm tadā tatra ye vṛjinam tasyāḥ an āpannāḥ bhujaṃgamāḥ te tasya vājinaḥ vālāḥ babhūvuḥ asita prabhāḥ tān dṛṣṭvā vāladhi sthān ca putrān kadrūḥ atha abravīt vinatām atha saṃhṛṣṭā hayaḥ asau dṛśyatām iti udakāt uttarantam tam hayam ca eva ca bhāminī abravīt bhaginīm kim cit vihasantī iva bhārata hanta paśyāva gacchāvaḥ su kṛtaḥ nau paṇaḥ śubhe dāsī vā te bhaviṣyāmi tvam vā dāsī bhaviṣyasi evam sthiram paṇam kṛtvā hayam te tu dadarśatuḥ kṛtvā sākṣiṇam ātmānam bhaginyau kurusattama sā dṛṣṭvā eva hayam mandam vinatā śoka karśitā śvetam candra aṃśu vālam tam kāla vālam manojavam tatra sā vrīlitā vākyam vinatā sa aśru bindukā uvāca kāla vālaḥ ayam turagaḥ vijitam tvayā dāsī mām preṣayasva ārye yathākāma vaśām śubhe bhīṣmaḥ dāsyaḥ ca kāma kārāḥ hi bhartṝṇām na atra saṃśayaḥ tatas kadrūḥ hasantī iva vinatām dharma cāriṇīm dāsī vat preṣayām āsa sā ca sarvam cakāra tat na vivarṇā na saṃkṣubdhā na ca kruddhā na duḥkhitā preṣya karma cakāra asyāḥ vinatā kamala īkṣaṇā imāḥ diśaḥ catasraḥ asyāḥ preṣya bhāvena vartitāḥ tam darpa vaśam āpannam paridhāvantam antikāt balena dṛptaḥ satatam ahaṃmāna kṛtaḥ sadā dāsīm pannaga rājasya mātuḥ antargṛhe satīm janayitrī mayi sute jātā dāsī tapasvinī athā bravīt ṛṣiḥ vākyam dīvyatī vijitā khaga garuḍaḥ katham jitā nikṛtyā sā bhagavan jananī mama brūhi tat me yathāvṛttam śrutvā vetsye tatas param ācakhyau bharata śreṣṭha yathāvṛttam patatriṇaḥ tatas tu roṣāt duḥkhāt ca tūrṇam utpatya pakṣirāj tatra apaśyat tatas dīnām jaṭilām malinām kṛśām toyadena praticchannām sūrya ābhām iva mātaram tasya duḥkhāt ca roṣāt ca netrābhyām aśru ca asravat pravṛttim ca nivṛttim ca pauruṣe pratitasthuṣaḥ an uktvā mātaram kim cit patatri vara puṃgavaḥ kadrūm eva sa dharma ātmā vacanam pratyabhāṣata jyeṣṭhā tvam asi me mātā dharmaḥ sarvaḥ sa me mataḥ iyam tu me syāt kṛpaṇā mayi putre amba duḥkhitā anujānīhi tām sādhu mad kṛte dharma darśinī kadrūḥ śrutvā asya tat vākyam vainateyasya dhīmataḥ uvāca vākyam duṣprajñā parītā duḥkha mūrchitā kuryām priyam aniṣṭa ātmā mām bravīṣi khaga dvija tām tadā bruvatīm vākyam aniṣṭām krūra bhāṣiṇīm dāruṇām sūnṛtābhiḥ tām anunetum pracakrame garuḍaḥ jyeṣṭhā tvam asi kalyāṇi mātuḥ me bhāmini priyā kadrūḥ sodaryā mama ca asi tvam jyeṣṭhā mātā na saṃśayaḥ sūnṛtābhiḥ tvayā mātā na a dāsī śakyam aṇḍaja amṛtam yadi āhareḥ tvam vihaṃga jananīm tava bhīṣmaḥ adāsīm mama paśya imām vainateya na saṃśayaḥ amṛta āharaṇe vācam tatas pitaram abravīt kāmam vai sūnṛtā vācaḥ visṛjya ca muhur muhur yat ca api anujñām mātuḥ vai na ca sā hi anumanyate sā mā bahuvidhāḥ vācaḥ vajra kalpāḥ visṛjya vai bhagavan vinatā dāsī mama mātā mahā dyute kadrūḥ preṣayate ca eva dāsīyam iti ca abravīt āhara amṛtam iti eva mokṣyate vinatā tatas saḥ aham mātuḥ vimokṣa artham āhariṣye iti bruvan kaśyapaḥ amṛtam prārthitaḥ tūrṇam āhartum pratinandya vai amṛtam tāta duṣprāpam devaiḥ api kutas tvayā tatas śata sahasrāṇi prayutāni arbudāni ca rakṣanti amṛtam atyartham kiṃkarāḥ nāma rākṣasāḥ teṣām śakti ṛṣṭi śūlān ca śataghnyaḥ paṭṭasāḥ tathā āyudhāḥ rakṣiṇām tāta vajra kalpāḥ śilāḥ tathā ayasmayena vai tāta vṛtrahantuḥ sma śāsanāt garuḍaḥ putra gṛddhyā bravīṣi etat śṛṇu tāta viniścayam balavān upāyavān asmi bhūyas kim karavāṇi te tam abravīt pitā hṛṣṭaḥ prahasan vai punar punar tathā balam ameyam te bhavitā tat na saṃśayaḥ garuḍaḥ amṛtasya eva ca āhartā bhaviṣyasi na saṃśayaḥ bhakṣayiṣyāmi aham tāta balasya āpyāyanam prati kaśyapaḥ uragaḥ nāma duṣprāpaḥ purā deva gaṇaiḥ api gorutāni sa vistīrṇaḥ puṣpita druma sānumān tatra panthāḥ kṛtaḥ tāta kuñjareṇa balīyasā gacchatā a gacchatā ca eva kṣapitaḥ sa mahā giriḥ tāvān bhūmi samaḥ tāta kṛtaḥ panthāḥ samutthitaḥ tam atītya tu śaila indram hradaḥ kokanada āyutaḥ gorutāni sa vistīrṇaḥ kacchapaḥ kuñjaraḥ ca saḥ yadā sa nāgaḥ vrajati pipāsuḥ tam jalāśayam tadā enam kacchapaḥ roṣāt pratiyāti mahā balaḥ virarāda agra hastena kuñjaram tam jalecaraḥ nāga rāj api toya arthī pipāsuḥ caraṇaiḥ api pipāsuḥ klinna hasta agraḥ rudhireṇa samukṣitaḥ garuḍaḥ kaśyapaḥ uttiṣṭhati jalāt tūrṇam yoddhu kāmaḥ punar punar jalajam nirjalam tāta pramattam ca eva vāraṇam bhīṣmaḥ tām sa nāgaḥ gireḥ vīthim saṃprāptaḥ iva bhārata sa tam dṛṣṭvā mahābhāgaḥ saṃprahṛṣṭa tanūruhaḥ bibhakṣayiṣataḥ rājan dāruṇasya mahātmanaḥ mātaṃgam kacchapam ca eva praharṣaḥ su mahān abhūt saṃkucya sarva gātrāṇi kṛcchreṇa eva anvapadyata tathā gatvā tam adhvānam vāraṇa pravaraḥ balī niśaśvāsa mahā śvāsam śramāt viśramaṇāya ca udatiṣṭhat mahā kūrmaḥ vāraṇa pratiṣedhakaḥ tayoḥ su tumulam yuddham dadarśa pataga īśvaraḥ spṛśantam agra hastena toyam vāraṇa yūthapam jalajam vāraṇaḥ api evam caraṇaiḥ puṣkareṇa ca toya gṛdhnuḥ śanais tarṣāt apāsarpata pṛṣṭhatas tam dṛṣṭvā jalajam tūrṇam apasarpantam āhavāt tam roṣāt sthalam uttīrṇam a saṃprāptam gaja uttamam utpapāta tatas tūrṇam pannaga indra niṣūdanaḥ divam kham ca samāvṛtya pakṣābhyām aparājitaḥ tena ca utpatatā tūrṇam saṃgṛhītau nakhaiḥ bhṛśam vajra garbhaiḥ su niśitaiḥ prāṇān tūrṇam mumocatuḥ tau gṛhya balavān tūrṇam srasta pāda śirodharau attu kāmaḥ tatas vīraḥ pṛthivyām pṛthivīpate naimiṣam tu atha saṃprāpya deva araṇyam mahā dyutiḥ apaśyata drumam kam cit śākhā skandha samāvṛtam pariṇāhena rāja indra nalva mātram samantataḥ tasya śākhā bhavat kā cit āyatā pañca yojanam dṛḍha mūlā dṛḍha skandhā vajra patra samācitā attu kāmaḥ tatas śākhām tasya vegāt avāpatat tām patantīm abhiprekṣya prekṣya ca ṛṣi gaṇān atha āsīnān vasubhiḥ sārdham satreṇa jagatīpate vaikhānasān nāma yatīn vālakhilya gaṇān api tān dṛṣṭvā sa yatīn tatra samāsīnān suraiḥ saha tuṇḍena gṛhya tām śākhām utpapāta khageśvaraḥ tau ca pakṣī bhujaṃga āśaḥ vyomni krīḍan iva avrajat tam dṛṣṭvā guru saṃbhāram pragṛhya utpatitam khagam na tu anyaḥ kṣamate kaḥ cit yathā ayam vīryavān khagaḥ ayam krīḍan iva ākāśe tasmāt garuḍaḥ eva saḥ akārṣuḥ pakṣirājasya garuḍa iti eva nāma ha sa pakṣī pṛthivīm sarvām paridhāvan tatas tatas mumukṣuḥ śākhinaḥ śākhām na sma deśam apaśyata sa vācam aśṛṇot divyām upari upari jalpataḥ deva dūtasya vispaṣṭam ābhāṣya garuḍa iti ca pātyatām śākhinaḥ śākhā na hi te dharma niścayaḥ tat śrutvā garuḍaḥ tūrṇam jagāma lavaṇāmbhasaḥ uddeśam yatra te mandāḥ kuvindāḥ pāpa karmiṇaḥ tatas gatvā tatas śākhām mumoca patatām varaḥ tayā hatāḥ janapadāḥ tadā ṣaṭtriṃśataḥ nṛpa sa deśaḥ rāja śārdūla khyātaḥ parama dāruṇaḥ bhīṣmaḥ śākhāpatagaḥ iti eva kuvindānām durātmanām hatvā tam pakṣi śārdūlaḥ kuvindānām jana vrajam upopaviśya śaila agre bhakṣayām āsa tau ubhau vāraṇam kacchapam ca eva saṃhṛṣṭaḥ sa patatri rāj tayoḥ sa rudhiram pītvā medasī ca paraṃtapa saṃhṛṣṭaḥ patatām śreṣṭhaḥ labdhvā balam anuttamam jagāma devarājasya bhavanam pannagāśanaḥ tam praṇamya mahātmānam pāvakam visphuliṅginam rātriṃdivam prajvalitam rakṣā artham amṛtasya ha tam dṛṣṭvā vihaga indrasya bhayam tīvram atha āviśat pakṣi tvam ātmanaḥ dṛṣṭvā jvalantam ca hutāśanam pitāmaham atho gatvā dadarśa bhujagāśanaḥ tam praṇamya mahātmānam garuḍaḥ prayata añjaliḥ provāca tat asaṃdigdham vacanam pataga īśvaraḥ vimokṣaṇa artham mātuḥ hi dāsa bhāvāt aninditam kadrū sakāśam amṛtam mayā hartavyam īśvara tadā me jananī deva dāsa bhāvāt pramokṣyate tatra amṛtam prajvalitaḥ nityam īśvara rakṣati hiraṇyaretāḥ bhagavān pākaśāsana śāsanāt jvalantam pāvakam dṛṣṭvā pakṣi tvam ca ātmanaḥ prabho samatikramitum śakyaḥ katham syāt pāvakaḥ mayā tam abravīt mahābhāga tapyamānam vihaṃgamam agneḥ saṃśamana upāyam utsmayan sa punar punar payasā śāmyate vatsa sarpiṣā ca hutāśanaḥ śarīra sthaḥ api bhūtānām kim punar prajvalan bhuvi navanītam payaḥ vā api pāvake tvam samādadheḥ bhīṣmaḥ tatas gaccha yathākāmam na tvā dhakṣyati pāvakaḥ pitāmaha vacaḥ śrutvā garuḍaḥ patatām varaḥ jagāma gokulam kim cit navanīta jihīrṣayā tam atītya āśu garuḍaḥ hṛṣṭa ātmā jātavedasam rakṣāṃsi samatikrāmat pakṣa vātena pātayan te patanti śirobhiḥ ca jānubhiḥ caraṇaiḥ tathā utsṛjya śastra āvaraṇam pakṣi pakṣa samāhatāḥ utplutya ca āvṛtān nāgān hatvā cakram vyatītya ca ara antareṇa śirasā bhittvā jālam samādravat sa bhittvā śirasā jālam vajra vega samaḥ balī ujjahāra tatas śīghram amṛtam bhujagāśanaḥ tat ādāya adravat śīghram garuḍaḥ śvasanaḥ yathā tatas mātali saṃyuktam haribhiḥ svarṇa mālibhiḥ āruroha ratham śīghram sūrya agni sama tejasam saḥ abhyadravat pakṣirājam vṛtrahā pākaśāsanaḥ tatas vai mātalim prāha śīghram vāhaya vājinaḥ utsisṛkṣantam ājñāya vajram vai tridaśeśvaram tūrṇam vegataraḥ bhūtvā jagāma patatām varaḥ pitāmaha nisargeṇa jñātvā labdha varaḥ khagaḥ āyudhānām varam vajram atha śakram uvāca ha atas saṃmānam ākāṅkṣan muñcāmi ekam tanūruham hanyāḥ tvam parayā śaktyā gacchāmi aham anāmayaḥ tat tu tūrṇam tadā vajram svena vegena bhārata jaghāna parayā śaktyā na ca enam adahat bhṛśam dṛṣṭvā vajram viṣaktam tam pakṣiparṇe abruvan vacaḥ suparṇaḥ pakṣi garuḍaḥ yasya parṇe vara āyudham viṣaktam devarājasya vṛtrahantuḥ sanātanam ṛṣayaḥ tam vijānanti ca āgneyam vaiṣṇavam punar tanu parṇam suparṇasya jagṛhuḥ barhiṇaḥ tathā bhīṣmaḥ mayūrāḥ vismitāḥ sarve ādravanti sma vajriṇam tatas vajram sahasrākṣaḥ dṛṣṭvā saktam vara āyudham ṛṣīn ca dṛṣṭvā sahasā suparṇam idam abravīt iti ukte na mayā rakṣā śakyā kartum atas anyathā idam vajram mayā sārdham nivṛttam hi yathāgatam garuḍaḥ kadrūm abhyāgamat tūrṇam amṛtam gṛhya bhārata tat āhṛtam mayā śīghram amṛtam jananīkṛte kadrūḥ adāsī sā bhavatu adya vinatā dharma cāriṇī svāgatam su āhṛtam ca idam amṛtam vinatā ātmaja adāsī jananī te adya putra kāma vaśā śubhā evam ukte tadā sā ca saṃprāptā vinatā gṛham upanīya yathānyāyam vihagaḥ balinām varaḥ smṛtvā nikṛtyā vijayam mātuḥ saṃpratipadyata vadham ca bhujaga indrāṇām ye vālāḥ tasya vājinaḥ babhūvuḥ asita prakhyāḥ nikṛtyā vai jitam tvayā tām uvāca tatas nyāyam vihagaḥ balinām varaḥ ujjahāra amṛtam tūrṇam utpapāta ca raṃhasā tat gṛhītvā amṛtam tūrṇam prayāntam aparājitam punar harasi durbuddhe mā jātu vṛjinam kṛthāḥ suparṇaḥ amṛta āharaṇam me adya yat kṛtam jananī kṛte āhṛtam tat idam śīghram muktā ca jananī mama yadi mām bhavatī brūyāt amṛtam me ca dīyatām tathā kuryām na vā kuryām na hi tvam amṛta kṣamā bhīṣmaḥ idam mayā vṛtra hantar hṛtam te amṛtam uttamam mātṛ arthe hi tathā eva idam gṛhāṇa amṛtam āhṛtam mātā ca mama deveśa dāsī tvam upajagmuṣī bhujaṃgamānām mātuḥ vai sā mukta amṛta darśanāt uvāca ca mudā yuktaḥ diṣṭyā diṣṭyā iti vāsavaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ye sahasrākṣam upāsanti suraiḥ saha tatas tam ṛṣayaḥ sarve devāḥ ca bharata ṛṣabha ūcuḥ puraṃdaram hṛṣṭāḥ garuḍaḥ labhatām varam janiṣyati hṛṣīkeśaḥ svayam evā eṣa pakṣirāj keśavaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ śūra putrasya veśmani svayam dharmasya rakṣā artham vibhajya bhujagāśanaḥ eṣa te pāṇḍava śreṣṭha garuḍaḥ atha patatri rāj tat etat dadatām śreṣṭha mayā ākhyānam prakīrtitam suparṇasya mahā bāho kim bhūyas kathayāmi te saṃbhāṣamāṇau tu tatas sakhyam tatra parasparam etat mayā te kathitam sṛgālaḥ aham yathā abhavam vānaraḥ uvāca tvam tu vānara tām prāptaḥ karmaṇā kena tat vada pārakyāṇi purā lobhāt phalāni apahṛtāni ca bhīṣmaḥ uvāca tena aham karma doṣeṇa vānara tvam upāgataḥ sṛgālaḥ vānaraḥ ca api pratiyātau yathāgatam bhīṣmaḥ uvāca sṛgālaḥ vānaram prāha punar eva nṛpa uttama kim tvayā pātakam karma kṛtam yena asi vānaraḥ sa ca api āha brāhmaṇānām phala āhāraḥ plavaṃgamaḥ tasmāt na brāhmaṇa svam tu hartavyam viduṣā sadā sīmā vivāde moktavyam dātavyam ca pratiśrutam na hartavyam vipra dhanam kṣantavyam teṣu nityaśas bālāḥ ca na avamantavyāḥ daridrāḥ kṛpaṇāḥ api duruktam vacanam rājan yathānyāyam yathāgatam śuśrūṣayā parān lokān avāpsyasi na saṃśayaḥ atha grāhayitum vipram śūdraḥ nīvāra taṇḍulān avandhya kāleṣu sadā snāna śauca rateṣu ca viśuddha gṛha bhāṇḍāsu go dhānya abhiratāsu ca kṣāntāsu dāntāsu tathā abalāsu go brāhmaṇam ca arcati devatāḥ ca yudhiṣṭhiraḥ prāyaścittam kṛtaghnānām pratibrūhi pitāmaha mātṝḥ pitṝn gurūn ca eva ye avamanyanti mohitāḥ ye ca api anye pare tāta kṛtaghnāḥ nirapatrapāḥ bhīṣmaḥ teṣām gatim mahā bāho śrotum icchāmi tattvataḥ kṛtaghnānām gatiḥ tāta narake śāśvatīḥ samāḥ mātā pitṛ gurūṇām ca ye na tiṣṭhanta śāsane kṛmi kīṭa pipīleṣu jāyante sthāvareṣu ca durlabhaḥ hi punar teṣām mānuṣye punar udbhavaḥ atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam vatsanābhaḥ mahā prājñaḥ mahā ṛṣiḥ saṃśita vrataḥ tatra sapta aha varṣam tu mumuce pākaśāsanaḥ nimīlita akṣaḥ tat varṣam pratyagṛhṇata vai dvijaḥ viśīrṇa dhvasta śikharaḥ valmīkaḥ aśani tāḍitaḥ kāruṇyāt tasya dharmaḥ tu ānṛśaṃsyam atha akarot anurūpā matiḥ kṣipram upajātā svabhāva jā svam rūpam māhiṣam kṛtvā su mahāntam manoharam trāṇa artham vatsanābhasya catuṣpād upari sthitaḥ yadā tu apagatam varṣam vṛṣṭi vāta samanvitam tatas mahiṣa rūpeṇa dharmaḥ dharma bhṛtām vara sthite asmin vṛṣṭi saṃpāte vīkṣate sma mahā tapāḥ diśaḥ ca vipulāḥ tatra girīṇām śikharāṇi ca dṛṣṭvā ca pṛthivīm sarvām salilena pariplutām jalāśayān sa tān dṛṣṭvā vipraḥ pramuditaḥ abhavat acintayat vismitaḥ ca varṣāt kena asmi rakṣitaḥ atas nu bhadra mahiṣaḥ śilā paṭṭam avasthitaḥ kṛtaghnāḥ narakam yānti ye ca viśvasta ghātinaḥ niṣkṛtim nā iha paśyāmi kṛtaghnānām katham cana ṛte prāṇa parityāgāt dharma jñānām vacaḥ yathā a kṛtvā bharaṇam pitroḥ a dattvā guru dakṣiṇām kṛtaghna tām ca saṃprāpya maraṇa antā ca niṣkṛtiḥ ākāṅkṣāyām upekṣāyām ca upapātakam uttamam tasmāt prāṇān parityakṣye prāyaścitta artham iti uta sa meru śikharam gatvā niḥsaṅgena antarātmanā prāyaścittam kartu kāmaḥ śarīram tyaktum udyataḥ vatsanābha mahā prājña bahu varṣa śata āyuṣa parituṣṭaḥ asmi tyāgena niḥsaṅgena tathā ātmanaḥ evam dharma bhṛtaḥ sarve vimṛśanti kṛta akṛtam yaḥ ca anavadyaḥ carati śaktaḥ dharmam ca sarvaśas nivartasva mahā prājña pūta ātmā hi asi śāśvataḥ na ca eva asya antaram rājan sa dadarśa mahātmanaḥ hāva bhāva ādi lāvaṇya strī guṇāt vā kutūhalam nāma gotrāṇi ca ābhāṣya dārāṇām mantriṇām tathā sa putrāṇām śatam rājā abhiṣicya vanam gataḥ airāvata gaja ārūḍhaḥ sahasrākṣa namaḥ astu te deve nārāyaṇe bhaktiḥ śaṃkare sādhu pūjayā dhyānena atha japaiḥ kāryā svadharmaiḥ śuci tejasā śubhāni eva acaran loke bhaktaḥ nārāyaṇasya hi tasya eva tu padam sūkṣmam prasādāt aśnuyāt param yudhiṣṭhiraḥ aśāśvatam idam sarvam jagat sthāvara jaṃgamam ṛte nārāyaṇam puṇyam pratibhāti pitāmaha nārāyaṇaḥ hi viśvātmā puruṣaḥ puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ bhīṣmaḥ tasya asya devakī sūnoḥ śrutam kṛtsnam tvayā anagha yudhiṣṭhira mahā prājña mayā dṛṣṭam ca saṃgare yudhiṣṭhiraḥ tvattaḥ eva tu rāja indra rāja dharmāḥ ca puṣkalāḥ śrutam purāṇam akhilam nāradena niveditam guhyam nārāyaṇa ākhyānam trividha kleśa nāśanam ekānta dharma niyamāḥ samāsa vyāsa kalpitāḥ ātitheya vidhiḥ ca eva tapāṃsi niyamāḥ ca ye veda vāda prasiddhāḥ ca vājapeya ādayaḥ makhāḥ yajñāḥ draviṇa niṣpādyāḥ agnihotra anupālitāḥ japa yajñāḥ ca vividhāḥ brāhmaṇānām tapasvinām ekādaśavidhāḥ proktāḥ haviḥ yajñāḥ dvijātinām teṣām phala viśeṣāḥ ca uñcha dharmāḥ tathā eva ca ahani ahani ye proktāḥ mahā yajñāḥ dvijātinām veda śravaṇa dharmāḥ ca brahmayajña phalam tathā veda vrata vidhānam ca niyamāḥ ca eva vaidikāḥ svāhā svadhā praṇīte ca iṣṭāpūrta phalam tathā uttara uttara sevāyām āśramāṇām ca yat phalam pratyekaśaḥ ca niṣṭhāyām āśramāṇām mahāmate māsa pakṣa upavāsānām samyak uktam phalam ca yat an āśitānām ye lokāḥ ye ca pañcatapāḥ narāḥ vīrādhvānam prapannānām yā gatiḥ ca agnihotriṇām grīṣme pañcatapānām ca śiśire jalacāriṇām varṣe sthaṇḍilaśāyīnām phalam yat parikīrtitam loke cakracarāṇām ca dvijānām yat phalam smṛtam anna ādīnām ca dānānām yat phalam parikīrtitam sarva tīrtha abhiṣiktānām narāṇām ca phala udayaḥ rājñām dharmāḥ ca ye loke samyak pālayatām prajāḥ ye ca satya vratāḥ loke ye ca tīrthe kṛta śramāḥ mātā pitṛ parāḥ ye ca guru vṛttīḥ ca saṃśritāḥ go brāhmaṇa paritrāṇe rāṣṭra atikramaṇe tathā tyajanti abhimukhāḥ prāṇān nirbhayāḥ sattvam āśritāḥ sahasra dakṣiṇānām ca yā gatiḥ dadatām vara ve ca saṃdhyām upāsante samyak uktāḥ mahā vratāḥ veda ādyāḥ śrutayaḥ ca api śrutāḥ me kurusattama siddhānta nirṇayāḥ ca api dvaipāyana mukha udgatāḥ śrutāḥ pañca mahā yāgāḥ yeṣu sarvam pratiṣṭhitam tad prabhedeṣu ye dharmāḥ te api vai kṛtsnaśas śrutāḥ na ca dūṣayitum śakyāḥ sadbhiḥ uktāḥ hi te tathā eteṣām kila dharmāṇām uttamaḥ vaiṣṇavaḥ vidhiḥ rakṣate bhagavān viṣṇuḥ bhaktam ātma śarīra vat phalam hi avaśyam bhoktavyam ṛṣiḥ dvaipāyanaḥ abravīt bhoga ante ca api patanam gatiḥ pūrvam prabhāṣitā na me prīti karāḥ tu ete viṣa udarkāḥ hi me matāḥ vadhāt kaṣṭataram manye garbha vāsam mahā dyute diṣṭānte yāni duḥkhāni puruṣaḥ vindate vibho tatas kaṣṭatarāṇi iha garbha vāse hi vindati tatas ca abhyadhikām tīvrām vedanām labhate naraḥ garbha apakramaṇe tāta karmaṇām upasarpaṇe tasmāt me niścayaḥ jātaḥ dharmeṣu eteṣu bhārata tat icchāmi kuruśreṣṭha tvad prasādāt mahāmate yena uṣṇa dā vaitaraṇī asipatravanam ca tat kuṇḍāni ca agni taptāni kṣura dhārā pathaḥ tathā śālmalīm ca mahā ghorām āyasīm ghora kaṇṭakām mātā pitṛ kṛte ca api suhṛd mitra artha kāraṇāt ātma hetoḥ ca pāpāni kṛtāni iha naraiḥ ca yaiḥ teṣām phala udayam kaṣṭam ṛṣiḥ dvaipāyanaḥ abravīt kumbhīpāka pradīptānām śūla ārtānām ca krandatām raurave kṣipyamāṇānām prahāraiḥ mathita ātmanām stanatām apakṛttānām pibatām ātma śoṇitam teṣām eva pravadatām kāruṇyam na asti yantrataḥ tṛṣṇā śuṣka uṣṭha kaṇṭhānām vihvalānām acetasām sarva duḥkha abhitaptānām rujā ārtānām ca krośatām ekaḥ karoti pāpāni sahabhojyāni bāndhavaiḥ teṣām ekaḥ phalam bhuṅkte kaṣṭe vaivasvata kṣaye viṣṭhā mūtra kṛmī madhye bahu jantu niṣevite yatra vāsa kṛtaḥ yogaḥ kukṣau vāsaḥ vidhīyate jātaḥ vistīrṇa duḥkhaḥ syāt bhavate vigata jvaraḥ na ca eṣa labhyate kāmaḥ jāta mātram hi mānavam āviśanti iha duḥkhāni manaḥ vāc kāyikāni tu jarasā rūpa vidhvaṃsam prāpnoti avaśaḥ eva tu garbheṣu mriyate kaḥ cit jāyamānaḥ tathā aparaḥ nitya āviṣṭaḥ bhayena aham manasā kurusattama kāla ātmani tirobhūtaḥ nityam tad guṇa varjitaḥ candana agaru digdha aṅgam maṇi muktā vibhūṣitam ramitam su ciram kālam śarīram amita prabham a vitṛptāḥ gamiṣyanti hitvā prāṇān tathā apare svarge api aniyatā bhūtiḥ tathā eva ākāśa saṃśraye devāḥ api adhiṣṭhāna vaśāḥ tasmāt devam na kāmaye kāmānām na asti adhiṣṭhānam akāmaḥ tu nivartate dolā sadharmān dharma jñaḥ ṛṣiḥ dvaipāyanaḥ abravīt kasmāt kaḥ viṣamam duḥkham āroheta vicakṣaṇaḥ kaḥ hi ātmānam priyam loke ḍolā sādharmya tām nayet ghoram tamaḥ praveṣṭavyam a trātāram abāndhavam ye tu tam kila dharma jñāḥ dharmam nārāyaṇa īritam rakṣate bhagavān viṣṇuḥ bhaktān ātma śarīra vat kulāla cakra pratime bhrāmyamāṇeṣu jantuṣu mātā pitṛ sahasrāṇi saṃprāptāni mayā guro sneha āpannena pītāḥ tu mātṝṇām vividhāḥ stanāḥ putra dāra sahasrāṇi iṣṭa aniṣṭa śatāni ca prāptāni adhiṣṭhāna vaśāt atītāni tathā eva ca na kva cit ca sukham prāptam na kva cit śāśvatī gatiḥ dhana nāśaḥ ca saṃprāptaḥ labdhvā duḥkhena tat dhanam adhvagānām iva pathi chāyām āśritya saṃgamaḥ evam karma vaśaḥ lokaḥ jñātīnām hita saṃgamaḥ etat īdṛśakam dṛṣṭvā jñātvā ca eva samāgamam kaḥ na bibhyet kuruśreṣṭha viṣṭha annasya iva bhojanāt etat ādya yuga udbhūtam tretāyām tat tirohitam bhīṣmaḥ sadṛśam rāja śārdūla vṛttasya ca kulasya ca kaḥ rājyam vipulam gṛhya sphīta ākāra puram mahat nirjita arāti sāmantam deva rājya upamam sukham rājye rājya guṇāḥ ye ca tān vyudasya narādhipa atikrāntāḥ tvayā rājan vṛttena prapitāmahāḥ dharmaḥ vigrahavān dhīraḥ viduraḥ ca mahā yaśāḥ saṃjayaḥ ca mahā tejāḥ ye ca anye divya darśanāḥ pravṛtta jñāna saṃpannāḥ tattva jñāna vidaḥ nṛpa te atikrāntāḥ mahā rāja brahma ādyāḥ sa sura asurāḥ anityam duḥkha saṃtaptam jagat etat na saṃśayaḥ evam etān mahā bāho brahma ādyān sa sura asurān anityān satatam paśya manuṣya ādiṣu kā kathā nityām tu prakṛtīm āhuḥ yā asau prasava dharmiṇī arūpiṇīm anirdeśyām akṛtām puruṣa atigām tām atyanta sukhām saukhyām nirvāṇam iti saṃjñitām āhuḥ brahmarṣayaḥ hi ādyām bhuvi ca eva mahā ṛṣayaḥ tayā puruṣa rūpiṇyā dharma prakṛtikaḥ anagha sa yāti eva hi nirvāṇam yat tat prakṛti saṃjñitam saḥ eṣa prākṛtaḥ dharmaḥ bhrājati ādi yuge nṛpa vikāra dharmāḥ śeṣeṣu yugeṣu bharata ṛṣabha bhrājante abhyadhikam vīra saṃsāra patha gocarāḥ prakṛtīnām ca sarvāsām akṛtā prakṛtiḥ smṛtā evam prakṛti dharmāḥ hi varām prakṛtim āśritāḥ paśyanti paramām loke dṛṣṭa adṛṣṭa anudarśinīm kāmam kāmayamāneṣu brāhmaṇeṣu tirohitaḥ nityaḥ tu puruṣaḥ jñeyaḥ viśva rūpaḥ nirañjanaḥ brahma ādyāḥ api devāḥ ca yam sadā paryupāsate tam ca nārāyaṇam viddhi param brahma iti śāśvatam tat padam prāpnuhi prāpyam śāśvatam ca apunarbhavam iti etat viṣṇum āśritya saṃsāra graha mokṣaṇam kathitam te mahā bāho kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi yudhiṣṭhiraḥ kliśyamāneṣu bhūteṣu jātī maraṇa sāgare bhīṣmaḥ yat prāpya kleśam na āpnoti tat me brūhi pitāmaha atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam sanatkumārasya sataḥ saṃvādam nāradasya ca sanatkumāraḥ bhagavān brahma putraḥ mahā yaśāḥ sanakaḥ sanandanaḥ ca eva tṛtīyaḥ ca sanātanaḥ jāta mātrāḥ ca te sarve pratibuddhāḥ iti śrutiḥ hairaṇyagarbhaḥ sa muniḥ vasiṣṭhaḥ pañcamaḥ smṛtaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ sthāṇuḥ sa bhagavān ameya ātmā triśūla dhṛt tatas apare samutpannāḥ pāvakāḥ dāruṇe kratau bhṛguḥ marīceḥ anujaḥ bhṛgoḥ api aṅgirāḥ tathā mūrtimantaḥ amṛtībhūtāḥ tejasā ati tapaḥ nvitāḥ kṛtsnam kṣayam anuprāpya vimuktāḥ mūrti bandhanāt trīn lokān acarat śaśvat aiśvaryeṇa pareṇa hi rudraḥ ca api aṣṭa guṇitam yogam prāptaḥ mahā yaśāḥ sūkṣmam aṣṭaguṇam rājan itare nṛpa sattama marīci pramukhāḥ tāta sarve sṛṣṭi artham eva te niyuktāḥ rāja śārdūla teṣām sṛṣṭim śṛṇuṣva me sapta brahmāṇaḥ iti ete purāṇe niścayam gatāḥ sarve vedeṣu ca eva uktāḥ khileṣu ca na saṃśayaḥ itihāsa purāṇe ca śrutiḥ eṣā sanātanī brāhmaṇāḥ vara dān etān prāhuḥ vedānta pāragāḥ eteṣām pitaraḥ tāta putrāḥ iti anucakṣate gaṇāḥ sapta mahā rāja mūrtayaḥ amūrtayaḥ tathā pitṝṇām ca eva rāja indra putrāḥ devāḥ iti śrutiḥ kṛṣṇadvaipāyanāt ca eva devasthānāt tathā eva ca devalāt ca nara śreṣṭha kāśyapāt ca mayā śrutam gautamāt api kauṇḍinyāt bhāradvājāt tathā eva ca mārkaṇḍeyāt tathā eva etat ṛṣeḥ devamatāt tathā pitrā ca mama rāja indra śrāddha kāle prabhāṣitam param rahasyam vedāntam priyam hi paramātmanaḥ atas param pravakṣyāmi yat mām pṛcchasi bhārata tat iha eka manaḥ buddhiḥ śṛṇuṣva avahitaḥ nṛpa svāyaṃbhuvasya saṃvādam nāradasya ca dhīmataḥ aṅguṣṭha mātraḥ bhūtvā vai vicacāra mahā dyutiḥ sa kadā cit mahābhāgaḥ meru pṛṣṭham samāgamat nāradena nara śreṣṭha muninā brahma vādinā jijñāsamānau anyonyam sakāśe brahmaṇaḥ tatas kṣetra vid kṣetra vid śreṣṭham jñāna vid jñāna vittamam sarva veda artha kuśalam sarva śāstra viśāradam sāṃkhya yogam ca yaḥ veda pāṇau āmalakam yathā nāradaḥ nāradaḥ atha nara śreṣṭha tam papraccha mahā dyutiḥ kāla saṃkhyām ca sargam ca sraṣṭāram puruṣam prabhum sanatkumāraḥ yam viśvam upajīvanti yam āhuḥ puruṣam param tam vai śṛṇu mahābuddhe nārāyaṇam anāmayam eṣa nārāyaṇaḥ nāma yam viśvam upajīvati eṣa sraṣṭā vidhātā ca hartā pālayitā prabhuḥ param na vedmi tat sargam yāvān ca ayam yathā api ayam śrūyatām ānupūrvyeṇa nava sargāḥ prayatnataḥ yathā kāla parīmāṇam tattvānām ṛṣi sattama kāla saṃkhyām ca sargam ca sarvam eva mahā mune paryāya nāmāni etasya kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ tāni te saṃpravakṣyāmi tat iha ekamanāḥ śṛṇu mahā arṇavaḥ arṇavaḥ ca eva salilam ca guṇāḥ tathā vedāḥ tapaḥ ca yajñāḥ ca dharmāḥ ca bhagavān vibhuḥ nāmāni etāni brahmarṣe śarīrasya īśvarasya vai kīrtitāni dvijaśreṣṭha mayā śāstra anumānataḥ prāhuḥ kalpa sahasram vai brāhmaṇāḥ tattva darśinaḥ daśa kalpa sahasrāṇi avyayasya mahāniśā tathā eva divasam prāhuḥ yogāḥ sāṃkhyāḥ ca tattvataḥ paścāt buddhvā sasarja apaḥ tāsu vīryam avāsṛjat hiraṇyagarbham viśvātmā brahmāṇam jalavat munim mūrtimantam mahātmānam viśva śaṃbhum svayaṃbhuvam aṇimā laghimā prāptiḥ īśānaḥ jyotiṣām varam cakre tirodhām bhagavān etat kṛtvā mahā yaśāḥ etasya api niśām āhuḥ veda vedāṅga pāragāḥ sa sargam kurute brahmā tāmasasya anupūrvaśas sṛjate ha tu ahaṃkāram parameṣṭhinam avyayam ahaṃkāreṇa vai lokāḥ vyāptāḥ sāhaṃkṛtena vai yena āviṣṭāni bhūtāni majjanti avyakta sāgare deva ṛṣi dānava narāḥ yakṣa gandharva kiṃnarāḥ etasya api niśām āhuḥ tṛtīyām atha kurvataḥ ahaṃkāraḥ tu sṛjati mahābhūtāni pañca vai pṛthivī vāyuḥ ākāśam āpaḥ jyotiḥ ca pañcamam eteṣām guṇa tattvāni pañca prāhuḥ dvijātayaḥ guṇeṣu eteṣu abhiratāḥ paṅka lagnāḥ iva dvipāḥ na uttiṣṭhanti a vaśībhūtāḥ saktāḥ avyakta sāgare indriyāṇi daśa ekam ca yathāśruti nidarśanāt manaḥ sarvam idam tāta viśvam sarvam idam jagat na tathā anyāni bhūtāni balavanti yathā manaḥ etasya api ha vai sargam ṣaṣṭham āhuḥ dvijātayaḥ ahar kalpa sahasram vai rātriḥ etāvatī tathā aṣṭamam ca api adhaḥsrotaḥ tiryak tu navamaḥ smṛtaḥ sarvasya prabhavaḥ pūrvam uktaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ pravakṣyāmi aparam tattvam yasya yasya īśvaraḥ ca yaḥ yathāśrutam yathādṛṣṭam tattvataḥ vai nibodha me sanatkumāraḥ etābhyām īśvaram vidyāt ūrdhvasrotaḥ tathā eva ca karmendriyāṇām pañcānām īśvaraḥ buddhi gocaraḥ buddhīndriyāṇām atha tu īśvaraḥ manaḥ ucyate manasaḥ pañca bhūtāni sa guṇāni āhuḥ īśvaram bhūtānām īśvaram vidyāt brahmāṇam parameṣṭhinam bhavān hi kuśalaḥ ca eva dharmeṣu eṣu pareṣu vai kālāgniḥ ahnaḥ kalpa ante jagat dahati ca aṃśubhiḥ tatas sarvāṇi bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca mahābhūtāni dagdhāni sva yonim gamitāni vai kūrma pṛṣṭha nibhā bhūmiḥ nirdagdha kuśa kaṇṭakā nirvṛkṣā nistṛṇā ca eva dagdhā kālāgninā tadā naṣṭa gandhā tadā sūkṣmā jalam eva abhavat tadā rasātmā līyate ca arke tadā brāhmaṇa sattama vāyoḥ api guṇam sparśam ākāśam grasate yadā praśāmyati tatas vāyuḥ kham tu tiṣṭhati nānadat sa śabda guṇa hīna ātmā tiṣṭhate mūrtimān tu vai vyoma ātmani vinaṣṭe tu saṃkalpa ātmā vivardhate saṃkalpa ātmānam ādatte cittam vai svena tejasā vinaṣṭe ca tadā citte ahaṃkāraḥ abhavat mahān ahaṃkāram tat ādatte mahān brahmā prajāpatiḥ abhimāne vinaṣṭe tu mahān brahmā virājate tam tadā triṣu lokeṣu mūrtiṣu eva agra mūrti jam yena viśvam idam kṛtsnam nirmitam vai guṇa arthinā mūrtim jaleśvaram api vyavasāya guṇa ātmakam grasiṣṇuḥ bhagavān brahmā vyakta avyaktam asaṃśayaḥ adhyātmam adhibhūtam ca adhidaivam ca śrūyatām rasaḥ adhibhūtam varuṇaḥ adhidaivatam syāt pṛthivī pañcamam bhūtam ghrāṇam adhyātmam gandhaḥ adhibhūtam vāyuḥ adhidaivatam syāt adhibhūtam sūryaḥ adhidaivatam syāt āpaḥ caturtham bhūtam jihvā adhyātmam ākāśam prathamam bhūtam śrotram adhyātmam śabdaḥ adhibhūtam diśaḥ adhidaivatam vāyuḥ dvitīyam bhūtam tvac adhyātmam sparśaḥ adhibhūtam vidyut adhidaivatam syāt jyotiḥ tṛtīyam bhūtam cakṣuḥ adhyātmam rūpam adhibhūtam brahma adhidaivatam syāt atas ūrdhvam indriyam anuvarṇayiṣyāmaḥ pādau adhyātmam gantavyam adhibhūtam viṣṇuḥ adhidaivatam syāt hastau adhyātmam guṇān sṛjati saṃharate ca ca ūrdhvam uttiṣṭhate saṃharate ca yathā ca antarikṣāt abhra kośam uttiṣṭhati guṇān sṛjati saṃharate ca yathā arṇavāt ūrmi mālā nicayaḥ krīḍā artham yathā ādityaḥ aṃśu jālam kṣipati saṃharate ca evam avyaktaḥ śuciḥ tena adhiṣṭhitā prakṛtiḥ cetayati nityam sahadharmā yathā kūrmaḥ aṅgāni kāmāt prasārayate punar ca praveśayati evam lokān prakāśayate praveśayate ca evam cetanaḥ ca bhagavān pañcaviṃśakaḥ kriyāvān ajara amaraḥ kṣetrajñaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ puruṣaḥ bhīṣmaḥ iti etat nāradāya uktam kumāreṇa ca dhīmatā yudhiṣṭhiraḥ dvijātayaḥ vrata upetāḥ japa yajña parāyaṇāḥ yajanti ārambha yajñaiḥ ca mānasam yajñam āsthitāḥ agnibhyaḥ ca param na asti yeṣām eṣā vyavasthitiḥ teṣām gatiḥ mahā prājña kīdṛśī kiṃparāḥ ca te bhīṣmaḥ vaikuṇṭhasya ca saṃvādam suparṇasya ca bhārata ṣaṣṭim varṣa sahasrāṇi devāsuram avartata trātāram na adhigacchanti vadhyamānāḥ mahā asuraiḥ ārtāḥ te devadeveśam prapannāḥ śaraṇa eṣiṇaḥ pitāmaham mahā prājñam vadhyamānāḥ suretaraiḥ tāḥ dṛṣṭvā devatāḥ brahmā saṃbhrānta indriya mānasaḥ vaikuṇṭham śaraṇam devam pratipede ca taiḥ saha tuṣṭāva prāñjaliḥ bhūtvā nārāyaṇam anāmayam tvad rūpa cintanāt nāmnām smaraṇāt arcanāt api tapaḥ yoga ādibhiḥ ca eva śreyaḥ yānti manīṣiṇaḥ paramātma avikārāt ya nārāyaṇa namaḥ astu te devānām api devaḥ tvam sarva vidyā parāyaṇaḥ trāyasva devatāḥ vīra dānava ādyaiḥ su pīḍitāḥ lokān ca lokapālān ca ṛṣīn ca jayatām vara vedāḥ sa aṅga upaniṣadaḥ sa rahasyāḥ sa saṃgrahāḥ sa oṃkārāḥ sa vaṣaṭkārāḥ prāhuḥ tvām yajñam uttamam pavitrāṇām pavitram ca maṅgalānām ca maṅgalam evamādi puraskāraiḥ ṛk sāma yajuṣām gaṇaiḥ vaikuṇṭham tuṣṭuvuḥ devāḥ sarve brahmarṣibhiḥ saha tatas antarikṣe vāc āsīt megha gambhīra nisvanā jeṣyadhvam dānavān yūyam mayā eva saha saṃgare tatas deva gaṇānām ca dānavānām ca yudhyatām prādurāsīt mahā tejāḥ śārṅga cakra gadā dharaḥ suparṇa pṛṣṭham āsthāya tejasā pradahan iva vyadhamat dānavān sarvān bāhu draviṇa tejasā tam samāsādya samare daitya dānava puṃgavāḥ vyanaśyanta mahā rāja pataṃgāḥ iva pāvakam sa vijitya asurān sarvān dānavān ca mahāmatiḥ paśyatām eva devānām tatra eva antaradhīyata tam dṛṣṭvā antarhitam devam viṣṇum deva amita dyutim devāsure asmin saṃgrāme trātā yena vayam prabho kaḥ ayam asmān paritrāya tūṣṇīm eva yathāgatam pratiprayātaḥ divya ātmā tam naḥ śaṃsitum arhasi brahmā saṃkhyātum nā eva ca ātmānam nirguṇam guṇinām varam suparṇasya ca saṃvādam ṛṣīṇām ca api devatāḥ āśritya himavat pṛṣṭhe cakrire vividhāḥ kathāḥ teṣām kathayatām tatra kathā ante patatām varaḥ prādurāsīt mahā tejāḥ vāhaḥ cakra gadā bhṛtaḥ sa tān ṛṣīn samāsādya vinaya avanata ānanaḥ avatīrya mahā vīryaḥ tān ṛṣīn abhijagmivān abhyarcitaḥ saḥ ṛṣibhiḥ svāgatena mahā balaḥ upāviśata tejasvī bhūmau vegavatām varaḥ tam āsīnam mahātmānam vainateyam mahā dyutim ṛṣayaḥ paripapracchuḥ mahātmānaḥ tapasvinaḥ evam uktaḥ pratyuvāca prāñjaliḥ vinatā sutaḥ dhanyaḥ asmi anugṛhītaḥ asmi yat mām brahmarṣi sattamāḥ praṣṭavyam praṣṭum icchanti prītimantaḥ anasūyakāḥ kim mayā brūta vaktavyam kāryam ca vadatām varāḥ yūyam hi mām yathā yuktam sarvam vai praṣṭum arhatha namaskṛtvā hi anantāya tatas te ṛṣi sattamāḥ devadevam mahātmānam nārāyaṇam anāmayam prakṛtiḥ vikṛtiḥ vā asya kīdṛśī kva nu saṃsthitiḥ etat bhavantam pṛcchāmaḥ devaḥ ayam kva kṛta ālayaḥ tvam priyaḥ ca asya bhaktaḥ ca na anyaḥ kāśyapa vidyate muṣṇan iva manaḥ cakṣūṃṣi avibhāvya tanuḥ vibhuḥ pṛthivī vāyuḥ ākāśam āpaḥ jyotiḥ ca pañcamam guṇāḥ ca eṣām yathāsaṃkhyam bhāva abhāvau tathā eva ca tamaḥ sattvam rajaḥ ca eva bhāvāḥ ca eva tad ātmakāḥ jāyante tāta kasmāt hi tiṣṭhate teṣu asau vibhuḥ etam eva param praśnam kautūhala samanvitāḥ suparṇaḥ evam bhavantam pṛcchāmaḥ tat naḥ śaṃsitum arhasi trailokyasya tu rakṣā artham dṛśyate rūpam āsthitaḥ mayā tu mahat āścaryam purā dṛṣṭam sanātane deve śrīvṛkṣa nilaye tat śṛṇudhvam aśeṣatas yathā mām prāha bhagavān tathā tat śrūyatām mama mayā amṛtam devatānām miṣatām ṛṣi sattamāḥ hṛtam vipāṭya tat yantram vidrāvya amṛta rakṣiṇaḥ devatāḥ vimukhīkṛtya sa indrāḥ sa marutaḥ mṛdhe tam dṛṣṭvā mama vikrāntam vāc uvāca aśarīriṇī avṛthā te astu mad vākyam brūhi kim karavāṇi te tām evaṃvādinīm vācam aham pratyuktavān tadā prakṛtiḥ vikṛtiḥ vā tvam devaḥ vā dānavaḥ api vā uvāca vara daḥ prītaḥ kāle tvam mā abhivetsyasi na te vīryeṇa sadṛśaḥ kaḥ cit loke bhaviṣyati pataṃga patatām śreṣṭha na devaḥ nā api dānavaḥ tam abravam devadevam mām evaṃvādinam param prayataḥ prāñjaliḥ bhūtvā praṇamya śirasā vibhum vāhanam te bhaviṣyāmi yathā vadati mām bhavān mama api ca mahābuddhe niścayam śrūyatām iti tathā astu iti sa mām uktvā bhūyas prāha mahāmanāḥ evam kṛtvā tu samayam devadevaḥ sanātanaḥ mām uktvā sādhayasva iti yathā abhiprāyataḥ gataḥ tatas aham tena kena api sattamāḥ kautūhala samāviṣṭaḥ pitaram kāśyapam gataḥ saḥ aham pitaram āsādya praṇipatya abhivādya ca sa muhūrtam iva dhyātvā mām āha vadatām varaḥ dhanyaḥ asi anugṛhītaḥ ca yat tvam tena mahātmanā saṃvādam kṛtavān tāta guhyena paramātmanā sthūla dṛśyaḥ sa bhagavān tena tena eva hetunā dṛśyate avyakta rūpa sthaḥ pradhānam prabhava apyayaḥ mayā hi sa mahā tejāḥ na anya yoga samādhinā tapasā ugreṇa tejasvī toṣitaḥ tapasām nidhiḥ śveta pīta aruṇa nibhaḥ kadrū kapila piṅgalaḥ rakta nīla asita nibhaḥ sahasra udara pāṇimān dvi sāhasra mahā vaktraḥ eka akṣaḥ śata locanaḥ aniṣpandāḥ nirāhārāḥ samānāḥ sūrya tejasā samāsādya tu tam viśvam aham mūrdhnā praṇamya ca ṛk yajuḥ sāmabhiḥ stutvā śaraṇyam śaraṇam gataḥ mahā megha ogha dhīreṇa svareṇa jayatām varaḥ ābhāṣya putra putra iti idam āha dhṛtam vacaḥ tvayā abhyudaya kāmena tapaḥ cīrṇam mahā mune ekāntikīm matim kṛtvā mad bhaktaḥ mad parāyaṇaḥ jñāsyase mām tatas brahman vīta mohaḥ ca tattvataḥ tena tvam kṛta saṃvādaḥ svatas sarva hita eṣiṇā viśvarūpeṇa devena puruṣeṇa mahātmanā tam eva ārādhaya kṣipram tam ārādhya na sīdasi saḥ aham evam bhagavatā pitrā brahma ṛṣi sattamāḥ suparṇaḥ anugītaḥ yathānyāyam svam eva bhavanam gataḥ tad bhāva gata bhāva ātmā tad bhūta gata mānasaḥ govindam cintayan āse śāśvatam param avyayam dhṛtam babhūva hṛdayam nārāyaṇa didṛkṣayā saḥ aham vegam samāsthāya manaḥ māruta vegavān ramyām viśālām badarīm gataḥ nārāyaṇa āśramam tatas tatra harim dṛṣṭvā jagataḥ prabhavam vibhum govindam puṇḍarīkākṣam praṇataḥ śirasā harim ṛk yajuḥ sāmabhiḥ ca enam tuṣṭāva parayā mudā athā apaśyam su vipulam aśvattham deva saṃśrayam catur dviguṇa pīna aṃsaḥ śaṅkha cakra gadā dharaḥ prādurbabhūva puruṣaḥ pīta vāsāḥ sanātanaḥ madhyāhna arka pratīkāśaḥ tejasā bhāsayan diśaḥ saṃstutaḥ saṃvidam kṛtvā vraja iti śreyase rataḥ prāc udīcīm diśam devaḥ pratasthe puruṣottamaḥ diśaḥ ca vidiśaḥ ca eva bhāsayan svena tejasā yojanānām sahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭim aṣṭau tathā śatam tathā śata sahasram ca śatam dviguṇam eva ca śata yojana vistīrṇam tasmāt dviguṇam āyatam viveśa sa mahā yogī pāvakam pāvaka dyutiḥ sa tena saṃvidam kṛtvā pāvakam samatikramat gatvā sa dīrgham adhvānam bhāskareṇa avabhāsitam atha dṛṣṭiḥ pratihatā mama tatra babhūva ha yathāsvabhāvam bhūtātmā viveśa sa mahā dyutiḥ tatas aham abhavam mūḍhaḥ jaḍa andha badhira upamaḥ diśaḥ ca vidiśaḥ ca eva na vijajñe tamaḥ vṛtaḥ a vijānan aham kim cit tasmin tamasi saṃvṛte su saṃbhrāntena manasā vyathām paramikām gataḥ saḥ aham prapannaḥ śaraṇam devadevam sanātanam prāñjaliḥ manasā bhūtvā vākyam etat tadā uktavān śaraṇam saṃprapannam mām trātum arhasi ariṃdama aham tu tattva jijñāsuḥ kaḥ asi kasya asi kutra vā saṃprāptaḥ padavīm deva sa mām saṃtrātum arhasi āvirbhūtaḥ purāṇa ātmā mām ehi iti sanātanaḥ mohayām āsa mām tatra durvibhāvya vapuḥ vibhuḥ śramam me janayām āsa bhayam ca abhaya daḥ prabhuḥ śabdena āśvāsayām āsa jagāhe ca tamaḥ mahat aham tu kheda anugataḥ śramāt cala padaḥ caran tathā gatam tu mām jñātvā bhagavān amita dyutiḥ tamaḥ mama anugraha kāṅkṣayā apaśyam tejasā vyāptam madhyāhne iva bhāskaram svayaṃprabhān ca puruṣān striyaḥ ca parama adbhutāḥ na tatra candramāḥ bhāti na vāyuḥ vāti pāṃsulaḥ tatra tūryāṇi anekāni gītāni madhurāṇi ca adṛśyāni manojñāni śrūyante sarvatodiśam sravanti vaiḍūrya latāḥ padma utpala jhaṣa ākulāḥ agatiḥ tatra devānām asurāṇām tathā eva ca gandharva nāga yakṣāṇām rākṣasānām tathā eva ca svayaṃprabhāḥ tatra narāḥ dṛśyante adbhuta darśanāḥ yeṣām na devatāḥ tulyāḥ prabhābhiḥ bhāvitātmanām jvālābhiḥ mām praviṣṭam ca jvalantam sarvatodiśam praviṣṭaḥ ca apaviddhaḥ ca na ca mām dagdhavān kva cit ayam ca duḥsahaḥ śaśvat tejasā ati hutāśanaḥ ati āditya prakāśa arciḥ analaḥ dīpyate mahān prapannaḥ śaraṇam devam śaṅkha cakra gadā dharam bhaktaḥ ca anugataḥ ca iti trātum arhasi mām vibho evam vilapamānasya jñātvā me vacanam prabhuḥ sa mām āśvāsya vacanam prāha idam bhagavān vibhuḥ jñāpitaḥ ca api yat pitrā tat ca api viditam mahat mām hi vindanti vidvāṃsaḥ ye jñāne pariniṣṭhitāḥ nirmamāḥ nirahaṃkārāḥ niḥ āśīḥ bandhana ayutāḥ suparṇaḥ sthūlam mām vetsyate tasmāt jagataḥ kāraṇe sthitam evam datta abhayaḥ tena tatas aham ṛṣi sattamāḥ naṣṭa kheda śrama bhayaḥ kṣaṇena hi abhavam tadā sa śanais yāti bhagavān gatyā laghu parākramaḥ aham tu su mahā vegam āsthāya anuvrajāmi tam sa gatvā dīrgham adhvānam ākāśam amita dyutiḥ atha devaḥ samāsādya manasaḥ sadṛśam javam mohayitvā ca mām tatra kṣaṇena antaradhīyata tatra ambudhara dhīreṇa bho śabdena anunādinā ayam bho aham iti prāha vākyam vākya viśāradaḥ śabda anusārī tu tatas tam deśam aham āvrajam tatra apaśyam tatas ca aham śrīmat haṃsa yutam saraḥ sa tat saraḥ samāsādya bhagavān ātma vittamaḥ bho śabda pratisṛṣṭena svareṇa aprativādinā viveśa devaḥ svām yonim mām idam ca abhyabhāṣata tatas ca prāviśam tatra saha tena mahātmanā dṛṣṭavān adbhutataram tasmin sarasi bhāsvatām agnīnām apraṇītānām iddhānām indhanaiḥ vinā dīptānām ājya siktānām sthāneṣu arciṣmatām sadā dīptiḥ teṣām an ājyānām prāpta ājyānām iva abhavat an iddhānām iva satām iddhānām iva bhāsvatām atha aham vara dam devam na apaśyam tatra saṃgatam tatas saṃmoham āpannaḥ viṣādam agamam param apaśyam ca agnihotrāṇi śataśas atha sahasraśas vidhinā saṃpraṇītāni dhiṣṇyeṣu ājyavatām tadā a saṃmṛṣṭa talāḥ ca eva vedīḥ kusuma saṃstṛtāḥ kuśa padma utpala āsaṅgān kalaśān ca hiraṇmayān agnihotrāṇi citrāṇi śataśas atha sahasraśas tāni ca atra samṛddhāni dṛṣṭavān asmi anekaśas manohṛdyatamaḥ ca atra surabhiḥ puṇya lakṣaṇaḥ teṣām tatra agnihotrāṇām īḍitānām sahasraśas candra aṃśu kāśa śubhrāṇām tuṣāra udbheda varcasām vimala āditya bhāsānām sthaṇḍilāni sahasraśas dṛṣṭāni agni samīpeṣu dyutimanti mahānti ca eṣu ca agni samīpeṣu śuśrāva supada akṣarāḥ prabhāva antaritānām tu praspaṣṭa akṣara bhāṣiṇām ṛk yajuḥ sāma gānām ca madhurāḥ su svarāḥ giraḥ su saṃmṛṣṭa talaiḥ taiḥ tu bṛhadbhiḥ dīpta tejasaiḥ pāvakaiḥ pāvita ātmā aham abhavam laghu vikramaḥ tatas aham teṣu dhiṣṇyeṣu jvalamāneṣu yajvanām tam deśam praṇamitvā atha anveṣṭum upacakrame tāni aneka sahasrāṇi parīyan tu mahā javāt tatas aham tāni dīptāni parīya vyathita indriyaḥ na antam teṣām prapaśyāmi khedaḥ ca sahasā abhavat vihṛtya sarvatas dṛṣṭim bhaya moha samanvitaḥ śramam paramam āpannaḥ cintayāmi vicetanaḥ ṛk yajuḥ sāma nirghoṣaḥ śrūyate na ca dṛśyate na ca paśyāmi tam devam yena aham iha coditaḥ tatas cintayataḥ mahyam mohena āviṣṭa cetasaḥ mahān śabdaḥ prādurāsīt su bhṛśam me vyathā karaḥ athā aham sahasā tatra śṛṇomi vipula dhvanim apaśyam ca suparṇānām sahasrāṇi ayutāni ca dṛṣṭvā ātmānam parigatam saṃbhramam paramam gataḥ nārāyaṇāya śuddhāya śāśvatāya dhruvāya ca bhūta bhavya bhaveśāya śivāya śiva mūrtaye śivayoneḥ śiva ādyāya śiva pūjyatamāya ca viśvāya viśvadevāya viśveśāya mahātmane śuciśravāya mahate ṛtu saṃvatsarāya ca ṛk yajuḥ sāma vaktrāya atharvaśirase namaḥ hṛṣīkeśāya kṛṣṇāya druhiṇa urukramāya ca brahma udrekāya tārkṣyāya varāhāya ekaśṛṅgiṇe śipiviṣṭāya satyāya haraye atha śikhaṇḍine hutāya ūrdhvāya vakrāya raudra anīkāya sādhave garutmate trinetrāya sudhāmāya vṛṣāvṛṣe vṛṣāya vṛṣa rūpāya vibhave bhūrbhuvāya ca dīpta sṛṣṭāya yajñāya sthirāya sthavirāya ca acyutāya tuṣārāya vīrāya ca samāya ca jiṣṇave puruhūtāya vasiṣṭhāya varāya ca satyeśāya sureśāya haraye atha śikhaṇḍine kirīṭine sukeśāya vāsudevāya śuṣmiṇe bṛhadukthe suṣeṇāya yugme duṃdubhaye tathā bhāskarāya ca candrāya padmanābhāya bhūriṇe vaṣaṭkārāya svāhāya svadhāya nidhanāya ca śrī padmāya aja sadṛśe dharaṇe dhāraṇe pare viśvāya ca su viśvāya viśva rūpa dharāya ca keśavāya su keśāya raśmi keśāya bhūriṇe hiraṇyagarbhāya namaḥ saumyāya vṛṣa rūpiṇe nārāyaṇa agra vapuṣe puruhūtāya vajriṇe śaraṇāya śaraṇyāya śakta iṣṭa śaraṇāya ca pāhi mām devadeveśa kaḥ api ajaḥ asi sanātanaḥ evam gataḥ asmi śaraṇam śaraṇyam brahma yoninam śṛṇomi ca giram divyām antardhāna gatām śivām apaśyam ca aham āyāntam nara nārāyaṇa āśrame catur dviguṇa vinyāsam tam ca devam sanātanam yajataḥ tān ṛṣīn devān vadataḥ dhyāyataḥ munīn yuktān siddhān naiṣṭhikān ca japataḥ yajataḥ gṛhe puṣpa pūra parikṣiptam dhūpitam dīpitam hutam vanditam sikta saṃmṛṣṭam nara nārāyaṇa āśramam tat adbhutam aham dṛṣṭvā vismitaḥ asmi tadā anaghāḥ jagāma śirasā devam prayatena antarātmanā na adhyagaccham parām divyām tasya sarva bhava ātmanaḥ avocam tam adīna artham śreṣṭhānām śreṣṭham uttamam yat etat adbhutam deva mayā dṛṣṭam tvad āśrayam yadi jānāsi mām bhaktam yadi vā anugrahaḥ mayi śaṃsa sarvam aśeṣeṇa śrotavyam yadi ced mayā brūhi sarvam aśeṣeṇa tat āścaryam mahā mune kim tat ati adbhutam vṛttam teṣu agniṣu samantataḥ kāni tāni agnihotrāṇi keṣām śabdaḥ śrutaḥ mayā śṛṇvatām brahma satatam adṛśyānām mahātmanām etat me bhagavan kṛṣṇa brūhi sarvam aśeṣatas gṛṇanti agni samīpeṣu ke ca te brahma rāśayaḥ mām na devāḥ na gandharvāḥ na piśācāḥ na rākṣasāḥ caturdhā aham vibhakta ātmā lokānām hita kāmyayā bhūta bhavya bhaviṣya ādiḥ anādiḥ viśvakṛttamaḥ pṛthivī vāyuḥ ākāśam āpaḥ jyotiḥ ca pañcamam prakṛtiḥ vikṛtiḥ ca iti vidyā avidye śubha aśubhe sa kim cit śreyasā yuktaḥ śreṣṭha bhāvam vyavasyati yat svabhāva ātma tattva jñaiḥ kāraṇaiḥ upalakṣyate an ādi madhya nidhanaḥ saḥ antarātmā asmi śāśvataḥ yat tu me paramam guhyam rūpam sūkṣma artha darśibhiḥ gṛhyate sūkṣma bhāva jñaiḥ saḥ avibhāvyaḥ asmi śāśvataḥ mattaḥ jātāni bhūtāni mayā aharniśam yaḥ mām yathā vedayati tasya tasya asmi kāśyapa mām tu jñātum kṛtā buddhiḥ bhavatā pakṣi sattama śṛṇu yaḥ aham yatas ca aham yad arthaḥ ca aham udyataḥ ye ke cit niyata ātmānaḥ tretāgni parama arcitāḥ agni kārya parāḥ nityam japa homa parāyaṇāḥ ātmani agnīn samādhāya niyatāḥ niyata indriyāḥ yajantaḥ japa yajñaiḥ mām mānasaiḥ ca su saṃyatāḥ agnīn abhyudyayuḥ śaśvat agniṣu eva abhisaṃsthitāḥ an anya kāryāḥ śucayaḥ nityam agni parāyaṇāḥ ye evaṃbuddhayaḥ dhīrāḥ te mām gacchanti tādṛśāḥ a kāma hata saṃkalpāḥ jñāne nityam samāhitāḥ ātmani agnīn samādhāya nirāhārāḥ nirāśiṣaḥ viṣayeṣu nirārambhāḥ vimuktāḥ jñāna cakṣuṣaḥ an anya manasaḥ dhīrāḥ svabhāva niyama anvitāḥ tatra agnayaḥ saṃnihitāḥ dīpyante sma nirindhanāḥ jñāna amala āśayāḥ tasmin ye ca candra aṃśu nirmalāḥ upāsīnāḥ gṛṇantaḥ agnim praspaṣṭa akṣara bhāṣiṇaḥ ākāṅkṣamāṇāḥ śucayaḥ teṣu agniṣu vihaṃgama ye mayā bhāvita ātmānaḥ mayi eva abhiratāḥ sadā upāsate ca mām eva jyotiḥ bhūtāḥ nirāmayāḥ nirāhārāḥ hi aniṣpandāḥ candra aṃśu sadṛśa prabhāḥ nirmalāḥ nirahaṃkārāḥ nirālambāḥ caturdhā aham vibhakta ātmā carāmi jagataḥ hitaḥ lokānām dhāraṇa arthāya vidhānam vidadhāmi ca yathāvat tat aśeṣeṇa śrotum arhati me bhavān ekā mūrtiḥ nirguṇa ākhyā yogam paramam āsthitā dvitīyā sṛjate tāta bhūta grāmam carācaram sṛṣṭam saṃharate ca ekā jagat sthāvara jaṃgamam jāta ātma niṣṭhā kṣapayan mohayan iva māyayā kṣipantī mohayantī ca ātma niṣṭhā sva māyayā caturthī me mahā mūrtiḥ jagat vṛddhim dadāti sā mayā sarvam idam vyāptam mayi sarvam pratiṣṭhitam yāni tāni agnihotrāṇi ye ca candra aṃśu rāśayaḥ gṛṇanti vedam satatam teṣu agniṣu vihaṃgama krameṇa mām samāyānti sukhinaḥ jñāna saṃyutāḥ teṣām aham tapaḥ dīptam tejaḥ samyak samāhitam śakyaḥ samāsādayitum aham vai jñāna cakṣuṣā mām sthūladarśanam viddhi jagataḥ kāryakāraṇam mayā ca api caturdhā ātmā vibhaktaḥ prāṇiṣu sthitaḥ brahmaṇaḥ ca api saṃbhūtaḥ śivaḥ iti avadhāryatām śivāt skandaḥ saṃbabhūva etat sṛṣṭi catuṣṭayam tadā aham dharma vṛddhi artham mūrtimān bhavitā āśuga yoginaḥ yoga yuktāḥ ca te mām paśyanti na anyathā pañcabhiḥ saṃprayuktaḥ aham viprayuktaḥ ca pañcabhiḥ vartamānaḥ ca teṣu evam nivṛttaḥ ca eva teṣu aham ye viduḥ jāta saṃkalpāḥ te mām paśyanti tādṛśāḥ kham vāyuḥ āpaḥ jyotiḥ ca pṛthivī ca iti pañcamam vartamānam atītam ca pañcavargeṣu niścalam śabda sparśeṣu rūpeṣu rasa gandheṣu ca api aham rajaḥ tamobhyām āviṣṭā yeṣām buddhiḥ aniścitā draṣṭum vā veditum vā api na śakyā paramā gatiḥ mahā moha uttha paṅke tu nimagnānām gatiḥ hariḥ ekāntinaḥ dhyāna parāḥ yati bhāvāt vrajanti mām sattva yuktā matiḥ yeṣām kevala ātma viniścitā te paśyanti svam ātmānam parama ātmānam avyayam ahiṃsā sarva bhūteṣu teṣu avasthitam ārjavam ye tu agnihotra niyatāḥ japa yajña parāyaṇāḥ te mām upāsate śaśvat yān tān tvam dṛṣṭavān asi śāstra dṛṣṭa vidhāna jñāḥ asaktāḥ kva cit anyathā ye tu sāṃkhyam ca yogam ca jñātvā api adhṛta niścayāḥ na te gacchanti akuśalāḥ parām gatim anuttamām tasmāt jñānena śuddhena prasanna ātmā ātma vid śuciḥ śuddha abhijana saṃpannāḥ śraddhā yuktena cetasā mad bhaktyā ca dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ gacchanti paramām gatim na vāyuḥ pavate tatra na tasmin jyotiṣām gatiḥ na ca āpaḥ pṛthivī nā eva na ākāśam na manogatiḥ tasmāt ca etāni sarvāṇi prajāyante vihaṃgama sthūla darśanam etat me yat dṛṣṭam bhavata anagha etat sūkṣmasya ca dvāram kāryāṇām kāraṇam tu aham brahmaṇaḥ yat ahorātram saṃkhyāna jñaiḥ vibhāvyate mām yajñam āhuḥ yajña jñāḥ vedam veda vidaḥ janāḥ munayaḥ ca api mām eva japa yajñam pracakṣate vaktā mantā rasayitā ghrātā draṣṭā pradarśakaḥ boddhā bodhayitā ca aham gantā śrotā cit ātmakaḥ mām iṣṭvā svargam āyānti tathā ca āpnuvate mahat jñātvā mām eva ca edhante niḥsaṅgena antarātmanā aham tejaḥ dvijātīnām mama tejaḥ dvijātayaḥ prāṇa pālaḥ śarīre aham yoginām aham īśvaraḥ sāṃkhyānām idam eva agre mayi sarvam idam jagat dharmam artham ca kāmam ca mokṣam ca eva ārjavam japam sa tadā aham tathārūpaḥ tvayā dṛṣṭaḥ sanātanaḥ nirārambha namaskārāḥ tathā sthūlaḥ aham evam vihaga tvayā dṛṣṭaḥ tathā anagha mā matiḥ tava gāt nāśam eṣā gatiḥ anuttamā etat te sarvam ākhyātam rahasyam divya mānuṣam etat śreyaḥ param ca etat panthānam viddhi mokṣiṇām etat evaṃvidham tasya mahimānam mahātmanaḥ acyutasya aprameyasya dṛṣṭavān asmi yat purā etat vaḥ sarvam ākhyātam ceṣṭitam tasya dhīmataḥ ṛṣayaḥ mayā anubhūtam pratyakṣam dṛṣṭvā ca adbhuta karmaṇaḥ aho śrāvitam ākhyānam bhavatā ati adbhutam mahat puṇyam yaśasyam āyuṣyam svargyam svastyayanam mahat etat jñānavatām jñeyam eṣā gatiḥ anuttamā yaḥ imām śrāvayet vidvān kathām parvasu parvasu sa lokān prāpnuyāt puṇyān deva ṛṣibhiḥ abhiṣṭutān śrāddha kāle ca viprāṇām yaḥ imām śrāvayet śuciḥ na tatra rakṣasām bhāgaḥ na asurāṇām ca vidyate bhīṣmaḥ vaiśyaḥ tu dhana dhānya āḍhyaḥ śūdraḥ sukham avāpnuyāt svān eva ca āśramān jagmuḥ babhūvuḥ śānti tatparāḥ sthūla darśibhiḥ ākṛṣṭaḥ durjñeyaḥ hi akṛtātmabhiḥ eṣā śrutiḥ mahā rāja dharmyā dharma bhṛtām vara surāṇām brahmaṇā proktā vismitānām paraṃtapa mayā api eṣā kathā tāta kathitā mātuḥ antike tat agnihotra paramāḥ japa yajña parāyaṇāḥ santaḥ prayānti akṣara sāmya tām ārambha yajñān utsṛjya japa homa parāyaṇāḥ dhyāyantaḥ manasā viṣṇum gacchanti paramām gatim yathā viniścita ātmānaḥ gacchanti paramām gatim tvayā āpageya nāmāni śrutāni iha jagatpateḥ bhīṣmaḥ uvāca aśaktaḥ aham guṇān vaktum mahādevasya dhīmataḥ brahma viṣṇu sureśānām sraṣṭā yaḥ prabhuḥ eva ca brahma ādayaḥ piśāca antāḥ yam hi devāḥ upāsate prakṛtīnām para tvena puruṣasya ca yaḥ paraḥ prakṛtim puruṣam ca eva kṣobhayitvā sva tejasā brahmāṇam asṛjat tasmāt devadevaḥ prajāpatiḥ kaḥ hi śaktaḥ guṇān vaktum devadevasya dhīmataḥ kaḥ hi śaktaḥ bhavam jñātum madvidhaḥ parameśvaram ṛte nārāyaṇāt putra śaṅkha cakra gadā dharāt eṣa vidvān yaduśreṣṭhaḥ viṣṇuḥ parama durjayaḥ divya cakṣuḥ mahā tejāḥ vīkṣyate yoga cakṣuṣā rudra bhaktyā tu kṛṣṇena jagat vyāptam mahātmanā tam prasādya tadā devam badaryām kila bhārata prāptavān eva rāja indra suvarṇākṣāt maheśvarāt pūrṇam varṣa sahasram tu taptavān eṣa mādhavaḥ prasādya vara dam devam carācara gurum śivam yuge yuge tu kṛṣṇena toṣitaḥ vai maheśvaraḥ bhaktyā paramayā rājan prītaḥ ca eva mahātmanaḥ aiśvaryam yādṛśam tasya jagadyoneḥ mahātmanaḥ tat ayam dṛṣṭavān sākṣāt putra arthe hariḥ acyutaḥ yasmāt parataram ca eva na anyam paśyāmi bhārata vyākhyātum devadevasya śaktaḥ nāmāni aśeṣatas eṣa śaktaḥ mahā bāhuḥ vaktum bhagavataḥ guṇān vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca vibhūtim ca eva kārtsnyena satyām māheśvarīm nṛpa evam uktvā tadā bhīṣmaḥ vāsudevam mahā yaśāḥ bhava māhātmya saṃyuktam idam āha pitāmahaḥ dhyānena atha japaiḥ kāryā svadharmam ucitā sadā deve nārāyaṇe bhaktaḥ śaṃkaram sādhu pūjaya vāsudevam mahātmānam idam vacanam abravīt athā ūcatuḥ prīti yutau tadānīm prāpya anujñām guru mukhāt ājamīḍha tārkṣyam tadā manasā cintayitvā kṛtvā praṇāmam śirasā bhavāya tatas abhyagām prāk udīcīm su ramyām tatas mayā hi anusṛṣṭaḥ khaga indraḥ sthitaḥ prahṛṣṭaḥ bhava bhāva bhāvitaḥ nivedya mātuḥ ca pituḥ samīpe tathā abhyanujñāya gurūn ca sarvān mahā balam tārkṣyam acintayam vai saḥ aham himavataḥ pārśvam prāpya tārkṣyam vyasarjayam viśālaiḥ ca agni śaraṇaiḥ yathāsthāna āhitāgnibhiḥ prahṛṣṭa nānāvidha pakṣi sevitam mahīruhāṇām ca vanaiḥ vicitraiḥ nānā vṛndaiḥ veda ṣaḍaṅga pāragaiḥ dharma abhirāmaiḥ munibhiḥ samāhitaiḥ aśvattha phala bhakṣāḥ ca tathā hi udaka śāyinaḥ cīra carma ambara dharāḥ tathā valkala dhāriṇaḥ manuṣya tā anuvṛttyā tvām jñātvā tiṣṭhāma sarvagam utpāditam vṛṣāṅkena dīpta jvalana saṃnibham na śakyam draṣṭum anyena varjayitvā pinākinam sudarśanam tadā tasya loke nāma pratiṣṭhitam tat jīrṇam abhavat tāta grahasya aṅgeṣu keśava na śastrāṇi vahanti aṅge cakra vajra śatāni api yājñavalkyaḥ iti khyātaḥ ṛṣiḥ parama dhārmikaḥ ārādhya sa mahādevam prāptavān jñānam uttamam vedavyāsaḥ ca yoga ātmā parāśara sutaḥ muniḥ saḥ api śaṃkaram ārādhya prāptavān atulam yaśaḥ atreḥ bhāryā sutam dattam somam durvāsasam prabhoḥ vinā bhartrā ca rudreṇa bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ vikarṇaḥ ca mahādevam tathā bhakta sukha āvaham śakreṇa tu purā devaḥ vārāṇasyām janārdana ārādhitaḥ abhūt bhaktena digvāsāḥ bhasma guṇṭhanaḥ ārādhya sa mahādevam deva rājyam avāptavān nāradena tu bhaktyā asau bhavaḥ ārādhitaḥ purā śākalyaḥ grantha kartā ca sāvarṇiḥ ca tathā bhavat bāṇaḥ skanda sama tvam ca kāmaḥ darpa vimokṣaṇam mahādevaḥ mayā prabhuḥ dīyatām dīyatām kṣīram mama mātar iti īritam pitrā aham yajña kāle hi nītaḥ jñāti kulam mahat tatra sā kṣarate devī divyā gauḥ sura nandinī tasya aham tat payaḥ pītvā rasena hi amṛta upamam jñātvā kṣīra guṇān ca eva upalabhya hi saṃbhavam abhāvāt ca eva dugdhasya duḥkhitā jananī tadā āsthitānām nadīm divyām vālakhilyaiḥ niṣevitām kutas kṣīram vana sthānām munīnām giri vāsinām pāvanānām vana āśānām vana āśrama nivāsinām grāmya āhāra nivṛttānām āraṇya phala bhojinām nadī gahvara śaileṣu tīrtheṣu vividheṣu ca tapasā japya nityānām śivaḥ naḥ paramā gatiḥ sarva kāraṇa bhūtaḥ ayam dhātā rudraḥ trilocanaḥ su daṃṣṭraḥ su smitaḥ nāga kuṇḍalaḥ bhasma bhāsitaḥ sarva kāma pradaḥ bhaktyā pūjitaḥ puṣpa mūlakaiḥ bhaktyā pratyakṣatām nītaḥ sarva kāma pradaḥ haraḥ jananyāḥ tat vacaḥ śrutvā tadā prabhṛti śatru han prāñjaliḥ praṇataḥ bhūtvā idam ambām acodayam kutra vā vasate devaḥ draṣṭavyaḥ vā katham ca saḥ tuṣyate vā katham śarvaḥ rūpam vā tasya kīdṛśam katham jñeyaḥ prasannaḥ vā darśayet jananī mama evam uktā tadā kṛṣṇa mātā me suta vatsalā mūrdhani āghrāya govinda sa bāṣpa ākula locanā pramārjantī ca gātrāṇi mama vai madhusūdana ambā durādhṛṣyaḥ ca durgrāhyaḥ durdṛśyaḥ hi akṛtātmabhiḥ yasya rūpāṇi anekāni pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ sthānāni ca vicitrāṇi prasādāḥ ca api anekaśas kṛtavān yāni rūpāṇi devadevaḥ purā kila krīḍate ca yathā śarvaḥ prasīdati yathā ca vai hṛdisthaḥ sarva bhūtānām viśva rūpaḥ maheśvaraḥ bhaktānām anukampā artham darśanam ca yathāśrutam munīnām bruvatām divyām īśāna caritām kathām kṛtavān yāni rūpāṇi kathitāni divaukasaiḥ anugraha artham viprāṇām śṛṇu vatsa samāsatas tāni te kīrtayiṣyāmi yat mām tvam paripṛcchasi brahma viṣṇu sura indrāṇām rudra āditya aśvinām api viśveṣām api devānām vapuḥ dhārayate bhavaḥ narāṇām deva nārīṇām tathā preta piśācayoḥ kirāta śabarāṇām ca jalajānām anekaśas karoti bhagavān rūpam āṭavya śabarāṇi api kūrmaḥ matsyaḥ tathā śaṅkhaḥ pravāla aṅkura bhūṣaṇaḥ yakṣa rākṣasa sarpāṇām daitya dānavayoḥ api vyāghra siṃha mṛgāṇām ca tarakṣu ṛkṣa patatriṇām ulūka śva śṛgālānām rūpāṇi kurute api ca haṃsa kāka mayūrāṇām kṛkalāsaka sārasām rūpāṇi ca balākānām gṛdhra cakrāṅgayoḥ api go rūpī ca mahādevaḥ hasti aśva uṣṭra khara ākṛtiḥ chāga śārdūla rūpaḥ ca aneka mṛga rūpa dhṛk aṇḍajānām ca divyānām vapuḥ dhārayate bhavaḥ daṇḍī chatrī ca kuṇḍī ca dvijānām dhāraṇaḥ tathā aneka pāṇi pārśvaḥ ca aneka gaṇa saṃvṛtaḥ ṛṣi gandharva rūpaḥ ca siddha cāraṇa rūpadhṛk bhasma pāṇḍura gātraḥ ca candra ardha kṛta bhūṣaṇaḥ aneka rāva saṃghuṣṭaḥ ca aneka stuti saṃstutaḥ sarva bhūta antakaḥ sarvaḥ sarva loka pratiṣṭhitaḥ cakrī śūlī gadā pāṇiḥ musalī khaḍga paṭṭiśī bhūdharaḥ nāgamauñjī ca nāga kuṇḍala kuṇḍalī nāga yajña upavītī ca nāga carma uttaracchadaḥ vādayati api vādyāni vicitrāṇi gaṇaiḥ vṛtaḥ unmatta matta rūpam ca bhāṣate ca api susvaraḥ dadāti pratigṛhṇāti yuñjate dhyāyate api ca vedī madhye tathā yūpe goṣṭha madhye hutāśane dṛśyate dṛśyate ca api bālaḥ vṛddhaḥ yuvā tathā krīḍate ṛṣi kanyābhiḥ ṛṣi patnībhiḥ eva ca ūrdhva keśaḥ mahā śephaḥ nagnaḥ vikṛta locanaḥ gauraḥ śyāmaḥ tathā kṛṣṇaḥ pāṇḍuraḥ dhūma lohitaḥ arūpasya adya rūpasya atirūpa ādya rūpiṇaḥ hṛdi prāṇaḥ manaḥ jīvaḥ yoga ātmā yoga saṃjñitaḥ dhyānam tat paramātmā ca bhāva grāhyaḥ maheśvaraḥ evam varṣa sahasram tu divyam ārādhitaḥ mayā eka bhaktaḥ iti jñātvā jijñāsām kurute tadā satyam kauśika saṃsārāt mā mama astu vinirvṛtiḥ adya maheśvara baddhā kathā mama anyā priyā śrotum yeṣām na kṣaṇam api rucitaḥ hara caraṇa smaraṇa vicchedaḥ saṃsāra bhayam na bhavati hara bhakti rasāyanam pītvā divasam divasa ardham vā muhūrtam vā kṣaṇam lavam śvā api maheśvara vacanāt bhavāmi sa hi naḥ paraḥ kāmaḥ na nāka pṛṣṭham na ca deva rājyam na sarva kāmān akhilān vṛṇomi harasya dāsa tvam aham vṛṇomi dhik teṣām dhik teṣām punar api dhik astu dhik teṣām ātmānam paramam yasmāt varam tasmāt vṛṇīmahe abījāt bīja saṃbhūtam varam tasmāt vṛṇīmahe yam jñātvā na anuśocanti varam tasmāt vṛṇīmahe bhūta bhāvana bhāva jñam sarva bhūta abhibhāvanam sarvagam sarva dam devam pūjayāmi puraṃdara yam upāsanti tattva jñāḥ varam tasmāt vṛṇīmahe sarva bhūta gurum devam varam tasmāt vṛṇīmahe yaḥ pūrvam asṛjat devam brahmāṇam loka bhāvanam aṇḍam ākāśam āpūrya varam tasmāt vṛṇīmahe agniḥ āpaḥ anilaḥ pṛthvī kham buddhiḥ ca manaḥ mahān sraṣṭā ca eṣām bhavet yaḥ anyaḥ brūhi kaḥ parameśvarāt manaḥ matiḥ ahaṃkāraḥ tanmātrāṇi indriyāṇi ca brūhi ca eṣām bhavet śakra kaḥ anyaḥ asti paramaḥ śivāt sraṣṭāram bhuvanasya iha vadanti iha pitāmaham ārādhya sa tu deveśam aśnute mahatīm śriyam vidyate vai mahādevāt brūhi kaḥ parameśvarāt daitya dānava mukhyānām ādhipatya ari mardanāt kaḥ anyaḥ śaknoti deveśāt ṛte saṃpāditum sutān diś kāla sūrya tejāṃsi graha vāyu indu tārakāḥ viddhi tu ete mahādevāt brūhi kaḥ parameśvarāt kim ca atra bahubhiḥ sūktaiḥ hetuvādaiḥ puraṃdara sahasranayanam dṛṣṭvā tvām eva sura sattama cetana acetana ādyeṣu śakra viddhi maheśvarāt bhuva ādyeṣu mahā anteṣu loka aloka antareṣu ca dvīpa sthāneṣu meroḥ ca vibhaveṣu antareṣu ca yadi devāḥ surāḥ śakra paśyanti anyām bhava ākṛtim kim na gacchanti śaraṇam marditāḥ ca asuraiḥ surāḥ abhighāteṣu devānām sa yakṣa uraga rakṣasām vara dāna avaghātāya brūhi kaḥ anyaḥ maheśvarāt kasya ca ardhe sthitā kāntā anaṅgaḥ kena nirjitaḥ brūhi indra paramam sthānam kasya devaiḥ praśasyate śmaśāne kasya krīḍā artham nṛtye vā kaḥ abhibhāṣyate kasya aiśvaryam samānam vā bhūtaiḥ kaḥ vā api krīḍate kasya tulya balāḥ deva gaṇāḥ aiśvarya darpitāḥ kasmāt oṣadhi saṃpattiḥ kaḥ vā dhārayate vasu prakāmam krīḍate kaḥ vā trailokye sa carācare jñāna siddhi kriyā yogaiḥ sevyamānaḥ ca yogibhiḥ ṛṣi gandharva siddhaiḥ ca vihitam kāraṇam param karma yajña kriyā yogaiḥ sevyamānaḥ sura asuraiḥ nityam karma phalaiḥ hīnam tam aham kāraṇam vade sthūlam sūkṣmam anaupamyam agrāhyam guṇa gocaram guṇa hīnam guṇa adhyakṣam param māheśvaram padam bhūta a bhūta bhaviṣyat ca janakam sarva kāraṇam akṣara akṣaram avyaktam vidyā avidye kṛta akṛte dharma adharmau yatas śakra tam aham kāraṇam vade devadevena rudreṇa sṛṣṭi saṃhāra hetunā mātrā pūrvam mama ākhyātam kāraṇam loka kāraṇam nā asti ca īśāt param śakra tam prapadya yadi icchasi na anyam kāma sahasra kalpita dhiyaḥ śaṃsanti īśāt param kāma arthī varayāmi saṃyata manāḥ mokṣāya śarvam śivam na padma aṅkāḥ na cakra aṅkāḥ na vajra aṅkāḥ yatas prajāḥ liṅga aṅkā ca bhaga aṅkā ca tasmāt māheśvarī prajā devyāḥ kāraṇa rūpa bhāva janitāḥ sarvāḥ bhaga aṅkāḥ striyaḥ liṅgena api harasya sarva puruṣāḥ pratyakṣa cihnīkṛtāḥ yaḥ anyat kāraṇam īśvarāt pravadate devyā ca yat na aṅkitam pum liṅgam sarvam īśānam strī liṅgam viddhi ca api umām dvābhyām tanubhyām vyāptam hi carācaram idam jagat vajra sāra mayaiḥ śṛṅgaiḥ niṣṭapta kanaka prabhaiḥ kirīṭam ca jaṭā bhāraḥ sarpa ādi ābharaṇāni ca vajra ādi śūla mātaṅga gambhīra smitam āgatam sahasra bhuja jihvā āsyaḥ bhīṣaṇaḥ nāga vigrahaḥ śaṅkha śūla asibhiḥ ca eva paṭṭasaiḥ rūpavān sthitaḥ śūlam ca yauvana aśva ghnam lavaṇasya kare sthitam evam dṛṣṭvā mahādevam jānubhyām avanīm gataḥ paramam tīkṣṇa sāreṇa śastram tasya api yat purā sarva bhūta gaṇāḥ ca eva mātaraḥ vividhāḥ sthitāḥ jagat mūrtim mahā liṅgam tad madhye sphīta rūpiṇam ayutāni ca candra arkān apaśyam divi keśava namaḥ astu pīta varṇāya pīta ambara dharāya ca namaḥ astu ucchrita chatrāya kirīṭa vara dhāriṇe ardha hāra ardha keyūra ardha kuṇḍala karṇine namaḥ padma ardha mālāya utpalaiḥ miśritāya ca saumya rūpāya mukhyāya saumya daṃṣṭrā vibhūṣaṇe namaḥ śyāmāya gaurāya ardha pīta ardha pāṇḍure nārī nara śarīrāya strīpuṃsāya namaḥ astu te namaḥ astu gaṇa gītāya gaṇa vṛnda ratāya ca gaṇa anuyāta mārgāya gaṇa nitya vratāya ca namaḥ śveta abhra varṇāya saṃdhyā rāga prabhāya ca an uddiṣṭa abhidhānāya svarūpāya namaḥ astu te maṇi bhūṣita mūrdhāya namaḥ candra ardha bhūṣiṇe vicitra maṇi mūrdhāya kusuma aṣṭa dharāya ca bhūcarāya bhuvanāya anantāya śivāya ca sarpa kaṇṭha upahārāya vicitra ābharaṇāya ca strīpuṃsāya napuṃsāya namaḥ sāṃkhyāya yogine namaḥ sarva ārti nāśāya namaḥ śoka harāya ca bīja kṣetra adhipālāya sraṣṭārāya namaḥ namaḥ namaḥ sura asura īśāya viśveśāya namaḥ namaḥ namaḥ kāñcana mālāya giri mālāya vai namaḥ namaḥ surāri mālāya caṇḍavegāya vai namaḥ namaḥ strī rūpa dhārāya sarva rūpa dharāya ca namaḥ kāma aṅga nāśāya kāla daṇḍa dharāya ca namaḥ skanda viśākhāya brahmadaṇḍāya vai namaḥ namaḥ bhavāya śarvāya viśvarūpāya vai namaḥ īśānāya bhagaghnāya namaḥ astu andhakaghātine namaḥ viśvāya māyāya cintya acintyāya vai namaḥ abravam tam aham bhūyas praṇamya śirasā bhavam tam tvām praṇamya śirasā prasādya prārthaye prabho yaśasvī tejasā yuktaḥ divya jñāna samanvitaḥ ṛṣīṇām abhigamyaḥ ca mad prasādāt bhaviṣyasi tatra tatra eva sāṃnidhyam kariṣyati na saṃśayaḥ akṣayāḥ bāndhavāḥ ca eva kulam gotram ca te sadā bhaviṣyati dvijaśreṣṭha mayi bhaktiḥ ca śāśvatī paśya vṛkṣa latā gulmān sarva puṣpa phala pradān prasādāt devadevasya īśvarasya mahātmanaḥ vāsudevaḥ uvāca vismayam paramam gatvā abruvam tam mahā munim api tāvat mama api evam dadyāt sa bhagavān śivaḥ darśanam muni śārdūla prasādam ca api śaṃkaraḥ drakṣyase puṇḍarīkākṣa mahādevam na saṃśayaḥ acireṇa eva kālena yathā dṛṣṭaḥ mayā anagha ṣaṣṭhe māsi mahādevam drakṣyase puruṣottama ṣoḍaśa aṣṭau varān ca api prāpsyasi tvam maheśvarāt sa patnīkāt yaduśreṣṭha satyam etat bravīmi te atīta anāgatam ca eva vartamānam ca nityaśas viditam me mahā bāho prasādāt tasya dhīmataḥ evam kathayataḥ tasya mahādeva āśritām kathām maheśvaram nandi vināyaka ādyaiḥ ekādaśāḥ gatāḥ ca eva rudrāḥ ca enam sukha āvaham vṛkṣa oṣadhyaḥ ca rāja indra dhātavaḥ vividhāḥ tathā tvayā hi ārādhitaḥ ca aham śataśas atha sahasraśas tatas astuvam devadevam devīm ca śubha lakṣaṇām yoga īśvarāḥ su bahavaḥ maheśam bahudhā astuvan mahābhūtāni chandāṃsi prajānām patayaḥ makhāḥ saritaḥ sāgarāḥ nāgāḥ gandharva apsarasaḥ tathā vidyādharāḥ ca gītena vādya nṛtta ādinā arcayan tejasvinām madhya gatam tejorāśim jagatpatim īkṣitum ca mahādevam na me śaktiḥ abhūt tadā dṛṣṭvā devam tathā devīm astuvam saṃstutam suraiḥ tvayā sṛṣṭam idam kṛtsnam trailokyam sacarācaram mūrdhnā praṇamya tu bhavam devam īśānam avyayam gṛhe ca tubhyam prītimatām śatāni yam paṭhanti sadā sāṃkhyāḥ cintayanti ca yoginaḥ param pradhānam puruṣam adhiṣṭhātāram īśvaram utpattau ca vināśe ca kāraṇam yam viduḥ budhāḥ deva asura munīnām ca param yasmāt na vidyate ajam tam aham īśānam an ādi nidhanam prabhum niṣkalam sakalam brahma nirguṇam guṇa gocaram yoginām parama ānandam akṣaram mokṣa saṃjñitam durvijñeyam asaṃkhyeyam duṣprāpam akṛtātmabhiḥ param purāṇam puruṣam parebhyaḥ paratas param yaḥ prāṇavantam ātmānam jyotiḥ jīva sthitam manaḥ tam devam darśana ākāṅkṣī bahūn varṣa gaṇān ṛṣiḥ tapasi ugre sthitaḥ bhūtvā dṛṣṭvā tuṣṭāva ca īśvaram ṛtam satyam param brahma stuvanti ātharvaṇāḥ dvijāḥ evam stutaḥ mahādevaḥ taṇḍinā brahma vādinā vṛṣaḥ ca vṛṣa rūpaḥ ca vṛṣa sthaḥ vṛṣabha akṣa gaḥ bhīmaḥ ca śitikaṇṭhaḥ ca śivaḥ śvetaḥ śivottamaḥ niṣaṅgī śitikeśaḥ ca ditipaḥ nīlalohitaḥ śarabhaḥ śaṅkukarṇaḥ ca vilāsaḥ viśvakartṛkaḥ paraḥ pāśupataḥ bhadraḥ nīlagrīvaḥ vibhīṣaṇaḥ ca eva divyaḥ sūkṣmaḥ parāgaḥ ca vīrabhadraḥ vibhinna gaḥ śivaḥ śaṃbhuḥ bhuvaḥ bhartā śrīkaṇṭhaḥ tridaśa arcitaḥ aṇḍaḥ ca bahiraṇḍaḥ ca virāj bhūtaḥ viśeṣa kṛt jāgrat svapna suṣuptiḥ ca turīyaḥ ca vidhūtanaḥ manaḥ buddhiḥ ahaṃkāraḥ vyakta antaḥkaraṇa ātmakaḥ arūpaḥ caṇḍarūpaḥ ca vajrahastaḥ puraṃdaraḥ jātavedāḥ ca jāgartiḥ viśvāvāsaḥ vināyakaḥ ājānuḥ vainateyaḥ ca viśvakarmā vicakṣaṇaḥ hiraṇyākṣaḥ sukeśaḥ ca ṛkṣaḥ kakṣataraḥ tamaḥ sa kārmukaḥ mahā bāhuḥ mahā ghoraḥ mahā tapāḥ su tīkṣṇa daśanaḥ ca eva mahā kāyaḥ mahā ānanaḥ vīṇī ca paṇavī tālī nālī kaṭikaṭuḥ tathā bhikṣuḥ ca bhikṣurūpaḥ ca raudrarūpaḥ aṅgirāḥ khagaḥ vāsudevaḥ ca devaḥ ca vāmadevaḥ ca vāmanaḥ vajrahastaḥ pratiṣṭambhī camūruḥ stenaḥ eva ca nityaḥ hi anīśaḥ śuddha ātmā śuddhaḥ mānaḥ gatiḥ haviḥ mahā kaṭiḥ mahā grīvaḥ mahā bāhuḥ mahā karaḥ māyāvī sarva kāma kṛt mantavyaḥ prāṇeśaḥ bandhakī vṛkṣaḥ nakulaḥ ca adrikaḥ tathā hrasvagrīvaḥ mahājānuḥ alolaḥ ca mahauṣadhiḥ vasu bhṛt sarva bhūta ātmā niścalaḥ su viduḥ budhaḥ asuhṛd sarva bhūtānām niścalaḥ cala vid budhaḥ śirodharaḥ abhimarśanaḥ sarva lakṣaṇa bhūṣitaḥ girāvāsaḥ visargaḥ ca sarva lakṣaṇa lakṣa vid japata bahubhiḥ īritam viśeṣyam namaḥ śivāya iti sadā pradānam bhagavān iti bhaktyā hi ananyam īśānam param devam sanātanam karmaṇā manasā vācā bhāvena amita tejasaḥ svapantaḥ jāgramāṇāḥ ca vrajan upaviśan tathā unmiṣan nimiṣan ca eva cintayantaḥ punar punar śṛṇvantaḥ śrāvayantaḥ ca kathayantaḥ ca te bhavam idam puṇyam pavitram ca sarvadā pāpa nāśanam yoga dam mokṣa dam ca eva svarga dam toṣa dam tathā yā gatiḥ sāṃkhya yogānām vrajanti etām gatim tadā kapilaḥ ca tatas prāha sāṃkhya ṛṣiḥ deva saṃmataḥ mayā janmāni anekāni śaktyā ca ārādhitaḥ bhavaḥ prītaḥ ca bhagavān jñānam dadau mama bhava antakam viśvāmitraḥ tadā uvāca kṣatriyaḥ aham purā abhavam brāhmaṇaḥ aham bhavāni iti mayā ca ārādhitaḥ bhavaḥ tad prasādāt mayā prāptam brāhmaṇyam durlabham mahat suvarṇākṣaḥ mahādevaḥ tapasā toṣitaḥ mayā tatas atha bhagavān āha prītaḥ mām vai yudhiṣṭhira annāt priyataraḥ kṛṣṇa mad prasādāt bhaviṣyasi evam sahasraśas ca ātmā mahādevaḥ varam dadau maṇimanthe atha śaile vai purā saṃrādhitaḥ mayā varṣa ayuta sahasrāṇām sahasra śatam eva ca tatas mām bhagavān prītaḥ idam vacanam abravīt varam vṛṇīṣva bhadram te yaḥ te manasi vartate jaigīṣavyaḥ tathā prāha jagaddhātā maheśvaraḥ parāśaraḥ ca āha muniḥ purā mama ca toṣitaḥ tat abhijñāya mām āha śaṃkaraḥ tripura ardanaḥ pitṛ kanyā vasoḥ putrī śāpāt dāśa gṛha uditā tasyām viṣṇukṛtā nāma vidyayā śodhakaḥ satām māṇḍavyaḥ ca āha deveśa bhaktaḥ loka hite bhave vāsudevaḥ uvāca aśubhaiḥ pāpa karmāṇaḥ ye narāḥ kaluṣīkṛtāḥ īśānam na prapadyante tamaḥ rājasa vṛttayaḥ īśvaram saṃprapadyante dvijāḥ bhāvita bhāvanāḥ sadṛśaḥ araṇya vāsīnām munīnām bhāvitātmanām brahma tvam keśava tvam vā śakra tvam vā suraiḥ saha manasā api śivam tāta ye prapadyanti mānavāḥ yajet devam virūpākṣam na sa pāpena lipyate sarva lakṣaṇa hīnaḥ api yuktaḥ vā sarva pātakaiḥ sarvam tudati tat pāpam bhāvayan śivam ātmanā kīṭa pakṣi pataṃgānām tiraścām api keśava na te saṃsāra vaśa gāḥ iti me niścitā matiḥ tatas kṛṣṇaḥ abravīt vākyam dharmaputram yudhiṣṭhiram an anya strī janaḥ prājñaḥ hi a pravāsī priyaṃvadaḥ surūpaḥ saṃmataḥ vīraḥ śīlavān bhoga bhuj śuciḥ dāra anumata yajñaḥ ca sunakṣatrām atha udvahet kailāse śaṃkara āvāsam abhivīkṣya praṇamya ca gaurīśam śaṃkaram dāntam śaraṇa āgata vatsalam gaṅgā dharam gopati gam gaṇa āvṛtam akalmaṣam punar ca pātakam dṛṣṭam vipra kṣetre viśeṣataḥ prāyaścittam mahat atas dāra grahaṇa pūrvakam bījam na śudhyate voḍhuḥ asya ca a kṛta niṣkṛteḥ mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaḥ śuddhaḥ jñeyaḥ putraḥ yathārthataḥ atha ardharātre sā strī tu śayanam tat upāgamat aṣṭāvakraḥ atha sa muniḥ cintayām āsa vismitaḥ vidvān su śīlaḥ puruṣaḥ sa dāraḥ sukham aśnute nikṛtiḥ strī bhoga parā priya vāda apravāsanāt yudhiṣṭhiraḥ vaiśya yonyām samutpannāḥ śūdra yonyām tathā eva ca brahmarṣayaḥ iti proktāḥ purāṇāḥ dvijasattamāḥ bhīṣmaḥ alam kautūhalena atra nibodha tvam yudhiṣṭhira īśanti ātmanaḥ iti etat gatiḥ ca eṣām na sajjate brahma bhūyāṃsaḥ iti eva ṛṣayaḥ śruti coditāḥ nindyāḥ na ca ete rāja indra pramāṇam hi pramāṇinām evam mahātmabhiḥ tāta tapaḥ jñāna samanvitaiḥ pravartitāni kāryāṇi pramāṇāni eva sattama bhāryāḥ catasraḥ rāja indra brāhmaṇasya svadharmataḥ brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā śūdrā ca bharata ṛṣabha rājñām tu kṣatriyā vaiśyā śūdrā ca bharata ṛṣabha vaiśyasya vaiśyā vihitā śūdrā ca bharata ṛṣabha śūdrasya ekā smṛtā bhāryā pratilome tu saṃkaraḥ śūdrāyāḥ tu nara śreṣṭha catvāraḥ patayaḥ smṛtāḥ varṇa uttamāyāḥ tu patiḥ savarṇaḥ tu ekaḥ eva saḥ dvau kṣatriyāyāḥ vihitau brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyaḥ tathā vaiśyāyāḥ tu nara śreṣṭha vihitāḥ patayaḥ trayaḥ savarṇaḥ kṣatriyaḥ ca eva brāhmaṇaḥ ca viśām pate evam vidhim anusmṛtya tatas te ṛṣibhiḥ purā utpāditāḥ mahātmānaḥ putrāḥ brahmarṣayaḥ purā purāṇābhyām ṛṣibhyām tu mitreṇa varuṇena ca kakṣīvān ārṣṭiṣeṇaḥ ca puruṣaḥ kakṣaḥ eva ca māmateyasya vai putrāḥ gautamasya ātmajāḥ smṛtāḥ vatsapriyaḥ ca bhagavān sthūlaraśmiḥ tathā akṣaṇiḥ kapiṃjalādaḥ brahmarṣiḥ caṇḍālyām udapadyata vainateyaḥ tathā pakṣī turyāyām ca vasiṣṭhataḥ prasādāt ca vasiṣṭhasya śulka abhyupagamena ca adṛśyantyāḥ pitā vaiśyaḥ nāmnā citramukhaḥ purā vaiśyaḥ citramukhaḥ kanyām vasiṣṭha tanayasya vai śubhām prādāt yatas jātaḥ brahmarṣiḥ tu parāśaraḥ tathā eva dāśa kanyāyām satyavatyām mahān ṛṣiḥ parāśarāt prasūtaḥ ca vyāsaḥ yoga mayaḥ muniḥ vibhaṇḍakasya mṛgyām ca tapaḥ yoga ātmakaḥ muniḥ ṛśyaśṛṅgaḥ samutpannaḥ brahmacārī mahā yaśāḥ śāṅgaryām ca mandapālasya catvāraḥ brahma vādinaḥ jātāḥ brahmarṣayaḥ puṇyāḥ yaiḥ stutaḥ havyavāhanaḥ droṇaḥ ca stambamitraḥ ca śārisṛkvaḥ ca buddhimān jaritāriḥ ca vikhyātāḥ catvāraḥ sūrya saṃnibhāḥ mahā ṛṣeḥ kālavṛkṣasya śakuntyām eva jajñivān hiraṇyahastaḥ bhagavān mahā ṛṣiḥ kāñcana prabhaḥ pāvakāt tāta saṃbhūtāḥ manasā ca mahā ṛṣayaḥ savarṇaḥ ca api rājarṣiḥ savarṇāyām ajāyata mṛṇmayyām bharata śreṣṭhaḥ ādityena vivasvatā śāṇḍilyaḥ ca agnitaḥ jātaḥ kāśyapasya ātmajaḥ prabhuḥ śaradvataḥ śara stambāt kṛpaḥ ca kṛpayā saha reṇuḥ ca reṇukā ca eva rāma mātā yaśasvinī yamunāyām samudbhūtaḥ somakena mahātmanā arkadattaḥ mahān ṛṣiḥ prathitaḥ pṛthivī tale dhṛṣṭadyumnaḥ ca saṃbhūtaḥ vedyām kṛṣṇā ca bhārata vyāghra yonyām tatas jātāḥ vasiṣṭhasya mahātmanaḥ ekonaviṃśatiḥ putrāḥ khyātāḥ vyāghrapada ādayaḥ mandhaḥ ca bādalomaḥ ca jābāliḥ ca mahān ṛṣiḥ ete ca anye ca vikhyātāḥ pṛthivyām gotra tām gatāḥ viśvakāśasya rājarṣeḥ aikṣvākoḥ tu mahātmanaḥ bālāśvaḥ nāma putraḥ abhūt śikhām bhittvā viniḥsṛtaḥ māndhātā ca eva rājarṣiḥ yuvanāśvena dhīmatā gaurikaḥ ca api rājarṣiḥ cakravartī mahā yaśāḥ bāhudāyām samutpannaḥ nadyām rājñā subāhunā bhūmeḥ ca putraḥ narakaḥ saṃvartaḥ ca eva puṣkalaḥ adbhiḥ ca eva mahā tejāḥ ṛṣiḥ gārgyaḥ hi ajāyata ete ca anye ca bahavaḥ rājanyāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ tathā prabhāvena abhisaṃbhūtāḥ mahā ṛṣīṇām mahātmanām na asādhyam tapasā teṣām vidyayā ātma guṇaiḥ paraiḥ dharmam praṇayatā rājan tam nibodha yudhiṣṭhira ṛṣīṇām ca nadīnām ca sādhūnām ca mahātmanām prabhavaḥ na adhigantavyaḥ strīṇām duścaritasya ca tat nā atra cintā kartavyā mahā ṛṣīṇām samudbhave yudhiṣṭhiraḥ putraiḥ katham mahā rāja puruṣaḥ tāritaḥ bhavet bhīṣmaḥ atrā api udāharanti imam itihāsam purātanam nāradena purā gītam mārkaṇḍeyāya pṛcchate āraṇeyam ca raibhyam ca etān atra āgatān purā dṛṣṭvā pūrvam samāsīnān mārkaṇḍeyaḥ abhyagacchata ṛṣayaḥ tu munim dṛṣṭvā samutthāya unmukhāḥ sthitāḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ ayam samāgamaḥ sadbhiḥ yatnena āsāditaḥ mayā atra prāpsyāmi dharmāṇām ācārasya ca niścayam ṛjuḥ kṛta yuge dharmaḥ tasmin kṣīṇe vimuhyati ṛṣibhiḥ nāradaḥ proktaḥ brūhi yatra asya saṃśayaḥ dharma adharmeṣu tattva jña tvam hi chettāsi saṃśayān ṛṣibhyaḥ anumatam vākyam niyogāt nāradaḥ abravīt sarva dharma artha tattva jñam mārkaṇḍeyam tatas abravīt tapasā dīpta veda vedāṅga
