mahān ātmā guṇānām iti naḥ prabho guṇa stha tvāt guṇam tu enam āhuḥ avyakta lakṣaṇam budhyamānaḥ hi ahaṃkārī nityam māna a prabodhanāt vimala udāra śīlaḥ syāt budhyamāna aprabuddhayoḥ draṣṭā bhavati abhoktā ca sattva mūrtiḥ ca nirguṇaḥ upekṣakaḥ śuciḥ tābhyām ubhābhyām ayutaḥ tathā aham eva sṛjati anyam dvitīyam loka sārathiḥ sarva bhāvaiḥ ahaṃkāraiḥ tṛtīyam sarga saṃjñitam tatas bhūtāni ahaṃkāram ahaṃkāraḥ manaḥ asṛjat tathā eva yajñe bhūteṣu viṣaya arthī punar punar indriyaiḥ saha śūla aṅka pañca pañcabhiḥ eva ca manaḥ veda na ca ātmānam ahaṃkāram prajāpatiḥ na veda vā api ahaṃkāraḥ buddhim buddhimatām vara ahaṃkāreṇa saṃyuktaḥ srotasi abhimukhaḥ sadā evam eṣa vikāra ātmā mahāpuruṣa saṃjñakaḥ saṃviśat rajanīm kṛtsnām niśā ante divasa āgame sa punar punar ātmānam na kuryāt ākṣipeta ca pañcaviṃśam mahādeva mahāpuruṣa vaikṛtam guṇān punar ca tān eva saḥ ātmanā ātmani nikṣipet avyaktasya saḥ ajñānasya tama utmanaḥ upekṣakaḥ śuciḥ vyagraḥ saḥ aliṅgaḥ saḥ avraṇaḥ amalaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ bhagavān āste buddhaḥ śuddhaḥ nirāmayaḥ etaiḥ eva vihīnam tu ke cid āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ evam eṣa vikāra ātmā budhyamānaḥ mahā bhuja te avyaktam śaṃkara āgāḍham muñcante śāstra buddhayaḥ teṣām evam tu vadatām śāstra artham sūkṣma darśinām sā eṣā trayī parā vidyā caturthī ānvīkṣikī smṛtā ajñāna bandhanāt muktāḥ tīrṇāḥ saṃsāra bandhanāt ajñāna sāgaram ghoram agādham tam asaṃjñakam eṣā vidyā tathā avidyā kathitā te mayā arthataḥ yasmin deyam ca no grāhyam sāṃkhyāḥ sāṃkhyam tathā eva ca tathā ca eka tva nānātvam akṣaram kṣaram eva ca nigadiṣyāmi deveśa vimokṣam trividham ca te yat ca kim cit śrutam na syāt dṛṣṭam na eva ca kim cana sanatkumāraḥ tat ca te saṃpravakṣyāmi ekāgraḥ śṛṇu tatparaḥ ariṣṭāni pravakṣyāmi tattvena śṛṇu tat bhavān madhye uttaratas tāta dakṣiṇā mukha niṣṭhitam vidyut saṃsthāna puruṣam yadi paśyeta mānavaḥ etat phalam ariṣṭasya śaṃkara āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ śuddha maṇḍalam ādityam a raśmim atha paśyataḥ jānīyāt chidrām candramasaḥ chāyām pādau api anupaśyataḥ saṃvatsareṇa jānīyāt deha nyāsam upasthitam kanīnikāyām aśiraḥ puruṣam yadi paśyati jānīyāt ṣaṭsu māseṣu deha nyāsam upasthitam karṇau pidhāya hastābhyām śabdam na śṛṇuyāt yadi sarva aṅga dhāraṇa avasthām dhārayeta samāhitaḥ atha nā icchet ciram kālam tyajet ātmānam ātmanā atha tam nirvikāram tu prakṛteḥ paramam śuciḥ puruṣam tena sādharmyam deha nyāsam upāśnuyāt jāgrataḥ hi mayā uktāni tava ariṣṭāni tattvataḥ dhāraṇāt ca eva sarva aṅge mṛtyum jīyāt surarṣabha ekatva darśanam bhūyas nānātvam ca nibodha me akṣaram ca kṣaram ca eva catuṣṭaya vidhānataḥ avyakta ādīni tattvāni sarvāṇi eva mahā dyute āhuḥ caturviṃśatimam vikāram puruṣa anvitam ekatva darśanam ca eva nānātvena avaram smṛtam pañcaviṃśati vargaḥ syāt apavargaḥ ajara amaraḥ saḥ eva pañcaviṃśaḥ tu vikāraḥ puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ vikārāḥ vidyamānāḥ tu tattva saṃjñakaḥ ucyate yadi eṣaḥ avyakta tām nā eti vyatirekāt na saṃśayaḥ vikāra guṇa saṃtyāgāt prakṛti anya tvataḥ śuciḥ tadā nānātva tām eti sarga hīnaḥ apavarga bhāj akṣaraḥ ca bhavati eṣa yathā vā cyutavān kṣaṇāt avyaktā vyaktiḥ uktā syāt nirguṇasya guṇa ākarāt vyākhyātam tava śūla aṅka tathā ariṣṭāni ca eva hi vimokṣa lakṣaṇam śeṣam tat api iha bravīmi te yam jñātvā yatayaḥ prāptāḥ kevala tvam anāmayam sāṃkhyāḥ ca api atha yogāḥ ca dagdha paṅkāḥ gata jvarāḥ amūrti tvam anuprāptā nirguṇā nirbhayā bhava vipāpmānaḥ mahādeva muktāḥ saṃsāra sāgarāt saraṇe prajana ādāne guṇānām prakṛtiḥ sadā parā pramattā satatam etāvat kārya kāraṇam vyatiriktaḥ ca śuddhaḥ ca saḥ anyaḥ ca aprakṛtiḥ tathā upekṣakaḥ ca prakṛteḥ vikāra puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ vikāra puruṣeṇa eṣā saṃyuktā sṛjate jagat matsya udakāt sa saṃjñātaḥ prakṛteḥ eva karmaṇaḥ evam eṣa hi asat sat ca vikāra puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ vikāra āpadyamānam tu vikṛtim pravadanti naḥ yadā tu eṣa vikārasya prakṛtāni iti manyate tadā vikāra tām eti vikāra anya tva tām vrajet a yuktam niṣkalam śuddham avyaya ajaram avraṇam udumbarāt vā maśakaḥ pralayāt nirgataḥ yathā tathā avyaktasya saṃtyāgāt nirmamaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ ākāśe iva niḥsaṅgaḥ tathā asaṅgaḥ tathā varaḥ ādisargaḥ mahā bāho tāmasena āvṛtam param sanakaḥ sanandanaḥ ca eva tṛtīyaḥ ca sanātanaḥ avyaktāt paramāt sūkṣmāt avraṇāt mūrti saṃjñakāt kṣetrajñaḥ bhagavān āste nārāyaṇa parāyaṇaḥ asmākam sahajātānām utpannam jñānam uttamam punar punar prajātā vai tatra tatra pinākadhṛk kapilāt mūlaācāryāt tattva buddhi viniścayam tena saṃbodhitāḥ śiṣyāḥ bahavaḥ tattva darśinaḥ tat buddhvā bahavaḥ śiṣyāḥ mayā api etat nidarśitāḥ janma mṛtyu haram tathyam jñānam jñeyam sanātanam yat jñātvā na anuśocanti tattva jñānāt nirindriyāḥ śuddha bīja malāḥ ca eva vipaṅkāḥ vai nirakṣarāḥ svatantrāḥ te svatantreṇa saṃmitāḥ niṣkalāḥ smṛtāḥ śāśvatāḥ ca avyayāḥ ca eva tamaḥ grāhyāḥ ca bhāsvarāḥ vipāpmānaḥ tathā sarve sattva sthāḥ ca api nirvraṇāḥ vimuktāḥ kevalāḥ ca eva vīta moha bhayāḥ tathā amūrtāḥ te mahābhāga sarve ca vigata jvarāḥ hiraṇyanābhaḥ triśirāḥ tathā prahrāda bhāskarau vasuḥ viśvāvasuḥ ca eva sārdham pañcaśikhaḥ tathā ke cit muktāḥ sthitāḥ ke cid chandataḥ ca apare mṛtāḥ darśitāḥ trividham bandham vimokṣam trividham tathā ajñānam ca eva rāgaḥ ca saṃyogam prākṛtam tathā etebhyaḥ bandhanam proktam vimokṣam api me śṛṇu paritas tāvatā samyak saṃbandhaḥ yāvatā kṛtaḥ kṛtsna kṣaya parityāgāt vimokṣaḥ iti naḥ śrutiḥ bhīṣmaḥ nivṛttaḥ sarva saṅgebhyaḥ kevalaḥ puruṣaḥ amalaḥ iti evam uktvā bhagavān īśvarāya mahātmane sanatkumāraḥ prayayau ākāśam samupāśritaḥ nārāyaṇe atha rudre vā bhaktiḥ tīrtham param matam yudhiṣṭhiraḥ sarveṣām upavāsānām yat śreyaḥ su mahat phalam bhīṣmaḥ śṛṇu rājan yathā gītam svayam eva svayaṃbhuvā yat kṛtvā nirvṛtaḥ bhūyāt puruṣaḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ dvādaśyām mārgaśīrṣe tu ahorātreṇa keśavam arcya aśvamedham āpnoti duṣkṛtam ca asya naśyati tathā eva pauṣa māse tu pūjya nārāyaṇa iti ca ahorātreṇa dvādaśyām māgha māse tu mādhavam ahorātreṇa dvādaśyām caitre viṣṇuḥ iti smaran vaiśākha māse dvādaśyām pūjayan madhusūdanam ahorātreṇa dvādaśyām jyeṣṭhe māsi trivikramam āṣāḍhe māsi dvādaśyām vāmana iti ca pūjayet ahorātreṇa dvādaśyām śrāvaṇe māsi śrīdharam pañcayajñān avāpnoti vimāna sthaḥ sa modate tathā bhādrapade māsi hṛṣīkeśa iti pūjayan sautrāmaṇim avāpnoti pūta ātmā bhavate ca hi dvādaśyām āśvine māsi padmanābha iti ca arcayan go sahasra phalam puṇyam prāpnuyāt na atra saṃśayaḥ dvādaśyām kārttike māsi pūjya dāmodara iti ca gavām yajñam avāpnoti pumān strī vā na saṃśayaḥ abhyarcya puṇḍarīkākṣam evam saṃvatsaram tu yaḥ atas param na upavāsaḥ bhavati iti viniścayaḥ uvāca bhagavān viṣṇuḥ svayam eva purātanam vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ śara talpa gatam bhīṣmam vṛddham kuru pitāmaham upagamya mahā prājñaḥ paryapṛcchat yudhiṣṭhiraḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ jñānam rūpam ca saubhāgyam priya tvam ca katham bhavet bhīṣmaḥ dharma artha kāma saṃyuktaḥ sukha bhāgī katham bhavet mārgaśīrṣasya māsasya candre mūlena saṃyute pādau mūlena rāja indra jaṅghāyām atha rohiṇīm aśvinyām sakthinī ca eva ūrū ca āṣāḍhayoḥ tathā guhyam tu phālgunī vidyāt kṛttikā kaṭikāḥ tathā nābhim bhādrapade vidyāt revatyām akṣi maṇḍalam pṛṣṭham eva dhaniṣṭhāsu anurādhā uttarāḥ tathā bāhubhyām tu viśākhāsu hastau hasteṣu nirdiśet punarvasu aṅgulī rājan āśleṣāsu nakhāḥ tathā grīvā jyeṣṭhā ca rāja indra śravaṇena tu karṇayoḥ mukham puṣyeṇa rāja indra danta uṣṭhau svātiḥ ucyate hastam śatabhiṣām ca eva maghām ca eva atha nāsikām netre mṛgaśiraḥ vidyāt lalāṭam mitram eva tu bharaṇyām tu śiraḥ vidyāt keśān ārdrām narādhipa samāpte tu ghṛtam dadyāt brāhmaṇe vedapārage subhagaḥ darśanīyaḥ ca jñānī bhogī ca jāyate jāyate paripūrṇa aṅgaḥ paurṇamāsyām iva uḍurāj tatas asya karma paśyanti śubham vā yadi vā aśubham devatāḥ pañcabhūta sthāḥ kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi bhoga vaśyam karma vaśyam yātanā vaśyam iti api etat trayāṇām āsādya karmataḥ saḥ aśnute phalam iha sthānāni puṇyāni deva tulyāni bhū tale kukkuṭaḥ pañca varṣāṇi pañca varṣāṇi jambukaḥ gardabha tvam tu saṃprāpya daśa varṣāṇi jīvati saṃvatsaram tu kumbhīraḥ tatas jāyati mānavaḥ putrasya mātā pitarau yasya ruṣṭau ubhau api tatas śvā ca api bhavati tatas bhavati mānuṣaḥ asūyakaḥ kutsitaḥ ca caṇḍālaḥ duḥkham aśnute mūṣakaḥ jāyate paścāt sūkaraḥ jāyate tatas śvā biḍālaḥ kukkuṭaḥ ca jāyeta api pipīlikāḥ vilāḷaḥ pañca varṣāṇi pañca varṣāṇi kukkuṭaḥ śvā bhūtvā sapta janmāni tatas jāyati mānavaḥ dvija grāma ādhipatyam hi kṛtvā brahma sva bhakṣakaḥ śvāna yoni śatam prāpya caṇḍālaḥ puṣkalaḥ tatas varṣāt pañcadaśāt pūrvam kramāt bhavati mānuṣaḥ anyāḥ ca yātanāḥ prāpya kṛmi yonau prajāyate mṛgaḥ bhūtvā tatas śastraiḥ vadhyate bhṛśa dāruṇam daśan vai mānuṣān nityam pāpa ātmā sa viśām pate ghṛtam hṛtvā tu durbuddhiḥ kākamadguḥ prajāyate matsya māṃsam atho hṛtvā kākaḥ jāyati durmatiḥ phalāni vānaraḥ hṛtvā apūpam hṛtvā pipīlikaḥ corayitvā tu niṣpāvam jāyate hala golakaḥ kauśikam tu tatas hṛtvā naraḥ jāyati vartakaḥ aṃśukam corayitvā tu śukaḥ jāyati mānavaḥ corayitvā dukūlam tu mṛtaḥ haṃsaḥ prajāyate sarpaḥ kārpāsakam hṛtvā kṣaumam hṛtvā śaśaḥ bhavet chāgaḥ ca api āvikam hṛtvā kūrmaḥ carma apahārakaḥ tatra jīvati varṣāṇi tatas daśa ca pañca ca adharmasya kṣayam gatvā tatas jāyati mānuṣaḥ karmaṇā ca kṛtena iha kena yānti śubhām gatim tathā tathā śarīram tu tena adharmeṇa mucyate yadi vyāharate rājan viprāṇām dharma vādinām suvarṇa dānāt pāpāni naśyanti su bahūni api dattvā vṛtti karīm bhūmim pātakena api mucyate pārāyaṇaiḥ ca vedānām mucyate pātakaiḥ dvijaḥ gāyatryāḥ ca eva lakṣeṇa go sahasrasya tarpaṇāt veda artham jñāpayitvā tu śuddhān viprān yathārthataḥ sarva ātithyam param hi eṣām tasmāt annam param smṛtam śūdra annam nā eva bhoktavyam vipraiḥ dharma parāyaṇaiḥ hiṃsām tu na upayuñjīta tathā hiṃsā caturvidhā kāye manasi vākye ca doṣāḥ hi ete prakīrtitāḥ māṃsam moha samāviṣṭaḥ puruṣaḥ saḥ adhamaḥ smṛtaḥ hiṃsām kṛtvā avaśaḥ pāpaḥ bhūyiṣṭham jāyate tathā pareṣām dhana dhānyānām hiṃsakāḥ stāvakāḥ tathā praśaṃsakāḥ ca māṃsasya nityam svarge bahiṣkṛtāḥ bhasma viṣṭhā kṛmiḥ vā api niṣṭhā yasya īdṛśī dhruvā sa kāyaḥ para pīḍābhiḥ katham dhāryaḥ vipaścitā a viśvāsyaḥ avasīdet saḥ iti ha uvāca nāradaḥ vikīrṇa kaṇṭakena api tṛṇa praspandane bhayam mṛtyutaḥ bhayam asti iti śaṅkāyām duḥkham uttaram rākṣasa indra bhayāt muktāḥ sarva bhūta parāyaṇāḥ udyateṣu ca śastreṣu mṛga vyāla bhayeṣu ca śaraṇyaḥ sarva bhūtānām viśvāsyaḥ sarva jantuṣu bhīṣmaḥ mārkaṇḍeyena yat ca uktam śṛṇu me paramam vacaḥ yudhiṣṭhira mahā bāho ye doṣāḥ māṃsa bhakṣaṇe vidhinā api avaidikena iti dāntānām iha niścayaḥ kṛtaḥ hi manasaḥ yajñaḥ tena devāḥ ca tarpitāḥ satyam vadata mā a satyam satyam dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ etaiḥ ca anyaiḥ ca rāja indra kṛpeṇa bharatena ca virūpākṣeṇa kurūṣeṇa nṛga alarka naraiḥ tathā imam dharmam a māṃsa ādam yaḥ caret śrāvayeta vā paṭhet vā yaḥ idam rājan śṛṇuyāt vā api abhīkṣṇaśas a māṃsa bhakṣaṇa vidhim pavitram ṛṣi pūjitam ahiṃsraḥ dāna śīlaḥ ca madhu māṃsa vivarjitaḥ yathā etat yādṛśam ca etat guṇāḥ ye ca asya varjane nāśaḥ bhakṣaṇa doṣasya dānam eva satām matam kṣudhitānām dvijānām ca sarveṣām ca eva jīvanam dattvā bhavati pūta ātmā śraddhayā lobha varjitaḥ śikṣayanti na yācante darśayantaḥ sva mūrtibhiḥ dāna ādyaiḥ su śuciḥ māṃsam punar nā eva sa bhakṣayet rakṣaṇa arthāya bhūtānām hiṃsrān hanyāt mṛgān punar agastyena purā rājan māṃsa bhakṣam tu jāṅgale tyaktvā akasmāt niśāyām ca na dattam abhayam mayā tena eṣā kṛmi tā prāptā bhayasya jananī sadā śakaṭa vrajaḥ ca su mahān āgataḥ ca yadṛcchayā cakra ākrameṇa bhinnaḥ ca kīṭaḥ prāṇān mumoca ha saṃbhūtaḥ kṣatriya kule prasādāt amita ojasaḥ dadarśa ca mahā rāja saṃprāptam rāja putra tām etāsu eva krameṇa eva yoniṣu āsa sva karmaṇā paryāyeṇa tatas prāpya kṣātram sthānam anuttamam pālanāt ca viśiṣṭānām su yuddha maraṇena ca śiṣṭa saṃsevanāt ca eva sa gatim paramām gataḥ tatas sa para rāṣṭreṇa rājānam pīḍitāḥ prajāḥ ājagāma tatas prājñaḥ kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaḥ tadā pārāśaryam mahā prājñam upayāntam dayā param ajñāna timira ādityam nityam vyāsam namāmi aham iti uktvā patitam padbhyām prītyā prāha mahā muniḥ brāhmaṇān pūjayasva iha japa satya parāyaṇaḥ yajña dāna tapaḥ śīlāḥ narāḥ vai puṇya karmiṇaḥ ye abhidruhyanti bhūtāni te vai pāpa kṛtaḥ janāḥ nityam ca akṛpaṇaḥ bhuṅkte sva janaiḥ dehi yācitaḥ bhāgya kṣayeṇa kṣīyante na upabhogena saṃcayāḥ andham syāt tamaḥ eva idam na prajñāyeta kim cana cāturvarṇyam na varteta dharma adharmau ṛta anṛte a saṃskārāt kṣatra vaiśyau naśyete brāhmaṇāt ṛte śūdraḥ naśyati a śuśrūṣuḥ āśramāṇām yathārhataḥ yaḥ tārayati vai vidvān pitṝn devān samādṛtān tat tat bahu guṇam dānam śraddhayā sva guṇaiḥ api brāhmaṇaḥ sa mahābhāga taret saṃsāra sāgarāt vidyayā gata mohānām tāraṇāya hariḥ smṛtaḥ tad bhaktāḥ na vinaśyanti hi aṣṭa akṣara parāyaṇāḥ praṇava upāsana parāḥ parama artha parāḥ tu iha etaiḥ pāvaya ca ātmānam bhīṣmaḥ agni saṃrakṣaṇa parā gṛha śuddhim ca kāraye pātrāṇām dhana dhānyānām śayana āsana vastunām kumārān pālaye nityam kumārīḥ pariśikṣaye ātma priyāṇi hitvā api garbha saṃrakṣaṇe ratā bālānām varjaye nityam śāpam kopam pratāpanam a vikṣiptāni dhānyāni na anna vikṣepaṇam gṛhe samudgamya ca śuddhā aham bhikṣām dadyām dvijātiṣu yudhiṣṭhiraḥ yat jñeyam paramam kṛtyam anuṣṭheyam mahātmabhiḥ bhīṣmaḥ sāram me sarva śāstrāṇām vaktum arhasi anugrahāt śrūyatām idam atyantam gūḍham saṃsāra mocanam śrotavyam ca tvayā samyak jñātavyam ca viśām pate puṇḍarīkaḥ purā vipraḥ puṇya tīrthe japa anvitaḥ nāradaḥ ca abravīt enam brahmaṇā uktam mahātmanā śṛṇuṣva avahitaḥ tāta jñāna yogam anuttamam aprabhūtam prabhūta artham veda śāstra artha sārakam yaḥ paraḥ prakṛteḥ proktaḥ puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ narāt jātāni tattvāni nārāṇi iti tatas viduḥ tāni eva ca ayanam tasya tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ nārāyaṇāt jagat sarvam sarga kāle prajāyate nārāyaṇaḥ param brahma tattvam nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ parāt api paraḥ ca asau tasmāt na asti parāt param vāsudevam tathā viṣṇum ātmānam ca tathā viduḥ saṃjñā bhedaiḥ saḥ eva ekaḥ sarva śāstra abhisaṃskṛtaḥ āloḍya sarva śāstrāṇi vicārya ca punar punar idam ekam su niṣpannam dhyeyaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ sadā tasmāt tvam gahanān sarvān tyaktvā śāstra artha vistarān an anya cetāḥ dhyāyasva nārāyaṇam ajam vibhum muhūrtam api yaḥ dhyāyet nārāyaṇam atandritaḥ namaḥ nārāyaṇāya iti yaḥ veda brahma śāśvatam śravaṇāt mananāt ca eva gīti stuti arcana ādibhiḥ ārādhyam sarvadā brahma puruṣeṇa hita eṣiṇā lipyate na sa pāpena nārāyaṇa parāyaṇaḥ punāti sakalam lokam sahasrāṃśuḥ iva uditaḥ keśava ārādhanam hitvā nā eva yāti parām gatim svapne api puṇḍarīkākṣam śaṅkha cakra gadā dharam tam dṛṣṭvā devadeveśam prāṇamat saṃbhrama anvitaḥ atha kālena mahatā tathā pratyakṣatām gataḥ gataḥ saṃpūjitaḥ sarvaiḥ sa yogi nilayaḥ hariḥ sa guṇam aguṇam ādyam sthūlam atyanta sūkṣmam tribhuvana gurum īśam tvam prapadyasva viṣṇum nūnam śaktyā samārambhān vihitān su mahā udayān vipanna arthān vijānāsi tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ eka dravya niviṣṭena tulya artha śruta saṃpadā paribhūtaḥ asi nūnam tvam tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ iṣṭa bhāryasya te nūnam prātiveśyaḥ mahādhanaḥ yuvā su lalitaḥ kāmī tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ su durvinītaḥ putraḥ vā jāmātā vā pramārjakaḥ dārāḥ vā pratikūlāḥ te tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ bhrātaraḥ atīva viṣamāḥ pitā vā kṣudh kṣataḥ mṛtaḥ mātā jyeṣṭhaḥ guruḥ vā api tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ vā hataḥ gauḥ vā brahma svam vā hṛtam purā deva svam vā adhikam kāle tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ hṛta dāraḥ atha vṛddhaḥ vā loke dviṣṭaḥ atha vā naraiḥ a vijñātena vā vṛddhaḥ tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ vārdhakya artham dhanam dṛṣṭvā svā śrīḥ vā api paraiḥ hṛtā vṛttiḥ vā durjana apekṣā tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ saṃpad kāle na te dharmaḥ kṣīṇaḥ tāta suhṛd bruvaiḥ a saṃnyāsam atas tatra tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ parokṣa vādibhiḥ mithyā doṣaḥ te saṃpradarśitaḥ alam arthayase hīnān tena asi hariṇaḥ kṛśaḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ śreyaḥ arthinā daridreṇa kim kartavyam pitāmaha dānānām uttamam yat ca deyam yat ca yathā yathā vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ mānyān pūjyān ca gāṅgeya rahasyam vaktum arhasi evam pṛṣṭaḥ narendreṇa pāṇḍavena yaśasvinā bhīṣmaḥ dharmāṇām paramam guhyam bhīṣmaḥ provāca pārthivam śṛṇuṣva avahitaḥ rājan dharma guhyāni bhārata yathā hi bhagavān vyāsaḥ purā kathitavān mayi deva guhyam idam rājan yamena akliṣṭa karmaṇā ṛṣayaḥ pramathāḥ śrīḥ ca citraguptaḥ diśām gajāḥ ṛṣi dharmaḥ smṛtaḥ yatra sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ mahā dāna phalam ca eva sarva yajña phalam tathā yaḥ ca etat evam jānīyāt jñātvā vā kurute anagha sa doṣaḥ doṣavān ca iha taiḥ guṇaiḥ saha yujyate daśa sūnā samam cakram daśa cakra samaḥ dhvajaḥ daśa dhvaja samāḥ veśyāḥ daśa veśyā samaḥ nṛpaḥ ardhena etāni sarvāṇi nṛpatiḥ kathyate adhikaḥ trivarga sahitam śāstram pavitram puṇya lakṣaṇam dharma vyākaraṇam puṇyam rahasya śravaṇam mahat śrotavyam dharma saṃyuktam vihitam tridaśaiḥ svayam pitṝṇām yatra guhyāni procyante śrāddha karmaṇi devatānām ca sarveṣām rahasyam kathyate akhilam ṛṣi dharmaḥ smṛtaḥ yatra sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ mahāyajña phalam ca eva sarva dāna phalam tathā ye paṭhanti sadā martyāḥ yeṣām ca eva upatiṣṭhati śrutvā ca phalam ācaṣṭe svayam nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ gavām phalam tīrtha phalam yajñānām ca eva yat phalam śrotāraḥ śraddadhānāḥ ca yeṣām śuddham ca mānasam teṣām vyaktam jitāḥ lokāḥ śraddadhānena sādhunā yau tau kāma guṇa upetau aśvinau bhiṣajām varau ājñayā aham tayoḥ prāptaḥ sa narān pitṛ devatān kimartham ca trayaḥ piṇḍāḥ pravibhaktāḥ pṛthak pṛthak śraddadhānena dūtena bhāṣitam dharma saṃhitam pūrva sthāḥ tridaśāḥ sarve pitaraḥ pūjya khecaram svāgatam te astu bhadram te śrūyatām khecara uttama gūḍha arthaḥ paramaḥ praśnaḥ bhavatā samudīritaḥ śrāddham dattvā ca bhuktvā ca puruṣaḥ yaḥ striyam vrajet pravibhāgam tu piṇḍānām pravakṣyāmi anupūrvaśas piṇḍaḥ hi adhastāt gacchan tu apaḥ āviśya bhāvayet piṇḍam tu madhyamam tatra patnī tu ekā samaśnute piṇḍaḥ tṛtīyaḥ yaḥ teṣām tam dadyāt jātavedasi pitaraḥ tasya tuṣyanti prahṛṣṭa manasaḥ sadā deva dūtaḥ prajāḥ vivardhate ca asya akṣayam ca upatiṣṭhati ānupūrvyeṇa piṇḍānām pravibhāgaḥ pṛthak pṛthak pitṝṇām triṣu sarveṣām niruktam kathitam tvayā kam vā prīṇayate devam katham tārayate pitṝn madhyamam tu tadā patnī bhuṅkte anujñātam eva hi etat icchāmi aham śrotum piṇḍeṣu triṣu yā gatiḥ phalam vṛttim ca mārgam ca yaḥ ca enam pratipadyate su mahān eṣa praśnaḥ vai yaḥ tvayā samudīritaḥ rahasyam adbhutam ca api pṛṣṭāḥ sma gaganecara te api evam na abhijānanti pitṛ kārya viniścayam trayāṇām api piṇḍānām śrutvā bhagavataḥ gatim deva dūtena yaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ śrāddhasya vidhi niścayaḥ gatiḥ trayāṇām piṇḍānām śṛṇuṣva avahitaḥ mama apaḥ gacchati yaḥ hi atra śaśinam hi eṣa prīṇayet śaśī prīṇayate devān pitṝn ca eva mahāmate bhuṅkte tu patnī yam ca eṣām anujñātā tu madhyamam putra kāmāya putram tu prayacchanti pitāmahāḥ pitaraḥ tena tṛpyanti prītāḥ kāmān diśanti ca etat te kathitam sarvam triṣu piṇḍeṣu yā gatiḥ śucinā tu sadā śrāddham bhoktavyam khecara uttama ye mayā kathitāḥ doṣāḥ te tathā syuḥ na ca anyathā tasmāt snātaḥ śuciḥ kṣāntaḥ śrāddham bhuñjīta vai dvijaḥ tatas vidyutprabhaḥ nāma ṛṣiḥ āha mahā tapāḥ āditya tejasā tasya tulyam rūpam prakāśate kīṭān pipīlikān sarpān meṣān sa mṛga pakṣiṇaḥ kilbiṣam su bahu prāptāḥ kim svid eṣām pratikriyā tatas deva gaṇāḥ sarve ṛṣayaḥ ca tapodhanāḥ śakraḥ pitaraḥ ca mahābhāgāḥ pūjayanti sma tam munim kurukṣetram gayām gaṅgām prabhāsam puṣkarāṇi ca tathā mucyati pāpena rāhuṇā candramāḥ yathā tri aham snātaḥ sa bhavati nirāhāraḥ ca vartate spṛśate yaḥ gavām pṛṣṭham vāladhim ca namasyati ghṛṣṭaḥ vaṭa kaṣāyeṇa anuliptaḥ priyaṅguṇā kṣīreṇa ṣaṣṭikān bhuktvā sarva pāpaiḥ pramucyate śrūyatām ca aparam guhyam rahasyam ṛṣi cintitam śrutam me bhāṣamāṇasya sthāṇoḥ sthāne bṛhaspateḥ parvata ārohaṇam kṛtvā ekapādaḥ vibhāvasum nirīkṣeta nirāhāraḥ ūrdhva bāhuḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ grīṣma kāle atha vā śīte evam pāpam apohati tatas pāpāt pramuktasya dyutiḥ bhavati śāśvatī dharma guhyam tu bhagavān mānuṣāṇām sukha āvaham bṛhaspatiḥ sa rahasyāḥ ca ye doṣāḥ tān yathāvat udīraya bāla vatsām ca ye dhenum duhanti kṣīra kāraṇāt teṣām doṣān pravakṣyāmi tān nibodha śacīpate bhānumān anilaḥ ca eva havyavāhaḥ ca vāsava lokānām mātaraḥ ca eva gāvaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayaṃbhuvā lokān tārayitum śaktāḥ martyeṣu eteṣu devatāḥ varṣāṇi ṣaḍaśītim tu durvṛttāḥ kula pāṃsanāḥ striyaḥ sarvāḥ ca durvṛttāḥ pratimehanti yāḥ ravim anila dveṣiṇaḥ śakra garbha sthā cyavate prajā agni kāryeṣu vai teṣām havyam na aśnāti pāvakaḥ kṣīram tu bāla vatsānām ye pibanti iha mānavāḥ na teṣām kṣīrapāḥ ke cit jāyante kula vardhanāḥ prajā kṣayeṇa yujyante kula vaṃśa kṣayeṇa ca evam etat purā dṛṣṭam kula vṛddhaiḥ dvijātibhiḥ tasmāt varjyāni varjyāni kāryam kāryam ca nityaśas tatas sarve mahābhāgāḥ devatāḥ sa marut gaṇāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ca mahābhāgāḥ pṛcchanti sma pitṝn tatas ānṛṇyam vā katham martyāḥ gaccheyuḥ kena karmaṇā etat icchāmahe śrotum param kautūhalam hi naḥ nyāyataḥ vai mahābhāgāḥ saṃśayaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ śrūyatām yena tuṣyāmaḥ martyānām sādhu karmaṇām nīla ṣaṇḍa pramokṣeṇa amāvāsyām tila udakaiḥ varṣāsu dīpakaiḥ ca eva pitṝṇām anṛṇaḥ bhavet akṣayam nirvyalīkam ca dānam etat mahā phalam asmākam paritoṣaḥ ca akṣayaḥ parikīrtyate śraddadhānāḥ ca ye martyāḥ āhariṣyanti saṃtatim durgāt te tārayiṣyanti narakāt prapitāmahān pitṝṇām bhāṣitam śrutvā hṛṣṭa romā tapodhanaḥ vṛddhagārgyaḥ mahā tejāḥ tān evam vākyam abravīt ke guṇāḥ nīla ṣaṇḍasya pramuktasya tapodhanāḥ nīlaṣaṇḍasya lāṅgūlam toyam abhyuddharet yadi ṣaṣṭim varṣa sahasrāṇi pitaraḥ tena tarpitāḥ yaḥ tu śṛṅga gatam paṅkam kūlāt uddhṛtya tiṣṭhati varṣāsu dīpa dānena śaśi vat śobhate naraḥ tamaḥ rūpam na tasya asti dīpakam yaḥ prayacchati kṛtam bhavati taiḥ śrāddham sa rahasyam yathārthavat hṛṣṭa puṣṭa manāḥ teṣām prajā bhavati nityadā śraddadhānaḥ tu yaḥ kuryāt pitṝṇām anṛṇaḥ bhavet evam eṣa samuddiṣṭaḥ śrāddha kāla kramaḥ tathā bhīṣmaḥ iti pṛṣṭaḥ surendreṇa provāca hariḥ avyayaḥ brāhmaṇānām parīvādaḥ mama vidveṣaṇam mahat brāhmaṇaiḥ pūjitaiḥ nityam pūjitaḥ aham na saṃśayaḥ nitya abhivādyāḥ vipra indrāḥ bhuktvā pādau tathā ātmanaḥ teṣām tuṣyāmi martyānām yaḥ cakre ca balim haret vāmanam brāhmaṇam dṛṣṭvā varāham ca jala utthitam uddhṛtām dharaṇīm ca eva mūrdhnā dhārayate tu yaḥ na teṣām aśubham kim cit kalmaṣam ca upapadyate aśvattham rocanām gām ca pūjayet yaḥ naraḥ sadā tena rūpeṇa teṣām ca pūjām gṛhṇāmi tattvataḥ pūjā mama eṣā na asti anyā yāvat lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ anyathā hi vṛthā martyāḥ pūjayanti alpabuddhayaḥ indraḥ na aham tat pratigṛhṇāmi na sā tuṣṭi karī mama cakram pādau varāham ca brāhmaṇam ca api vāmanam uddhṛtām dharaṇīm ca eva kimartham tvam praśaṃsasi bhavān sṛjati bhūtāni bhavān saṃharati prajāḥ bhīṣmaḥ prakṛtiḥ sarva bhūtānām sa martyānām sanātanī cakreṇa nihatāḥ daityāḥ padbhyām krāntā vasuṃdharā vārāham rūpam āsthāya uddhṛtā ca vasuṃdharā vāmanam rūpam āsthāya jitaḥ rājā mayā baliḥ parituṣṭaḥ bhavāmi evam mānuṣāṇām mahātmanām tat mām ye pūjayiṣyanti na asti teṣām parābhavaḥ api vā brāhmaṇam dṛṣṭvā brahmacāriṇam āgatam brāhmaṇāya āhutim dattvā amṛtam tasya bhojanam aindrīm saṃdhyām upāsitvā āditya abhimukhaḥ sthitaḥ saṃśayam pṛcchamānānām kim bhūyas kathayāmi aham śrūyatām paramam guhyam mānuṣāṇām sukha āvaham a jānantaḥ yat abudhāḥ kliśyante bhūta pīḍitāḥ kalye utthāya yaḥ martyaḥ spṛśet gām vai ghṛtam dadhi bhūtāni ca eva sarvāṇi agratas pṛṣṭhatas api vā ucchiṣṭam vā api chidreṣu varjayanti tapodhanāḥ devāḥ pragṛhya audumbaram pātram toya pūrṇam udak mukhaḥ upavāsam tu gṛhṇīyāt yat vā saṃkalpayet vratam upavāse balau ca api tāmra pātram viśiṣyate baliḥ bhikṣā tathā arghyam ca pitṝṇām ca tila udakam guhyam etat samuddiṣṭam yathā tuṣyanti devatāḥ rāja pauruṣike vipre ghāṇṭike paricārake gorakṣake vāṇijake tathā kāru kuśīlave mitra druhi an adhīyāne yaḥ ca syāt vṛṣalī patiḥ eteṣu daivam pitryam vā na deyam syāt katham cana piṇḍa dāḥ tasya hīyante na ca prīṇāti vai pitṝn agniḥ pādam udyamya yaḥ martyaḥ spṛśet gāḥ ca su durmatiḥ brāhmaṇam vā mahābhāgam dīpyamānam tathā analam tasya doṣān pravakṣyāmi tat śṛṇudhvam samāhitāḥ vaimanasyam ca devānām kṛtam bhavati puṣkalam pāvakaḥ ca mahā tejāḥ havyam na pratigṛhṇati ājanmanām śatam ca eva narake pacyate tu saḥ tasmāt gāvaḥ na pādena spraṣṭavyāḥ vai kadā cana brāhmaṇaḥ ca mahā tejāḥ dīpyamānaḥ tathā analaḥ viśvāmitraḥ ete doṣāḥ mayā proktāḥ triṣu yaḥ pādam utsṛjet śrūyatām paramam guhyam rahasyam dharma saṃhitam paramānnena yaḥ dadyāt pitṝṇām aupahārikam gaja chāyāyām pūrvasyām kutupe dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ śrūyatām tasya dānasya yādṛśaḥ guṇa vistaraḥ kṛtam tena mahat śrāddham varṣāṇi iha trayodaśa gāvaḥ bhūyas ca yā viṣṇu pade sthitā yā vibhāvasoḥ ca api pathe sthitā yā vimucyate pāpa kṛtena karmaṇā etam hi mantram tridaśa abhijuṣṭam na tasya pāpam na bhayam na śokaḥ bhīṣmaḥ atha sapta mahābhāgāḥ ṛṣayaḥ loka viśrutāḥ vasiṣṭha pramukhāḥ sarve brahmāṇam padmasaṃbhavam pradakṣiṇam abhikramya sarve prāñjalayaḥ sthitāḥ uvāca vacanam teṣām vasiṣṭhaḥ brahma vittamaḥ sarva prāṇi hitam praśnam brahmakṣetre viśeṣataḥ dravya hīnāḥ katham martyāḥ daridrāḥ sādhu vṛttayaḥ prāpnuvanti iha yajñasya phalam kena iha karmaṇā aho praśnaḥ mahābhāgāḥ gūḍha arthaḥ paramaḥ śubhaḥ sūkṣmaḥ śreyān ca martyānām bhavadbhiḥ samudāhṛtaḥ śrūyatām sarvam ākhyāsye nikhilena tapodhanāḥ yathā yajña phalam martyaḥ labhate na atra saṃśayaḥ pauṣa māsasya śukle vai yadā yujyeta rohiṇī eka vastraḥ śuciḥ snātaḥ śraddadhānaḥ samāhitaḥ somasya raśmayaḥ pītvā mahāyajña phalam labhet vibhāvasuḥ yat mām bhavantaḥ pṛcchanti sūkṣma tattva artha darśinaḥ salilasya añjalim pūrṇam akṣatāḥ ca ghṛta uttarāḥ somasya uttiṣṭhamānasya tat jalam ca akṣatān ca tān agni kāryam kṛtam tena hutāḥ ca asya agnayaḥ trayaḥ vanaspatim ca yaḥ hanyāt amāvāsyām abuddhimān dantakāṣṭham tu yaḥ khādet amāvāsyām abuddhimān śrīḥ prakīrṇam bhājanam yatra pāna bhāṇḍam atha āsanam devatāḥ pitaraḥ ca eva utsave parvaṇīṣu vā nirāśāḥ pratigacchanti kaśmala upahatāt gṛhāt aṅgirāḥ yaḥ tu saṃvatsaram pūrṇam dadyāt dīpam karañjake gārgyaḥ ātithyam satatam kuryāt dīpam dadyāt pratiśraye varjayānaḥ divāsvapnam na ca māṃsāni bhakṣayet go brāhmaṇam na hiṃsyāt ca puṣkarāṇi ca kīrtayet eṣa śreṣṭhatamaḥ dharmaḥ sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ api kratu śataiḥ iṣṭvā kṣayam gacchati tat haviḥ na tu kṣīyanti te dharmāḥ śraddadhānaiḥ prayojitāḥ rajasvalā ca yā nārī śvitrikā putrikā ca yā etābhiḥ cakṣuṣā dṛṣṭam haviḥ na aśnanti devatāḥ pitaraḥ ca na tuṣyanti varṣāṇi asya trayodaśa śukla vāsāḥ śuciḥ bhūtvā brāhmaṇān svasti vācayet kīrtayet bhāratam ca eva tathā syāt akṣayam haviḥ bhinna bhāṇḍam ca khaṭvām ca kukkuṭam śunakam tathā a praśastāni sarvāṇi yaḥ ca vṛkṣaḥ gṛheruhaḥ bhinna bhāṇḍe kalim prāhuḥ khaṭvāyām tu dhana kṣayaḥ kukkuṭe śunake ca eva haviḥ na aśnanti devatāḥ vṛkṣa mūle dhruvam sattvam tasmāt vṛkṣam na ropayet avākśirāḥ vā lambeta satram vā sphītam āharet na ca asya hṛdayam śuddham narakam sa dhruvam vrajet tulyam yajñaḥ ca satyam ca hṛdayasya ca śuddha tā vāyuḥ kim cit dharmam pravakṣyāmi mānuṣāṇām sukha āvaham sa rahasyāḥ ca ye doṣāḥ tān śṛṇudhvam samāhitāḥ dīpakaḥ ca api kartavyaḥ pitṝṇām sa tila udakaḥ etena vidhinā martyaḥ śraddadhānaḥ samāhitaḥ caturaḥ vārṣikān māsān yaḥ dadāti tila udakam bhojanam ca yathāśaktyā brāhmaṇe vedapārage agneḥ tu vṛṣalaḥ netā haviḥ mūḍhāḥ ca yoṣitaḥ gavām mūtra purīṣeṇa payasā ca ghṛtena ca agnikāryam tri aham kuryāt nirāhāraḥ samāhitaḥ tatas saṃvatsare pūrṇe pratigṛhṇanti devatāḥ hṛṣyanti pitaraḥ ca asya śrāddha kāle upasthite lomaśaḥ martyānām svarga kāmānām pretya svarga sukha āvahaḥ paradāreṣu ye saktāḥ a kṛtvā dāra saṃgraham nirāśāḥ pitaraḥ teṣām śrāddha kāle bhavanti vai para dāra ratiḥ yaḥ syāt yaḥ ca vandhyām upāsate a saṃbhāṣyāḥ bhavanti ete pitṝṇām na atra saṃśayaḥ devatāḥ pitaraḥ ca eṣām na abhinandanti tat haviḥ tasmāt parasya vai dārān tyajet vandhyām ca yoṣitam śrūyatām ca aparam guhyam rahasyam dharma saṃhitam śraddadhānena kartavyam gurūṇām vacanam sadā dvādaśyām paurṇamāsyām ca māsi māsi ghṛta akṣatam aśvamedha catur bhāgam phalam sṛjati vāsavaḥ dānena etena tejasvī vīryavān ca bhavet naraḥ prītaḥ ca bhagavān somaḥ iṣṭān kāmān prayacchati śrūyatām ca aparaḥ dharmaḥ sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ imam kali yugam prāpya manuṣyāṇām sukha āvahaḥ kalyam utthāya yaḥ martyaḥ snātaḥ śuklena vāsasā tila pātram prayaccheta brāhmaṇebhyaḥ samāhitaḥ tila udakam ca yaḥ dadyāt pitṝṇām madhunā saha dīpakam kṛsaram ca eva śrūyatām tasya tat phalam tila pātre phalam prāha bhagavān pākaśāsanaḥ go pradānam ca yaḥ kuryāt bhūmi dānam ca śāśvatam agniṣṭomam ca yaḥ yajñam yajeta bahu dakṣiṇam dīpe ca kṛsare ca eva tuṣyante asya pitāmahāḥ evam etat mayā uddiṣṭam ṛṣi dṛṣṭam purātanam bhīṣmaḥ tatas tu ṛṣi gaṇāḥ sarve pitaraḥ ca sa devatāḥ arundhatīm tapaḥ vṛddhām apṛcchanta samāhitāḥ samāna śīlām vīryeṇa vasiṣṭhasya mahātmanaḥ tapaḥ vṛddhiḥ mayā prāptā bhavatām smaraṇena vai bhavatām ca prasādena dharmān vakṣyāmi śāśvatān sa guhyān sa rahasyān ca tān śṛṇudhvam aśeṣatas śraddadhāne prayoktavyāḥ yasya śuddham tathā manaḥ aśraddadhānaḥ mānī ca brahma hā guru talpa gaḥ a saṃbhāṣyāḥ hi catvāraḥ na eṣām dharmam prakāśayet ahani ahani yaḥ dadyāt kapilām dvādaśīḥ samāḥ gavām śata sahasram ca yaḥ dadyāt jyeṣṭhapuṣkare śrūyatām ca aparaḥ dharmaḥ manuṣyāṇām sukha āvahaḥ niṣiñceta gavām śṛṅge mastakena ca tat jalam pratīccheta nirāhāraḥ tasya dharma phalam śṛṇu śrūyante yāni tīrthāni triṣu lokeṣu kāni cit siddha cāraṇa juṣṭāni sevitāni mahā ṛṣibhiḥ abhiṣekaḥ samaḥ teṣām gavām śṛṅga udakasya ca sādhu sādhu iti ca uddiṣṭam daivataiḥ pitṛbhiḥ tathā pitāmahaḥ bhūtaiḥ ca eva su saṃhṛṣṭaiḥ pūjitā sā api arundhatī yamaḥ ramaṇīyā kathā divyā yuṣmattaḥ yā mayā śrutā śrūyatām citraguptasya bhāṣitam mama ca priyam rahasyam dharma saṃyuktam śakyam śrotum mahā ṛṣibhiḥ na hi puṇyam tathā pāpam kṛtam kim cit vinaśyati parva kāle ca yat kim cit ādityam ca adhitiṣṭhati pānīyam ca eva dīpam ca dātavyam satatam tathā puṣkare kapilā deyā brāhmaṇe vedapārage ayam ca eva aparaḥ dharmaḥ citraguptena bhāṣitaḥ tatra durgam anuprāptāḥ kṣudh tṛṣṇā paripīḍitāḥ pānīyasya guṇāḥ divyāḥ preta loke viśeṣataḥ tatra puṇyodakā nāma nadī teṣām vidhīyate akṣayam salilam tatra śītalam hi amṛta upamam pradīpasya pradānena śrūyatām guṇa vistaraḥ prabhām ca asya prayacchanti soma bhāskara pāvakāḥ devatāḥ ca asya manyante vimalāḥ sarvatodiśaḥ tasmāt dīpaḥ pradātavyaḥ pānīyam ca viśeṣataḥ puṣkare ca viśeṣeṇa śrūyatām tasya yat phalam go śatam sa vṛṣam tena dattam bhavati śāśvatam śodhayet kapilā hi ekā pradattam go śatam yathā tasmāt tu kapilā deyā kaumudyām jyeṣṭhapuṣkare na teṣām viṣamam kim cit na duḥkham na ca kaṇṭakāḥ chatra dāne sukhām chāyām labhate para loka gaḥ citragupta matam śrutvā hṛṣṭa romā vibhāvasuḥ uvāca devatāḥ sarvāḥ pitṝn ca eva mahā dyutiḥ śraddadhānāḥ ca ye martyāḥ brāhmaṇeṣu mahātmasu dharma doṣāḥ tu ime pañca yeṣām na asti iha niṣkṛtiḥ a saṃbhāṣyāḥ anācārāḥ varjanīyāḥ nara adhamāḥ aśraddadhānaḥ ca naraḥ striyam yaḥ ca upajīvati pacyante vai yathā mīnāḥ pūya śoṇita bhojanāḥ a saṃbhāṣyāḥ pitṝṇām ca devānām ca eva pañca te bhīṣmaḥ snātakānām ca viprāṇām ye ca anye ca tapodhanāḥ tatas sarve mahābhāgāḥ devāḥ ca pitaraḥ ca ha ṛṣayaḥ ca mahābhāgāḥ pramathān vākyam abruvan bhavantaḥ vai mahābhāgāḥ aparokṣa niśācarāḥ ucchiṣṭān aśucīn kṣudrān katham hiṃsatha mānavān ke ca smṛtāḥ pratīghātāḥ yena martyān na hiṃsatha rakṣaḥ ghnāni ca kāni syuḥ yaiḥ gṛheṣu praṇaśyatha pramathāḥ śrotum icchāma yuṣmākam sarvam etat niśācarāḥ mohāt māṃsāni khādetha vṛkṣa mūle ca yaḥ svapet āmiṣam śīrṣataḥ yasya pādataḥ yaḥ ca saṃviśet tatas ucchiṣṭakāḥ sarve bahu chidrāḥ ca mānavāḥ udake ca api amedhyāni śleṣmāṇam ca pramuñcati ete bhakṣyāḥ ca vadhyāḥ ca mānuṣāḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ evaṃśīla samācārān dharṣayāmaḥ hi mānavān śrūyatām ca pratīghātān yaiḥ na śaknuma hiṃsitum ghṛta akṣatam ca yaḥ dadyāt mastake tad parāyaṇaḥ ye ca māṃsam na khādanti tān na śaknuma hiṃsitum yasya ca agniḥ gṛhe nityam divā rātrau ca dīpyate tarakṣoḥ carma daṃṣṭrāḥ ca tathā eva giri kacchapaḥ ājya dhūmaḥ biḍālaḥ ca chāgaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ atha piṅgalaḥ yeṣām etāni tiṣṭhanti gṛheṣu gṛhamedhinām tāni adhṛṣyāṇi agārāṇi piśitāśaiḥ su dāruṇaiḥ tasmāt etāni geheṣu rakṣaḥ ghnāni viśām pate bhīṣmaḥ etat vaḥ kathitam sarvam yatra vaḥ saṃśayaḥ mahān tatas padma pratīkāśaḥ padma udbhūtaḥ pitāmahaḥ uvāca vacanam devān vāsavam ca śacīpatim ayam mahā balaḥ nāgaḥ rasātala caraḥ balī tejasvī reṇukaḥ nāma mahā sattva parākramaḥ ati tejasvinaḥ sarve mahā vīryāḥ mahā gajāḥ dhārayanti mahīm kṛtsnām sa śaila vana kānanām bhavadbhiḥ samanujñātaḥ reṇukaḥ tān mahā gajān pitāmaha vacaḥ śrutvā te devāḥ reṇukam tadā reṇukaḥ preṣayām āsuḥ avyagrāḥ yatra te dharaṇīdharāḥ anujñātaḥ asmi devaiḥ ca pitṛbhiḥ ca mahā balāḥ dharma guhyāni yuṣmākam śrotum icchāmi tattvataḥ diggajāḥ kathayadhvam mahābhāgāḥ yat vaḥ tattvam manīṣitam kārttike māsi ca āśleṣā bahulasya aṣṭamī śivā imam mantram japan śrāddhe yata āhāraḥ hi akopanaḥ anantāḥ hi akṣayāḥ nityam bhoginaḥ su mahā balāḥ teṣām kula udbhavāḥ ye ca mahā bhūtāḥ bhujaṃgamāḥ yadā nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān ujjahāra vasuṃdharām gaja indra kusuma ākīrṇam nīla vastra anulepanam evam tuṣṭāḥ tatas sarve adhastāt bhāra pīḍitā śramam tam na avabudhyāmaḥ dhārayantaḥ vasuṃdharām evam manyāmahe dharmam bhāra ārtāḥ nirapekṣiṇaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyaḥ vaiśyaḥ śūdraḥ vā yadi upoṣitaḥ evam saṃvatsaram kṛtvā dānam bahu phalam labhet ye ca nāgāḥ mahā vīryāḥ triṣu lokeṣu kṛtsnaśas kṛta ātithyāḥ bhaveyuḥ te śatam varṣāṇi tattvataḥ diggajānām ca tat śrutvā devatāḥ pitaraḥ tathā maheśvaraḥ ṛṣayaḥ ca mahābhāgāḥ pūjayanti sma reṇukam sāram uddhṛtya yuṣmābhiḥ sādhu dharmaḥ udāhṛtaḥ yeṣām dharma āśritā buddhiḥ śraddha dhānāḥ ca ye narāḥ teṣām syāt upadeṣṭavyaḥ sa rahasyaḥ mahā phalaḥ eka bhaktam tathā aśnīyāt śrūyatām tasya yat phalam imāḥ gāvaḥ mahābhāgāḥ pavitram paramam smṛtāḥ trīn lokān dhārayanti sma sa deva asura mānuṣān tāsu ca eva mahā puṇyam śuśrūṣā ca mahā phalam mayā hi etāḥ hi anujñātāḥ pūrvam āsan kṛte yuge tasmāt vraja sthāna gataḥ tiṣṭhati upari me vṛṣaḥ rame aham saha gobhiḥ ca tasmāt pūjyāḥ sadā eva tāḥ mahā prabhāvāḥ vara dāḥ varān dadyuḥ upāsitāḥ mama api anumataḥ dharmaḥ tam śṛṇudhvam samāhitāḥ nīlaṣaṇḍasya śṛṅgābhyām gṛhītvā mṛttikām tu yaḥ pragṛhya audumbaram pātram pakva annam madhunā saha somasya uttiṣṭhamānasya paurṇamāsyām balim haret sādhyāḥ rudrāḥ tathā ādityāḥ viśvedevāḥ tathā aśvinau viṣṇuḥ eṣa dharmaḥ mayā uddiṣṭaḥ sa rahasyaḥ sukha āvahaḥ dharma guhyāni sarvāṇi devatānām mahātmanām ṛṣīṇām ca eva guhyāni yaḥ paṭhet āhnikam sadā śṛṇuyāt vā anasūyuḥ yaḥ śraddadhānaḥ samāhitaḥ ye ca dharmāḥ śubhāḥ puṇyāḥ sa rahasyāḥ udāhṛtāḥ teṣām dharma phalam tasya yaḥ paṭheta jita indriyaḥ paṭhet vā śrāvayet vā api śrutvā vā labhate phalam śrāvayan ca api vipra indrān parvasu prayataḥ naraḥ ṛṣīṇām devatānām ca pitṝṇām ca eva nityadā bhavati abhimataḥ śrīmān dharmeṣu prayataḥ sadā kṛtvā api pāpakam karma mahāpātaka varjitam bhīṣmaḥ vyāsa uddiṣṭam mayā proktam sarva deva namaskṛtam pṛthivī ratna saṃpūrṇā jñānam ca idam anuttamam na aśraddadhānāya na nāstikāya na naṣṭa dharmāya na nirguṇāya na hetu duṣṭāya guru dviṣe vā na an ātma bhūtāya nivedyam etat yudhiṣṭhiraḥ bhīṣmaḥ tathā vaiśyasya ke bhojyāḥ ke ca śūdrasya bhārata brāhmaṇāḥ brāhmaṇasya iha bhojyāḥ ye ca eva kṣatriyāḥ vaiśyāḥ ca api tathā bhojyāḥ śūdrāḥ ca parivarjitāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāḥ vaiśyāḥ bhojyāḥ vai kṣatriyasya ha varjanīyāḥ tu vai śūdrāḥ sarva bhakṣyāḥ vikarmiṇaḥ vaiśyāḥ tu bhojyāḥ viprāṇām kṣatriyāṇām tathā eva ca nitya agnayaḥ viviktāḥ ca cāturmāsya ratāḥ ca ye śūdrāṇām atha yaḥ bhuṅkte sa bhuṅkte pṛthivī malam malam nṛṇām sa pibati malam bhuṅkte janasya ca śūdrāṇām yaḥ tathā bhuṅkte sa bhuṅkte pṛthivī malam pṛthivī malam aśnanti ye dvijāḥ śūdra bhojinaḥ śūdrasya karma niṣṭhāyām vikarma sthaḥ api pacyate svādhyāya niratāḥ viprāḥ tathā svastyayane nṛṇām rakṣaṇe kṣatriyam prāhuḥ vaiśyam puṣṭi artham eva ca kṛṣi gorakṣya vāṇijyam akutsā vaiśya karmaṇi sa vijñeyaḥ yathā śūdraḥ na ca bhojyaḥ kadā cana cikitsakaḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaḥ purādhyakṣaḥ purohitaḥ sāṃvatsaraḥ vṛthādhyāyī sarve te śūdra saṃmitāḥ puṃścalī annam ca mūtram syāt kārukā annam ca śoṇitam vacanīyasya yaḥ bhuṅkte tam āhuḥ śoṇitam hradam piśunam bhojanam bhuṅkte brahmahatyā samam viduḥ asatkṛtam avajñātam na bhoktavyam kadā cana vyādhim kula kṣayam ca eva kṣipram prāpnoti brāhmaṇaḥ nagarī rakṣiṇaḥ bhuṅkte śvapaca pravaṇaḥ bhavet bhuktvā annam jāyate vipraḥ rakṣasām kula vardhanaḥ nyāsa apahāriṇaḥ bhuktvā kṛtaghne klība vartini jāyate śabara āvāse madhyadeśa bahiṣkṛte abhojyāḥ ca eva bhojyāḥ ca mayā proktāḥ yathāvidhi yudhiṣṭhiraḥ uktāḥ tu bhavatā bhojyāḥ tathā abhojyāḥ ca sarvaśas brāhmaṇānām viśeṣeṇa havya kavya pratigrahe bhīṣmaḥ nānāvidheṣu bhojyeṣu prāyaścittāni śaṃsa me hanta vakṣyāmi te rājan brāhmaṇānām mahātmanām āditya udayanam sthitvā pūtaḥ bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ kāñcanam pratigṛhya atha japamānaḥ guru śrutim kṛṣṇāyasam ca trivṛtam dhārayan mucyate dvijaḥ yāvake dadhi dugdhe ca sāvitrīm śataśas anvitām trīṇi rātrāṇi upoṣitvā tena pāpmā vimucyate kṛṣṇa pakṣe tu yaḥ śrāddham pitṝṇām aśnute dvijaḥ annam etat ahar rātrāt pūtaḥ bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ na saṃkiret tat annam ca tathā pūyeta brāhmaṇaḥ ityartham aparāhṇe tu pitṝṇām śrāddham ucyate yathā uktānām yat aśnīyuḥ brāhmaṇāḥ pūrva ketitāḥ mṛtakasya tṛtīya ahe brāhmaṇaḥ yaḥ annam aśnute dvādaśa ahe vyatīte tu kṛta śaucaḥ viśeṣataḥ mṛtasya daśa rātreṇa prāyaścittāni dāpayet sāvitrīm raivatīm iṣṭim kūṣmāṇḍam aghamarṣaṇam mṛtakasya tri rātre yaḥ samuddiṣṭe samaśnute sapta triṣavaṇam snātvā pūtaḥ bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ siddhim āpnoti vipulām āpadam ca eva na āpnuyāt yaḥ tu śūdraiḥ saha aśnīyāt brāhmaṇaḥ api eka bhojane aśaucam vidhivat tasya śauca mātram vidhīyate yaḥ tu vaiśyaiḥ saha aśnīyāt brāhmaṇaḥ api eka bhojane sa vai tri rātram dīkṣitvā mucyate tena karmaṇā kṣatriyaiḥ saha yaḥ aśnīyāt brāhmaṇaḥ api eka bhojane śūdrasya tu kulam hanti vaiśyasya paśu bāndhavān prāyaścittam ca śāntim ca juhuyāt tena mucyate sāvitrīm raivatīm iṣṭim kūṣmāṇḍam aghamarṣaṇam atha ucchiṣṭam atha anyonyam saṃprāśet na atra saṃśayaḥ rocanā virajāḥ rātriḥ maṅgala ālambhanāni ca yudhiṣṭhiraḥ kim svid pṛthivyām hi etat me bhavān śaṃsitum arhati bhīṣmaḥ śṛṇu yaiḥ dharma nirataiḥ tapasā bhāvitātmabhiḥ lokāḥ hi asaṃśayam prāptāḥ dāna puṇya rataiḥ nṛpaiḥ satkṛtaḥ ca tathā ātreyaḥ śiṣyebhyaḥ brahma nirguṇam śibiḥ auśīnaraḥ prāṇān priyasya tanayasya ca brāhmaṇa artham upākṛtya nāka pṛṣṭham itas gataḥ pratardanaḥ kāśi patiḥ pradāya tanayam svakam brāhmaṇāya atulām kīrtim iha ca amutra ca aśnute rantidevaḥ ca sāṃkṛtyaḥ vasiṣṭhāya mahātmane divyam śata śalākam ca yajña artham kāñcanam śubham chatram devāvṛdhaḥ dattvā brāhmaṇāya āsthitaḥ divam bhagavān ambarīṣaḥ ca brāhmaṇāya amita ojase pradāya sakalam rāṣṭram sura lokam avāptavān sāvitraḥ kuṇḍalam divyam yānam ca janamejayaḥ brāhmaṇāya ca gāḥ dattvā gataḥ lokān anuttamān vṛṣādarbhiḥ ca rājarṣiḥ ratnāni vividhāni ca ramyān ca āvasathān dattvā dvijebhyaḥ divam āgamat nimiḥ rāṣṭram ca vaidarbhiḥ kanyām dattvā mahātmane jāmadagnyaḥ ca viprāya bhūmim dattvā mahā yaśāḥ rāmaḥ akṣayān tathā lokān jagāma manasaḥ adhikān vasiṣṭhaḥ jīvayām āsa yena yātaḥ akṣayām gatim rāmaḥ dāśarathiḥ ca eva hutvā yajñeṣu vai vasu kakṣasenaḥ ca rājarṣiḥ vasiṣṭhāya mahātmane nyāsam yathāvat saṃnyasya jagāma su mahā yaśāḥ kanyām aṅgirase dattvā divam āśu jagāma saḥ brahmadattaḥ ca pāñcālyaḥ rājā dharma bhṛtām varaḥ nidhim śaṅkham anujñāpya jagāma paramām gatim rājā mitrasahaḥ ca eva vasiṣṭhāya mahātmane madayantīm priyām bhāryām dattvā ca tridivam gataḥ daṇḍam uddhṛtya dharmeṇa gataḥ lokān anuttamān sahasracityaḥ rājarṣiḥ prāṇān iṣṭān mahā yaśāḥ sarva kāmaiḥ ca saṃpūrṇam dattvā veśma hiraṇmayam bhakṣya bhojyasya ca kṛtān rāśayaḥ parvata upamān śāṇḍilyāya purā dattvā sa manyuḥ divam āsthitaḥ nāmnā ca dyutimān nāma śālva rājaḥ mahā dyutiḥ dattvā rājyam ṛcīkāya gataḥ lokān anuttamān madirāśvaḥ ca rājarṣiḥ dattvā kanyām sumadhyamām hiraṇyahastāya gataḥ lokān devaiḥ adhiṣṭhitān lomapādaḥ ca rājarṣiḥ śāntām dattvā sutām prabhuḥ ṛṣyaśṛṅgāya vipulaiḥ sarvaiḥ kāmaiḥ ayujyata kautsāya dattvā kanyām tu haṃsīm nāma yaśasvinīm gataḥ akṣayān atas lokān rājarṣiḥ ca bhagīrathaḥ dattvā śata sahasram tu gavām rājā bhagīrathaḥ sa vatsānām kohalāya gataḥ lokān anuttamān yudhiṣṭhira gatāḥ svargam nivartante punar punar teṣām pratiṣṭhitā kīrtiḥ yāvat sthāsyati medinī śiṣṭānām caritam hi etat kīrtitam me yudhiṣṭhira dāna yajña prajā sargaiḥ ete hi divam āsthitāḥ dattvā tu satatam te astu kauravāṇām dhuraṃdhara dāna yajña kriyā yuktā buddhiḥ dharma upacāyinī yatra te nṛpa śārdūla saṃdehaḥ vai bhaviṣyati yudhiṣṭhiraḥ śvas prabhāte hi vakṣyāmi saṃdhyā hi samupasthitā dāna dharmeṇa mahatā ye prāptāḥ tridivam nṛpāḥ imān tu śrotum icchāmi dharmān dharma bhṛtām vara katham kebhyaḥ ca dharmyam ca dānam dātavyam iṣyate bhīṣmaḥ kaiḥ kāraṇaiḥ katividham śrotum icchāmi tattvataḥ śṛṇu tattvena kaunteya dānam prati mama anagha yathā dānam pradātavyam sarva varṇeṣu bhārata dharmāt arthāt bhayāt kāmāt kāruṇyāt iti bhārata dānam pañcavidham jñeyam kāraṇaiḥ yaiḥ nibodha tat iti dānam pradātavyam brāhmaṇebhyaḥ anasūyatā dadāti vā dāsyati vā mahyam dattam anena vā na asya aham na madīyaḥ ayam pāpam kuryāt vimānitaḥ iti dadyāt bhayāt eva dṛḍham mūḍhāya paṇḍitaḥ vayasyāya evam akliṣṭam dānam dadyāt atandritaḥ dīnaḥ ca yācate ca ayam alpena api hi tuṣyati iti dadyāt daridrāya kāruṇyāt iti sarvathā iti pañcavidham dānam puṇya kīrti vivardhanam yathāśaktyā pradātavyam evam āha prajāpatiḥ vānaprastha uñcha vṛttīnām tapaḥ vistṛta cetasām nāradaḥ uvāca evam uktvā mahādevaḥ śrotu kāmaḥ svayam prabhuḥ anukūlām priyām bhāryām pārśva sthām samabhāṣata guṇavān sa bhaviṣyati daṃpatyoḥ eṣa vai dharmaḥ sahadharma kṛtaḥ śubhaḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ anuśāsya śubhaiḥ vākyaiḥ bhīṣmaḥ tu āha mahāmatim prītyā punar sa śuśrūṣuḥ vacanam yat yudhiṣṭhire pitāmahaḥ me viprarṣe bhīṣmam kāla vaśam gatam kim apṛcchat tadā rājā sarvasāmāsikam hitam vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ tat me vada mahā prājña śrotum kautūhalam hi me bhūyas eva mahā rāja śṛṇu dharmasamuccayam yat apṛcchat tadā rājā kuntī putraḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ śara talpa gatam bhīṣmam sarva pārthiva saṃnidhau yudhiṣṭhiraḥ ajātaśatruḥ prīta ātmā punar eva abhyabhāṣata pitāmaha mahā prājña sarva śāstra viśārada parāvara jñaḥ bhūtānām dayāvān sarva jantuṣu tvādṛśaḥ durlabhaḥ loke sāṃpratam jñāna darśanaḥ ayam sa kālaḥ saṃprāptaḥ durlabhaḥ jñāti bāndhavaiḥ śāstā nu nā asti naḥ kaḥ cit tvat ṛte puruṣa ṛṣabha āścaryam paramam vākyam śrotum icchāmi bhārata ayam nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān sarva pārthiva saṃnidhau asya eva tu samakṣam naḥ pārthivānām tathā eva ca itivṛttam purāṇam ca śrotṝṇām paramam hitam vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ tasya tat vacanam śrutvā snehāt āgata viklavaḥ prapiban iva tam dṛṣṭvā bhīṣmaḥ vacanam abravīt śṛṇu rājan purā vṛttam itihāsam purātanam etāvat uktvā gāṅgeyaḥ praṇamya śirasā harim dharmarājam samīkṣya idam punar vaktum samārabhat ayam nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān putra artham vrata kāṅkṣayā dīkṣitaḥ abhūt mahābāhuḥ purā dvādaśa vārṣikam dīkṣitam keśavam draṣṭum abhijagmuḥ mahā ṛṣayaḥ sevitum ca mahātmānaḥ prīyamāṇāḥ janārdanam nāradaḥ parvataḥ ca eva kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaḥ tathā devalaḥ kāśyapaḥ ca eva hastikāśyapaḥ eva ca apare api tapaḥ siddhāḥ satya vrata parāyaṇāḥ keśavaḥ tān abhigatān prītyā saṃpratigṛhya ca teṣām atithi satkāram pūjana artham kula ucitam upaviṣṭeṣu sarveṣu viṣṭareṣu tadā anagha viśvasteṣu hi tuṣṭeṣu keśava arcanayā punar parasparam kathāḥ divyāḥ prāvartanta manoramāḥ viṣṇoḥ nārāyaṇasya eva prasādāt kathayāmi tāḥ yaḥ ca go vṛṣabhāṅkasya prabhāvaḥ abhūt mahātmanaḥ yatra devī mahādevam apṛcchat saṃśayān purā kathayām āsa śarvaḥ tān devyāḥ priya cikīrṣayā umāpatyoḥ ca saṃvādam śṛṇu tāta manoramam varṇa āśramāṇām dharmaḥ ca tatra tāta samāhitaḥ ṛṣi dharmaḥ ca nikhilaḥ rāja dharmaḥ ca puṣkalaḥ prāṇamokṣa vidhiḥ ca eva tīrtha caryā ca puṣkalā strī dharmaḥ ca svayam devyā devadevāya bhāṣitaḥ evamādi śubham sarvam tatra tāta samāhitam rudrāṇyāḥ saṃśaya praśnaḥ yatra tāta pravartate dhanyam yaśasyam āyuṣyam dharmyam ca paramam hitam puṣṭi yogam idam divyam kathyamānam mayā śṛṇu itihāsam imam divyam pavitram paramam śubham sāyam prātar sadā samyak śrotavyam ca bubhūṣatā tatas nārāyaṇaḥ devaḥ saṃkliṣṭaḥ vrata caryayā vahniḥ viniḥsṛtaḥ vaktrāt kṛṣṇasya adbhuta darśanaḥ agninā tena mahatā niḥsṛtena mukhāt vibhoḥ paśyatām eva sarveṣām dagdhaḥ eva naga uttamaḥ mṛga pakṣi samākīrṇaḥ śvāpadaiḥ abhisaṃkulaḥ vṛkṣa gulma latā ākīrṇaḥ mathitaḥ dīna darśanaḥ punar sa dṛṣṭa mātreṇa hariṇā saumya cetasā matta vāraṇa saṃyuktaḥ nānā pakṣi gaṇaiḥ yutaḥ tam dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭa romāṇaḥ sarve muni gaṇāḥ tadā vismitāḥ parama āyastāḥ sādhvasa ākula locanāḥ tān samīkṣya eva madhuram babhāṣe puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ etam me saṃśayam sarve yāthātathyam aninditāḥ ṛṣayaḥ vaktum arhanti niścayena artha kovidāḥ keśavasya vacaḥ śrutvā tuṣṭuvuḥ muni puṃgavāḥ bhavān sṛjati vai lokān bhavān saṃharati prajāḥ bhavān śītam bhavān uṣṇam bhavān satyam bhavān kratuḥ nityam hita artham lokānām bhavadbhiḥ kriyate tapaḥ tasmāt loka hitam guhyam śrūyatām kathayāmi vaḥ asuraḥ sāṃpratam kaḥ cit ahitaḥ loka nāśanaḥ babhūva sa mayā vadhyaḥ lokānām hita kāmyayā putreṇa me vadhaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ tasya vai muni puṃgavāḥ tad artham putram eva aham sisṛkṣuḥ vanam āgataḥ ātmanaḥ sadṛśam putram aham janayitum vrataiḥ atha ātmā mama deha sthaḥ saḥ agniḥ bhūtvā viniḥsṛtaḥ viniḥsṛtya gataḥ draṣṭum kṣaṇena ca pitāmaham anujñātaḥ ca tena eva punar āyāt mama antikam etat me vaiṣṇavam tejaḥ mama vaktrāt viniḥsṛtam dṛṣṭvā nāśam gireḥ tasya saumya bhāva tayā mama punar sa dṛṣṭa mātreṇa giriḥ āsīt yathāpuram etat guhyam mayā tathyam kathitam vaḥ samāsatas ṛṣīṇām evam uktvā tu tān punar pratyabhāṣata bhavatām darśanāt eva prītiḥ abhyadhikā mama sarvatra gatimantaḥ ca jñāna vijñāna bhāvitāḥ gatāgata jñāḥ lokānām sarve nirdhūta kalmaṣāḥ tasmāt bhavadbhiḥ yat kim cit dṛṣṭam vā api atha vā śrutam yuṣmābhiḥ kathitam yat syāt tapasā bhāvitātmabhiḥ tat syāt amṛta saṃkāśam vāc madhu śravaṇe spṛhā rāga dveṣa viyuktānām satatam satya vādinām śraddheyam śravaṇīyam ca vacanam hi satām bhavet tat aham sat jana mukhāt niḥsṛtam jana saṃsadi kathayiṣyāmi aharahar buddhi dīpa karam nṛṇām vāc śalya vigatāḥ ca anye praśaṃsanti purātanam bhīṣmaḥ evam bruvati govinde śravaṇa artham mahā ṛṣayaḥ vāgbhiḥ sa añjali mālābhiḥ idam ūcuḥ janārdanam ayuktam asmān evam tvam vācā vara da bhāṣitum tvad śāsana mukhāḥ sarve tvad adhīna pariśramāḥ evam pūjayitum ca asmān na ca eva arhasi keśava evam uktaḥ hṛṣīkeśaḥ sa smitam ca idam abravīt aham mānuṣa yoni sthaḥ sāṃpratam muni puṃgavāḥ tasmāt mānuṣa vat vīryam mama jānīta suvratāḥ bhīṣmaḥ evam saṃcoditāḥ sarve keśavena mahātmanā ṛṣayaḥ ca anuvartante vāsudevasya śāsanam tatas tu ṛṣi gaṇāḥ sarve nāradam devadarśanam ṛṣiḥ ugra tapāḥ ca ayam keśavasya priyaḥ adhikam purāṇa jñaḥ ca vāgmī ca kāraṇaiḥ tam ca menire sarve tad arhaṇam kṛtvā nāradam vākyam abruvan dṛṣṭam vai yat tat āścaryam śrotṝṇām paramam priyam tattvam tvam aviśeṣeṇa hita artham sarvam āditas bhīṣmaḥ tadā saṃcoditaḥ sarvaiḥ ṛṣibhiḥ nāradaḥ tathā praṇamya śirasā viṣṇum sarva loka hite ratam samudvīkṣya hṛṣīkeśam vaktum evā upacakrame tatas nārāyaṇa suhṛd nāradaḥ vadatām varaḥ nāradaḥ śaṃkarasya umayā sārdham saṃvādam anvabhāṣata bhagavan tīrtha yātrā artham tathā eva caratā mayā divyam adbhuta saṃkāśam dṛṣṭam haimavatam vanam nānā vṛkṣa latā yuktam nānā pakṣi gaṇaiḥ yutam nānā ratna samākīrṇam nānā bhāva samanvitam siddha cāraṇa saṃvāsam bhūta saṃghaiḥ niṣevitam variṣṭha apsarasā ākīrṇam nānā gandharva saṃkulam mṛdaṅga muraja udghuṣṭam śaṅkha vīṇā abhināditam nānā rūpaiḥ virūpaiḥ ca bhīma rūpaiḥ bhayānakaiḥ ulūka śyena vadanaiḥ kāka gṛdhra mukhaiḥ tathā evam bahuvidha ākāraiḥ bhūta saṃghaiḥ bhṛśa ākulam nānadyamānam bahudhā hara pāriṣadaiḥ bhṛśam ghora rūpam su durdarśam rakṣaḥ gaṇa śataiḥ vṛtam samājam tad vane dṛṣṭam mayā bhūtapateḥ purā ṣaṭpadaiḥ upagītam ca prathame māsi mādhave utkrośat krauñca kuraraiḥ sārasaiḥ jīvajīvakaiḥ mattābhiḥ parapuṣṭābhiḥ kūjantībhiḥ samākulam uttama utsava saṃkāśam bhīma rūpataram tatas mārtāṇḍa raśmi saṃcārāḥ viśvedeva gaṇāḥ tathā tathā nāgāḥ tathā ādityāḥ lokapālāḥ hutāśanāḥ vātāḥ ca sarve vāyanti divya puṣpa samākulāḥ kirantaḥ sarva puṣpāṇi kirantaḥ adbhuta darśanāḥ paryaṅke iva babhrāje upaviṣṭaḥ mahā dyutiḥ vyāla yajñopavītaḥ ca lohita antra vibhūṣitaḥ hari śmaśru jaṭaḥ bhīmaḥ bhaya kartā suradviṣām bhaya ghnaḥ sarva bhūtānām bhaktānām abhayaṃkaraḥ ṛṣibhiḥ ca apsarobhiḥ ca sarvatas ca api śobhitaḥ tatra bhūtapateḥ sthānam deva dānava saṃkulam mahā uraga samākīrṇam sarveṣām loma harṣaṇam bhīma rūpam anirdeśyam apradhṛṣyatamam vibho tatra bhūtapatim devam āsīnam śikhara uttame ṛṣayaḥ bhūta saṃghāḥ ca praṇamya śirasā haram gīrbhiḥ parama śuddhābhiḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ ca maheśvaram ṛṣayaḥ vālakhilyāḥ ca tathā viprarṣayaḥ ca ye a yoni jāḥ yoni jāḥ ca tapaḥ siddhāḥ mahā ṛṣayaḥ tatas tasmin kṣaṇe devī bhūta strī gaṇa sevitā hara tulya ambara dharā samāna vrata cāriṇī kāñcanam kalaśam gṛhya sarva tīrtha ambu pūritam puṣpa vṛṣṭyā abhivarṣantī divya gandha samāyutā sarit varābhiḥ sarvābhiḥ pṛṣṭhatas anugatā varā sevitum bhagavat pārśvam ājagāma śuci smitā manaḥ priyam cikīrṣantī krīḍā artham śaṃkara antike manoharābhyām pāṇibhyām hara netre pidhāya tu avekṣya hṛṣṭā sva gaṇān smayantī pṛṣṭhatas sthitā devyā ca andhīkṛte deve kaśmalam samapadyata nimīlite bhūtapatau naṣṭa candra arka tārakam niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkāram tamasā ca abhisaṃvṛtam viṣaṇṇam bhaya saṃtrastam jagat āsīt bhaya ākulam hāhākāraḥ tu ṛṣīṇām ca lokānām abhavat tadā tamaḥ abhibhūte saṃbhrānte loke jīvita śaṅkite tṛtīyam ca asya saṃbhūtam lalāṭe netram āyatam dvādaśa āditya saṃkāśam lokān bhāsau abhāsayat paśyatām eva sarveṣām deva dānava rakṣasām tam dṛṣṭvā mathitam śailam śailaputrī sa viklavā pituḥ śamanam icchantī papāta bhuvi pādayoḥ tat dṛṣṭvā devadeveśaḥ devyāḥ duḥkham anuttamam dṛṣṭa mātraḥ bhagavatā saumya yuktena cetasā kṣaṇena himavān śailaḥ prakṛti sthaḥ abhavat punar hṛṣṭa puṣṭa vihaṃgaḥ ca praphulla druma kānanaḥ siddha cāraṇa saṃghaiḥ ca prīti yuktaiḥ samākulaḥ pitaram prakṛti stham tu dṛṣṭvā haimavatī bhṛśam devī vismaya saṃyuktā praṣṭu kāmā maheśvaram hita artham sarva lokānām prajānām hita kāmyayā devadevam mahādevī babhāṣa idam vacaḥ arthavat bhagavan devadeveśa śūlapāṇe mahā dyute kimartham devadeveśa lalāṭe asmin prakāśate ati sūrya agni saṃkāśam tṛtīyam netram āyatam punar sa dṛṣṭa mātraḥ tu prakṛti sthaḥ pitā mama nāradaḥ evam uktaḥ tayā devyā prīyamāṇaḥ abravīt bhavaḥ sthāne saṃśayitum devi dharma jñe priya bhāṣiṇi tvat ṛte mām hi vai praṣṭum na śakyam kena cit priye prakāśam yadi vā guhyam tvad priya artham bravīmi aham śṛṇu tat sarvam akhilam asyām saṃsadi bhāmini sarveṣām eva lokānām kūṭastham viddhi mām priye mad adhīnāḥ trayaḥ lokāḥ yathā viṣṇau tathā mayi sraṣṭā viṣṇuḥ aham goptā iti tat viddhi bhāmini tasmāt yadā mām spṛśati śubham vā yadi vā itarat netre me pihite devi krīḍana artham dṛḍha vrate tvad kṛte naṣṭa candra arkam jagat āsīt bhṛśa ākulam tṛtīyam locanam sṛṣṭam lokān saṃrakṣitum mayā asya eva tejasā akṣṇaḥ me mathitaḥ ayam giriḥ śubhe vṛkṣa gulma latā ākīrṇaḥ vyāla adhyuṣita kaṃdaraḥ nāradaḥ kathitam saṃśaya sthānam nirviśaṅkā bhava priye kṣaṇa jñā devadevasya śrotu kāmā priyam hitam uttaram ca api bhagavan paścimam śubha darśanam dakṣiṇam ca mukham raudram kena ūrdhvam jaṭila āvṛtam yathādiśam mahādeva śrotum icchāmi kāraṇam maheśvaraḥ tayoḥ eva vināśāya nirmitāḥ viśvakarmaṇā mṛga pakṣi gaṇānām ca sthāvarāṇām tathā eva ca sarvatas sāram uddhṛtya tilaśas loka pūjitā tilottamā iti vikhyātā apsarāḥ sā babhūva ha deva kāryam kariṣyantī hāva bhāva samanvitā sā tu paryantam āgamya rūpeṇa apratimā bhuvi mayā bahu matā ca iyam deva kāryam kariṣyati iti matvā tadā ca aham kurvantīm mām pradakṣiṇam aindram mukham idam pūrvam tapaḥ caryā param sadā dakṣiṇam me mukham divyam raudram saṃharati prajāḥ umā etat te sarvam ākhyātam kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi bhagavan śrotum icchāmi śūlapāṇe vara prada maheśvaraḥ kimartham nīla tā kaṇṭhe bhāti barha nibhā tava hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā purā yuga antare yatnāt amṛta artham sura asuraiḥ viṣam tatra samudbhūtam sarva loka vināśanam tat dṛṣṭvā vibudhāḥ sarve tadā vimanasaḥ abhavan grastam hi tat mayā devi lokānām hita kāraṇāt tad kṛtā nīla tā ca āsīt kaṇṭhe barhi nibhā śubhe tadā prabhṛti ca eva aham nīlakaṇṭhaḥ iti smṛtaḥ umā etat te sarvam ākhyātam kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi bahūnām āyudhānām tvam pinākam dhartum icchasi maheśvaraḥ śastra āgamam te vakṣyāmi śṛṇu dharmyam śuci smite yuga antare mahādevi kaṇvaḥ nāma mahā muniḥ dharamāṇaḥ ca tat sarvam tapaḥ caryām atha akarot tasmai brahmā varam dātum jagāma tapasā arcitaḥ dattvā tasmai varam devaḥ veṇum dṛṣṭvā tu acintayat loka kāryam samuddiśya veṇunā anena bhāmini viṣṇoḥ mama ca sāmarthyam jñātvā lokapitāmahaḥ dhanuṣī dve tadā prādāt viṣṇave ca mama eva ca pinākam nāma me cāpam śārṅgam nāma hareḥ dhanuḥ tṛtīyam avaśeṣeṇa gāṇḍīvam abhavat dhanuḥ umā ayam tu vṛṣabhaḥ kasmāt vāhana tvam upāgamat maheśvaraḥ eṣa me saṃśayaḥ deva tam me śaṃsitum arhasi tat aham te pravakṣyāmi vāhanam sa yathā bhavat ādisarge purā gāvaḥ śveta varṇāḥ śuci smite bala saṃhanana upetāḥ darpa yuktāḥ caranti tāḥ aham tu tapaḥ ātiṣṭhe tasmin kāle śubha ānane gāvaḥ me pārśvam āviśya darpa utsiktāḥ samantataḥ apacāreṇa ca etāsām manaḥ kṣobhaḥ abhavat mama tasmāt dagdhāḥ mayā gāvaḥ roṣa āviṣṭena cetasā anena vṛṣabheṇa aham śamitaḥ saṃprasādanaiḥ tadā prabhṛti śāntāḥ ca varṇa bheda tvam āgatāḥ śvetaḥ ayam vṛṣabhaḥ devi pūrva saṃskāra saṃyutaḥ umā prasannaḥ ca abhavam devi tadā gopati tām gataḥ bhagavan sarva bhūta īśa śūlapāṇe vṛṣadhvaja āvāseṣu vicitreṣu ramyeṣu ca śubheṣu ca satsu ca anyeṣu deśeṣu śmaśāne ramase katham keśa asthi kalile bhīme kapāla śata saṃkule sṛgāla gṛdhra saṃpūrṇe śava dhūma samākule maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā āvāsa artham purā devi śuddha anveṣī śuci smite na adhyagaccham ciram kālam deśam śucitamam śubhe eṣa me abhiniveśaḥ abhūt tasmin kāle prajāḥ prati ākulam su mahat ghoram prādurāsīt samantataḥ saṃbhūtā bhūta sṛṣṭiḥ ca ghorā loka bhaya āvahā nānā varṇa virūpāḥ ca tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭrāḥ prahāriṇaḥ piśāca rakṣaḥ vadanāḥ prāṇinām prāṇa hāriṇaḥ evam loke kṣayam yāte prāṇi hīne pitāmahaḥ cintayan tad pratīkāram mām ca śaktam hi nigrahe evam jñātvā tadā brahmā tasmin karmaṇi ayojayat tat ca prāṇi dayā artham tu mayā api anumatam priye tasmāt saṃrakṣitāḥ devi bhūtebhyaḥ prāṇinaḥ bhayāt asmāt śmaśānāt medhyam tu na asti kim cit anindite niḥsaṃpātam manuṣyāṇām tasmāt śucitamam smṛtam bhūta sṛṣṭim tu tām ca aham śmaśāne saṃnyaveśayam tasmāt me saṃnivāsāya śmaśāne rocate manaḥ kapāla śata saṃpūrṇam bhīma rūpam bhayānakam tatrasthaḥ aham prajāḥ sarvāḥ pālayāmi dine dine tasmāt loka hita arthāya śmaśāne ramayāmi aham umā etat te sarvam ākhyātam kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi piṅgalam vikṛtam bhāti rūpam te su bhayānakam bhasma digdham virūpa akṣam tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭram jaṭā ākulam vyāghra uraga tvac saṃvītam kapila śmaśru saṃtatam raudram bhayānakam ghoram śūla paṭṭasa saṃyutam maheśvaraḥ kimartham īdṛśam rūpam tat me śaṃsitum arhasi tat aham kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu tattvam samāhitā dvividhaḥ laukikaḥ bhāvaḥ śītam uṣṇam iti priye saumya tvam satatam viṣṇau mayi āgneyam pratiṣṭhitam raudra ākṛti virūpa akṣam śūla paṭṭasa saṃyutam yadi aham viparītaḥ syām etat tyaktvā śubha ānane tasmāt mayā idam dhriyate rūpam loka hita eṣiṇā umā bhagavan devadeveśa śūlapāṇe vṛṣadhvaja maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam purā aham kāraṇāt devi kopa yuktaḥ śuci smite dakṣa yajña vadha arthāya bhūta saṃgha samāvṛtaḥ devāḥ vibhraṃśitāḥ te vai yeṣām bhāgaḥ kratau kṛtaḥ paśyan ca anaparādhī san pādāṅguṣṭhena pīḍitaḥ tathā vinikṛtena aham sāma pūrvam prasāditaḥ tat me cintayataḥ ca āsīt paścāttāpaḥ punar priye tadā prabhṛti somam hi śirasā dhārayāmi aham tadā prabhṛti vai somaḥ mūrdhni saṃdṛśyate sadā nāradaḥ evam bruvati deveśe vismitāḥ parama ṛṣayaḥ vāgbhiḥ sa añjali mālābhiḥ abhituṣṭuvuḥ īśvaram namaḥ deva ādidevāya namaḥ śaśi kalā dhara namaḥ śāntatarāt śānta namaḥ candrasya pālaka namaḥ triśūla hastāya pannaga ābharaṇāya ca namaḥ astu viṣama akṣāya dakṣa yajña pradāhaka aho devasya māhātmyam mahādevasya vai kṛpā prīti artham ca munīnām sā kṣaṇajñā paramam hitam varṇa āśrama gatam dharmam vaktum arhasi aśeṣatas na tṛptiḥ asti deveśa śravaṇīyam hi te vacaḥ vaktum arhasi deveśa lokānām hita kāmyayā maheśvaraḥ śṛṇu tat sarvam akhilam dharmam varṇa āśrama āśritam brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāḥ vaiśyāḥ śūdrāḥ ca iti caturvidhāḥ brahmaṇā vihitāḥ pūrvam lokatantram abhīpsatā karmāṇi ca tad arhāṇi śāstreṣu vihitāni vai mukhataḥ brāhmaṇāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ tasmāt te vāc viśāradāḥ bāhubhyām kṣatriyāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ tasmāt bāhu bala anvitāḥ udarāt udgatāḥ vaiśyāḥ tasmāt vārttā upajīvinaḥ śūdrāḥ ca pādataḥ sṛṣṭāḥ tasmāt te paricārakāḥ teṣām dharmān ca karmāṇi śṛṇu devi samāhitā viprāḥ kṛtāḥ bhūmidevāḥ lokānām dhāraṇe kṛtāḥ te kaiḥ cit na avamantavyāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ hitam icchubhiḥ yadi te brāhmaṇāḥ na syuḥ dāna yoga vahāḥ sadā abudhaḥ vā daridraḥ vā pūjanīyaḥ sadā eva saḥ prahareta haret vā api dhanam teṣām nara adhamaḥ kārayet dhīna karmāṇi kāma lobha vimohanāt mām eva praharet mūḍhaḥ mad dhanasya apahārakaḥ mama eva prekṣaṇam kṛtvā nandate mūḍha cetanaḥ karmāṇi adhyāpanam ca eva yājanam ca pratigrahaḥ satyam śāntiḥ tapaḥ śaucam tasya dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ vikrayaḥ rasadhānyānām brāhmaṇasya vigarhitaḥ anāpadi ca śūdra annam vṛṣalī saṃgrahaḥ tathā tapaḥ eva sadā dharmaḥ brāhmaṇasya na saṃśayaḥ sa tu dharma artham utpannaḥ pūrvam dhātrā tapaḥ balāt agni kāryam paraḥ dharmaḥ nityam yajña upavīti tā amṛtāśyam param dharmaḥ gṛha puṣpa baliḥ tathā gṛha saṃmārjanam dharmaḥ ālepana vidhiḥ tathā iṣṭayaḥ paśubandhāḥ ca vidhi pūrvam param tapaḥ daṃpatyoḥ sama śīla tvam dharmaḥ vai gṛhamedhinām tam aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā kṣatriyāḥ tu tathā devi prajānām pālane smṛtāḥ rakṣaṇāt kṣatriyaiḥ eva jagat bhavati śāśvatam tasya api adhyayanam dānam yajanam dharmataḥ smṛtam bhītānām rakṣaṇam ca eva pāpānām anuśāsanam satām saṃpoṣaṇam ca eva kara ṣaṣ bhāga jīvanam utsāhaḥ śastra jīvi tvam tasya dharmāḥ sanātanāḥ bhṛtyānām bharaṇam dharmaḥ kṛte karmaṇi amogha tā evam te vihitāḥ pūrvaiḥ dharmāḥ karma vidhānataḥ tathā eva devi vaiśyāḥ ca lokayātrā hitāḥ smṛtāḥ teṣām adhyayanam dānam yajanam dharmaḥ iṣyate vaiśyasya satatam dharmaḥ pāśupālyam kṛṣiḥ tathā vāṇijyam sat pathe sthānam ātitheyam sadā bhavet viprāṇām svāgatam nyāyaḥ vaiśya dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ evam te vihitā devi lokayātrā svayaṃbhuvā tathā eva śūdrāḥ vihitāḥ sarva dharma prasādhakāḥ śūdrāḥ ca yadi te na syuḥ karma kartā na vidyate trayaḥ pūrve śūdra mūlāḥ tasmāt karmakarāḥ smṛtāḥ brāhmaṇa ādiṣu śuśrūṣā dāsa dharmaḥ iti smṛtaḥ vārtā ca kāru karmāṇi śilpam nāṭyam tathā eva ca te api evam vihitāḥ loke karma yogāḥ śubha ānane evam caturṇām varṇānām puṇya lokāḥ paratra ca vihitāḥ ca tathā dṛṣṭāḥ yathāvat dharma cāriṇām eṣa varṇa āśrayaḥ dharmaḥ karma ca eva tad arpaṇam umā kathitam śrotu kāmāyāḥ kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi maheśvaraḥ śrotum icchāmi aham deva dharmam āśramiṇām vibho atha āśrama gatam dharmam śṛṇu devi samāhitā āśramāṇām tu yaḥ dharmaḥ kriyate brahma vādibhiḥ gṛhasthaḥ pravaraḥ teṣām gārhasthya vratam āśritaḥ pañcayajña kriyā śaucam dāra tuṣṭiḥ atandri tā ṛtu kāla abhigamanam dāna yajña tapāṃsi ca a vipravāsaḥ tasya iṣṭam svādhyāyaḥ ca agni pūjanam atithīnām ābhimukhyam śaktyā ca iṣṭa nimantraṇam anugrahaḥ ca sarveṣām manaḥ vāc kāya karmabhiḥ evamādi śubham ca anyat kuryāt tad vṛttavān gṛhī evam saṃcarataḥ tasya puṇya lokāḥ na saṃśayaḥ tathā eva vānaprasthasya dharmāḥ proktāḥ sanātanāḥ gṛha vāsam samutsṛjya niścitya ekamanāḥ śubhaiḥ bhūmiśayyā jaṭāḥ śmaśru carma valkala dhāraṇam devatā atithi satkāraḥ mahā kṛcchra abhipūjanam agnihotram triṣavaṇam nityam tasya vidhīyate brahmacaryam kṣamā śaucam tasya dharmāḥ sanātanāḥ yati dharmāḥ tathā devi saṅgān tyaktvā yatas tatas ākiṃcanyam anārambhaḥ sarvatas śaucam ārjavam sadā dhyāna para tvam ca doṣa śuddhiḥ kṣamā dayā brahmacārī ca yaḥ devi janma prabhṛti dīkṣitaḥ sarva kāleṣu sarvatra guru pūjām samācaret bhaikṣa caryā agni kāryam ca sadā jala niṣevaṇam svādhyāyaḥ satatam devi tasya dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ tasya ceṣṭā tu guru artham ā prāṇa antam iti sthitiḥ evam saḥ api amalān lokān brāhmaṇaḥ pratipadyate eṣa te kathitaḥ devi dharmaḥ ca āśrama vāsinām umā kathitam te samāsena kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi tapasā karśitāḥ nityam tapoḥ jana parāyaṇāḥ asya kiṃlakṣaṇaḥ dharmaḥ kīdṛśaḥ ca āgamaḥ tathā nāradaḥ evam vadantyām rudrāṇyām ṛṣayaḥ sādhu sādhu iti abruvan hṛṣṭa manasaḥ sarve tad gata mānasāḥ śṛṇvantīm ṛṣi dharmān tu ṛṣayaḥ ca abhyapūjayan tvad prasādāt vayam devi śroṣyāmaḥ paramam hitam dhanyāḥ khalu vayam sarve pāda mūlam tava āśritāḥ maheśvaraḥ iti sarve tadā devīm vācā samabhipūjayan nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā manasvini bahu śākhaḥ bahuvidhaḥ ṛṣi dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ prāyaśas svarga bhoga artham ṛṣibhiḥ kriyate tapaḥ tathā saṃcaratām teṣām devi dharma vidhim śṛṇu bhūtvā pūrvam gṛhasthaḥ tu putra ānṛṇyam avāpya ca kalatra kāryam saṃsthāpya kāraṇāt saṃtyajet gṛham avasthāpya manaḥ dhṛtyā vyavasāya puraḥsaraḥ nirdvaṃdvaḥ vā sa dāraḥ vā vana vāsāya saṃvrajet deśāḥ parama puṇyāḥ ye nadī vana samanvitāḥ ābādha muktāḥ prāyeṇa tīrtha āyatana saṃyutāḥ tatra gatvā vidhim jñātvā dīkṣām kuryāt yathākramam dīkṣitvā ekamanāḥ bhūtvā paricaryām samācaret kālya utthānam ca śaucam ca sarva deva praṇāmanam śakṛt ālepanam kāye tyakta doṣa apramādi tā sāyam prātar ca abhiṣekam agnihotram yathāvidhi kāya śaucam ca kāryam ca jaṭā valkala dhāraṇam nīvāra grahaṇam kāle śāka mūla upacāyanam atithīnām ābhimukhyam tad para tvam ca sarvaśas pādya āsanābhyām saṃpūjā tathā āhāra nimantraṇam a grāmyapacanam kāle pitṛ deva arcanam tathā paścāt atithi satkāraḥ tasya dharmāḥ sanātanāḥ śiṣṭaiḥ dharma āsane ca eva dharma artha sahitāḥ kathāḥ pratiśraya vibhāgaḥ ca bhūmi śayyā śilāsu vā divā rātrau yathāyogam śaucam dharma artha cintanā evam dharmāḥ purā dṛṣṭāḥ sāmānyāḥ vanavāsinām paribhraṣṭaḥ yadā svargāt viśiṣṭaḥ tu bhavet nṛpa evam dharmaḥ sadā devi sarveṣām vana vāsinām umā bhagavan devadeveśaḥ ṛṣīṇām caritam śubham maheśvaraḥ viśeṣa dharmān icchāmi śrotum kautūhalam hi me tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā vīraśayyām upāsadbhiḥ vīrasthāna upasevibhiḥ pañcayajña paraiḥ nityam paurṇamāsya parāyaṇaiḥ maṇḍūkaśāyaiḥ hemante śaivāla aṅkura bhojanaiḥ cīra valkala saṃvītaiḥ mṛga ajina dharaiḥ tathā cāturmāsya paraiḥ kaiḥ cit deva dharma parāyaṇaiḥ evam kṛtvā śubham karma paścāt yāti triviṣṭapam umā tapaḥ viśeṣa saṃyuktāḥ kathitāḥ te śuci smite bhagavan sarva bhūta īśa teṣu ye dāra saṃyutāḥ maheśvaraḥ kīdṛśam caritam teṣām tat me śaṃsitum arhasi ye eka patnī dharmāṇaḥ caranti vipulam tapaḥ puṣkareṣu ca ye ca anye nadī vana samāśritāḥ hiṃsā droha vimuktāḥ ca sarva bhūta anukampinaḥ śāntāḥ dāntāḥ jita krodhāḥ sarva ātithya parāyaṇāḥ prāṇiṣu ātma upamāḥ nityam ṛtu kāla abhigāminaḥ teṣām paricchada ārambhāḥ kṛtyā upakaraṇāni ca gṛhastha vat vidhīyante yathāyogam pramāṇataḥ poṣaṇa artham ca dārāṇām agni kārya artham eva ca gāvaḥ ca karṣaṇam ca eva sarvam etat vidhīyate te sva dāraiḥ samāyānti puṇyān lokān dṛḍha vratāḥ patibhiḥ saha ye dārāḥ caranti vipulam tapaḥ umā maheśvaraḥ vānaprastha gatam sarvam phala pākam śṛṇu priye agniyogam vrajan grīṣme naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam rudra loke hi jāyeta vidhi dṛṣṭena karmaṇā uda vāsa vratam kurvan varṣa kāle dṛḍha vrataḥ kāṣṭha vat maunam āsthāya naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam saṃviśet varṣāṇām bhoga saṃyuktaḥ naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam vīrāsana gataḥ yaḥ tu kaṇṭaka āphalaka āśritaḥ gandharveṣu abhijāyeta naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam vīra sthāyī ca ūrdhvabāhuḥ naraḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam pādāṅguṣṭhena yaḥ tiṣṭhet ūrdhvabāhuḥ jita indriyaḥ āhāra niyamam kṛtvā muniḥ dvādaśa vārṣikam nāga loke abhijāyeta saṃvatsara gaṇān bahūn evam dṛḍha vratāḥ devi vānaprasthāḥ ca karmabhiḥ sthāneṣu teṣu tiṣṭhanti tadvat bhoga samanvitāḥ tebhyaḥ bhraṣṭāḥ punar devi jāyante nṛṣu bhoginaḥ umā eṣām yāyāvarāṇām tu dharmam icchāmi mānada maheśvaraḥ kṛpayā parayā āviṣṭaḥ tam me brūhi maheśvara dharmam yāyāvarāṇām tu śṛṇu bhāmini tad parā vrata upavāsa śuddha aṅgāḥ tīrtha snāna parāyaṇāḥ dhṛtimantaḥ kṣamā yuktāḥ satya vrata parāyaṇāḥ pakṣa māsa upavāsaiḥ ca karśitāḥ dharma darśinaḥ varṣa śīta ātapaiḥ eva kurvantaḥ paramam tapaḥ divya bhūṣaṇa saṃyuktāḥ vimāna varam āsthitāḥ vicaranti yathākāmam divya strī gaṇa saṃyutāḥ umā maheśvaraḥ teṣām cakracarāṇām tu dharmam icchāmi vai prabho hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu śākaṭikam śubhe saṃvahantaḥ dhuram dāraiḥ śākaṭānām tu sarvadā prārthayante yathā grāmyāḥ śakaṭaiḥ bhaikṣa caryayā tapoḥ jana para adhīnāḥ tapasā kṣīṇa kalmaṣāḥ paryaṭantaḥ diśaḥ sarvāḥ kāma krodha vivarjitāḥ sva vaśāt eva te mṛtyum abhikāṅkṣanti nityaśas indra loke tathā teṣām nirmitāḥ bhoga saṃcayāḥ vara apsarobhiḥ saṃyuktāḥ cira kālam anindite umā maheśvaraḥ vaikhānasānām vai dharmam śrotum icchāmi aham prabho teṣu vaikhānasāḥ nāma vānaprasthāḥ śubha īkṣaṇe tīvreṇa tapasā yuktāḥ dīptimantaḥ sva tejasā satya vrata para adhīnāḥ teṣām niṣkalmaṣam tapaḥ aśmakuṭṭāḥ tathā anye ca dantolūkhalinaḥ tathā śīrṇa parṇa āśinaḥ ca anye uñchayanti tathā apare kapotavratinaḥ ca anye kāpotīm vṛttim āsthitāḥ paśu pracāra niratāḥ phenapāḥ ca tathā apare mṛga vat mṛga caryāyām saṃcaranti tathā apare abbhakṣāḥ vāyubhakṣāḥ ca nirāhārāḥ tathā eva ca sva vaśāt eva te mṛtyum abhikāṅkṣanti nityaśas indra loke tathā teṣām nirmitāḥ bhoga saṃcayāḥ vara apsarobhiḥ saṃyuktāḥ cira kālam anindite umā maheśvaraḥ bhagavan śrotum icchāmi vālakhilyān tapodhanān dharma caryām tathā devi vālakhilya gatām śṛṇu mṛga nirmoka vasanāḥ nirdvaṃdvāḥ te tapodhanāḥ aṅguṣṭha mātrāḥ suśroṇi sveṣu eva aṅgeṣu saṃyutāḥ te mṛtyu viṣaya atītāḥ tapaḥ siddhāḥ tapaḥ balāḥ jita āhārāḥ jita krodhāḥ sarva bhogeṣu niḥspṛhāḥ dyotayante diśaḥ sarvāḥ dharma jñāḥ satya vādinaḥ teṣu eva nirmalam satyam loka artham tu pratiṣṭhitam kṣamayā ca mahābhāge bhūtānām saṃsthitim viduḥ dharma anveṣī tapaḥ kartum yatate satatam priye amara tvam vaśi tvam ca tapasā prārthayet sadā etat te kathitam sarvam śṛṇvantyāḥ te śrutam priye priya artham ṛṣi saṃghasya lokānām hita kāmyayā nāradaḥ iti bruvantam deveśam ṛṣayaḥ ca api tuṣṭuvuḥ umā bhūyas paramakam yatnam tadā prabhṛti cakrire maheśvaraḥ brāhmaṇāḥ loka saṃsāre sṛṣṭāḥ dhātrā guṇa arthinā lokān tārayitum yuktāḥ martyeṣu kṣitidevatāḥ brāhmaṇeṣu abhayaḥ dharmaḥ paramaḥ śubha lakṣaṇaḥ ime hi dharmāḥ loka artham pūrvam sṛṣṭāḥ svayaṃbhuvā pṛthivyām sat janaiḥ nityam kīrtyamānam nibodha me śūdraḥ dharma paraḥ nityam śuśrūṣā abhimanāḥ bhavet ṣaḍ karmāṇi ca proktāni sṛṣṭāni brahmaṇā purā dharmiṣṭhāni variṣṭhāni yāni tāni śṛṇu uttame yajanam yājanam ca eva dānam pātre pratigrahaḥ adhyāpanam adhyayanam ṣaṣ karmā dharma bhāj ṛjuḥ dānam praśasyate nityam brāhmaṇeṣu tri karmasu dāntaḥ brāhmaṇa satkartā su saṃmṛṣṭa niveśanaḥ pādyam arghyam yathānyāyam āsanam śayanam tathā dīpam pratiśrayam ca eva yaḥ dadāti sa dhārmikaḥ pravṛtti lakṣaṇaḥ dharmaḥ gṛhastheṣu vidhīyate tat aham kīrtayiṣyāmi trivargeṣu ca yat yathā ekena aṃśena kāma artham ekam aṃśam vivardhayet nivṛtti lakṣaṇaḥ puṇyaḥ dharmaḥ mokṣe vidhīyate tasya vṛttim pravakṣyāmi tām śṛṇuṣva samāhitā bubhukṣitam pipāsā ārtam atithim śrāntam āgatam pātram iti eva dātavyam sarvasmai dharma kāṅkṣibhiḥ āgamiṣyati yat pātram tat pātram tārayiṣyati kedārāḥ ca taṭākāni sarāṃsi saritaḥ tathā toya pūrṇāni dṛśyante apratarkyāṇi śobhane pīḍayā api ca bhṛtyānām dānam eva viśiṣyate putra dārāḥ dhanam dhānyam na mṛtān anugacchati śreyaḥ dānam ca bhogaḥ ca dhanam prāpya yaśasvini dānena hi mahābhāgāḥ bhavanti manuja adhipāḥ na asti bhūmi samam dānam na asti dāna samaḥ nidhiḥ na asti satyāt paraḥ dharmaḥ na anṛtāt pātakam param āśrame yaḥ tu tapyeta tapaḥ mūla phala aśanaḥ āditya abhimukhaḥ bhūtvā jaṭā valkala saṃvṛtaḥ maṇḍūkaśāyī hemante grīṣme pañcatapāḥ bhavet samyak tapaḥ caranti iha śraddadhānāḥ vana āśrame gṛha āśramasya te devi kalām na arhanti ṣoḍaśīm umā gṛhāśramasya yā caryā vratāni niyamāḥ ca ye yathā ca devatāḥ pūjyāḥ satatam gṛhamedhinā maheśvaraḥ gṛha āśramasya yat mūlam phalam dharmaḥ ayam uttamaḥ pādaiḥ caturbhiḥ satatam dharmaḥ yatra pratiṣṭhitaḥ sāra bhūtam varārohe dadhnaḥ ghṛtam iva uddhṛtam tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śrūyatām dharma cāriṇi śuśrūṣate yaḥ pitaraḥ mātaram ca gṛha āśrame bhartāram ca eva yāḥ nārī agnihotram ca ye dvijāḥ teṣu teṣu ca prīṇanti devāḥ indra purogamāḥ umā mātā pitṛ viyuktānām kā caryā gṛhamedhinām vidhavānām ca nārīṇām bhavān etat bravītu me maheśvaraḥ devatā atithi śuśrūṣā guru vṛddha abhivādanam ahiṃsā sarva bhūtānām alobhaḥ satyasaṃdhatā brahmacaryam śaraṇya tvam śaucam pūrva abhibhāṣaṇam kṛtajña tvam apaiśunyam satatam dharma śīla tā gavāhnika pradānam ca saṃvibhāgaḥ atithiṣu api dīpam pratiśrayam ca eva dadyāt pādya āsanam tathā pañcame ahani ṣaṣṭhe vā dvādaśe vā api atha aṣṭame caturdaśe pañcadaśe brahmacārī sadā bhavet śmaśrukarma śiraḥ abhyaṅgam añjanam dantadhāvanam na eteṣu ahaḥsu kurvīta teṣu alakṣmīḥ pratiṣṭhitā bharaṇam bhṛtya vargasya dīnānām anukampanam para dāra nivṛttiḥ ca sva dāreṣu ratiḥ sadā śarīram ekam daṃpatyoḥ vidhātrā pūrva nirmitam śīla vṛtta vinītasya nigṛhīta indriyasya ca ārjave vartamānasya sarva bhūta hita eṣiṇaḥ gṛha āśrama pada sthasya kim anyaiḥ kṛtyam āśramaiḥ yathā mātaram āśritya sarve jīvanti jantavaḥ tathā gṛha āśramam prāpya sarve jīvanti ca āśramāḥ rājānaḥ sarva pāṣaṇḍāḥ sarve raṅga upajīvinaḥ vyāla grāhāḥ ca dambhāḥ ca corāḥ rāja bhaṭāḥ tathā sa vidyāḥ sarva śilpa jñāḥ sarve ca eva cikitsakāḥ dūra adhvānam prapannāḥ ca kṣīṇa pathya odanāḥ narāḥ mārjārāḥ mūṣikāḥ śvānaḥ sūkarāḥ ca śukāḥ tathā kapota kāka caṭakāḥ sarīsṛpa niṣeviṇaḥ araṇya vāsinaḥ ca anye saṃghāḥ ye mṛga pakṣiṇām gṛhe kṣetre bile ca eva śataśas atha sahasraśas upayuktam ca yat teṣām matimān na anuśocati āśā pāśa vimokṣaḥ ca viddhi dharmam anuttamam vṛkṣa mūla caraḥ nityam śūnya āgāra niveśanam nadī pulina śāyī ca nadī tīram anuvrajan ātma bhūtaḥ yata āhāraḥ mokṣa dṛṣṭena karmaṇā umā saṃgṛhītam mayā tat ca tava vākyam anuttamam idānīm asti saṃdehaḥ mānuṣeṣu eva kaḥ cana tulya pāṇi śiraḥ kāyaḥ rājā ayam iti dṛśyate pretyabhāve katham lokān labhate puṇya karmaṇām maheśvaraḥ rāja vṛttam aham tasmāt śrotum icchāmi mānada tat aham te pravakṣyāmi rāja dharmam śubha ānane rāja āyattam hi tat sarvam loka vṛttam śubha aśubham tapaḥ dāna mayam rājyam param sthānam vidhīyate tasmāt rājñaḥ sadā martyāḥ praṇamanti yatas tatas nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā asi bhāmini arājake purā tu āsīt prajānām saṃkulam mahat tat dṛṣṭvā saṃkulam brahmā manum rājye nyaveśayat tadā prabhṛti saṃdṛṣṭam rājñām vṛttam śubha aśubham yathā pretya labhet svargam yathā vīryam yaśaḥ tathā pitryam vā bhūta pūrvam vā svayam utpādya vā punar anu bhṛtyān prajāḥ paścāt iti eṣa vinaya kramaḥ svāminam ca upamām kṛtvā prajāḥ tad vṛtta kāṅkṣayā svayam vinaya saṃpannāḥ bhavanti iha śubha īkṣaṇe tasmāt pūrvataram rājā ātmānam vinaye nayet yaḥ vinītaḥ svayam rājā vinayati eva vai prajāḥ apahāsyaḥ bhavet tādṛś sva doṣasya anavekṣaṇāt vidyā abhyāsaiḥ vṛddha yogaiḥ ātmānam vinayam nayet vidyā dharma artha phalinī tad vidaḥ vṛddha saṃjñitāḥ indriyāṇām jayaḥ devi atas ūrdhvam udāhṛtam ajaye su mahān doṣaḥ rājānam vinipātayet pañca eva sva vaśān kṛtvā tad arthān pañca śoṣayet ṣaṭ utsṛjya yathāyogam jñānena vinayena ca śāstra cakṣuḥ naya paraḥ bhūtvā bhṛtyān samāharet vṛtta śruta kula upetān upadhābhiḥ parīkṣitān amātyān upadhā atītān sa apasarpān jita indriyān amātyāḥ buddhi saṃpannāḥ rāṣṭram bahu jana priyam durādharṣam pura śreṣṭham kośaḥ kṛcchra sahaḥ smṛtaḥ etāḥ prakṛtayaḥ ṣaḍ ca svāmī ca naya tattva vid prajānām rakṣaṇa arthāya sarvam etat vinirmitam ābhiḥ karaṇa bhūtābhiḥ kuryāt loka hitam nṛpaḥ ātma rakṣā nṛpa indrasya prajā rakṣā artham iṣyate tasmāt satatam ātmānam saṃrakṣet a pramādavān bhojana ācchādanāt snānāt bahis niṣkramaṇāt api nityam strī gaṇa saṃyogāt rakṣet ātmānam ātmavān satatam putra dārebhyaḥ rakṣet ātmānam ātmavān prajānām rakṣaṇa arthāya prajā hita karaḥ bhavet prajā kāryam tu tat kāryam prajā saukhyam tu tat sukham prakṛtīnām vivāda artham rāga dveṣau vyudasya ca ubhayoḥ pakṣayoḥ vādam śrutvā ca eva yathātatham tam artham vimṛśet buddhyā svayam tattva darśanāt kartāram aparādham ca deśa kālau naya anayau jñātvā samyak yathāśāstram tatas daṇḍam nayet nṛṣu pratyakṣa āpta upadeśābhyām anumānena vā punar boddhavyam satatam rājñā deśa vṛttam śubha aśubham cāraiḥ karma pravṛttyā ca tat vijñāya vicārayan daṇḍyān ca daṇḍayet devi na atra kāryā vicāraṇā pañca apekṣam sadā mantram kuryāt buddhi yutaiḥ naraiḥ rājā rāṣṭra hita apekṣaḥ satya dharmāṇi kārayet sarva udyogam svayam kuryāt durga ādiṣu sadā nṛpaḥ deśa vṛddhi karān bhṛtyān apramādena kārayet deśa kṣaya karān sarvān saṃpriyān api varjayet su mukhaḥ su priyaḥ bhūtvā samyak vṛttam samādiśet adharmyam paruṣam tīkṣṇam vākyam vaktum na ca arhati sva iṅgitam vṛṇuyāt dhairyāt na kuryāt kṣudra saṃvidam para iṅgita jñaḥ loka jñaḥ bhūtvā saṃsargam ācaret amānuṣa bhayebhyaḥ ca svāḥ prajāḥ pālayet nṛpaḥ lubdhāḥ kaṭhorāḥ ca adhyakṣāḥ mānavāḥ dasyu vṛttayaḥ nigrāhyāḥ eva te rājñā saṃgṛhītāḥ yatas tatas teṣām ātma guṇa upetam yauvarājye niyojayet prakṛtīnām yathā na syāt rājya bhraṃśa udbhavam bhayam arājakam kṣaṇam api rāṣṭram na syāt hi śobhane ātmanaḥ anuvidhānāya yauvarājyam sadā iṣyate kula jānām ca vaidyānām śrotriyāṇām tapasvinām anyeṣām vṛtta yuktānām viśeṣam kartum arhati ātma artham rājya tantra artham kośa saṃcayam ārabhet durgāt rāṣṭrāt samudrāt ca vaṇigbhyaḥ puruṣa atyayāt parātma guṇa sārābhyām bhṛtya poṣaṇam ācaret vāhanānām vidhāya agre poṣaṇam yaudha karmasu sa ādaraḥ satatam bhūtvā apekṣā vratam ācaret caturdhā vibhajet kośam dharma bhṛtya ātma kāraṇāt āpad artham ca nīti jñaḥ deśa kāla vaśena tu anāthān vyādhitān vṛddhān sva deśe poṣayet nṛpaḥ saṃdhim ca vigraham ca eva tad viśeṣān tathā aparān yathāvat saṃvimṛśya eva buddhi pūrvam samācaret sarveṣām sa priyaḥ bhūtvā maṇḍalam satatam caret śubheṣu api ca kāryeṣu na ca ekaḥ antar samācaret pareṇa dhārmikān nyāyān na atīyāt dveṣa lobhataḥ ku nṛpaiḥ pīḍitāḥ tasmāt prajāḥ sarvatra pālayet yātrā kālam avekṣya eva paścāt kopaḥ phala udayam tad yuktāḥ ca āpadaḥ ca eva prāk yānāt iti cintayet prāyaśas varjayet yuddham prāṇa rakṣaṇa kāraṇāt kāraṇāt eva yoddhavyam na ātmanaḥ para doṣataḥ su yuddhe prāṇa mokṣaḥ ca tasya dharmāya iṣyate abhiyuktaḥ balavatā kuryāt āpad vidhim nṛpaḥ anunīya tathā sarvān prajānām hita kāraṇāt anya prakṛti yuktānām rājñām vṛtta vicāriṇām anyān ca āpad prapannānām na tān saṃyoktum arhati śubha aśubham yadā devi vṛttam tu ubhayatas smṛtam etat evam samāsena rāja dharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ evam saṃvartamānaḥ tu daṇḍayan bhartsayan prajāḥ svarga loke tadā rājā tridaśaiḥ saha toṣyate evam nyāya anugam vṛttam kathitam te śubha īkṣaṇe rājyam nyāya anugam nāma buddhi śāstra anugam bhavet daivapakṣye sati nṛpe rājyam nyāya anugam bhavet kriyante yatra karmāṇi śāstra yuktim vimṛśya vai rājyam bhoga anugam nāma ayathāvat pradṛśyate yatra bhāgya vaśāt bhṛtyāḥ labhante na viśeṣataḥ yatra daṇḍyāḥ na daṇḍyante pūjyante vā nara adhamāḥ śubha aśubham yadā yatra viparītam pradṛśyate rājñi vāsura pakṣe tu tatra bhāgya anugam bhavet bhāgya anuge tu rājānaḥ vartamānāḥ yathā tathā prāpya a kīrtim anartham ca iha loke śubha īkṣaṇe mokṣaṇam duṣkṛtīnām tu vidyate kāla paryayāt na asti eva mokṣaṇam devi rājñām duṣkṛta kāriṇām etat sarvam samāsena rāja vṛttam śubha aśubham umā kathitam te mahābhāge bhūyas śrotum kim icchasi maheśvaraḥ rahasyam śrūyatām devi mānuṣāṇām sukha āvaham yatra vā tatra vā bījam dharma arthī na utsṛjet punar naraḥ bīja vināśena lipyate bhrūṇa hatyayā ahiṃsā paramaḥ dharmaḥ ahiṃsā paramam sukham ahiṃsā dharma śāstreṣu sarveṣu paramam padam devatā atithi śuśrūṣā satatam dharma śīla tā veda adhyayana yajñāḥ ca tapaḥ dānam damaḥ tathā ācārya guru śuśrūṣā tīrtha abhigamanam tathā ahiṃsāyāḥ varārohe kalām na arhanti ṣoḍaśīm umā yajñeṣu paśubandheṣu hanyante paśavaḥ dvijaiḥ katham ca bhagavan bhūyas hiṃsamānāḥ narādhipāḥ yasya eva go sahasrāṇi viṃśatiḥ sādhikāni tu ahani ahani hanyante dvijānām māṃsa kāraṇāt sa māṃsam tu sa dattvā annam rantidevaḥ narādhipaḥ kim nu dharmam na śṛṇvanti na śraddadhati vā śrutam mṛgayāyai vinirgatya mṛgān ghnanti narādhipāḥ etat sarvam viśeṣeṇa vistareṇa vṛṣadhvaja maheśvaraḥ bahu mānyam idam devi nā asti kaḥ cit ahiṃsakaḥ śrūyatām kāraṇam ca atra yathā anekavidham bhavet dṛśyate ca api loke asmin na kaḥ cit avihiṃsakaḥ dharaṇī saṃśritān jīvān su sūkṣmān ca eva madhyamān saṃcaran caraṇābhyām ca hanti jīvān anekaśas ajñānāt jñānataḥ vā api ye jīvāḥ śayana āsane upāviśan śayānaḥ ca hanti jīvān anekaśas śiraḥ vastreṣu ye jīvāḥ narāṇām sveda saṃbhavāḥ tān ca hiṃsanti satatam daṃśān ca maśakān api jale jīvāḥ tathā ākāśe pṛthivī jīva mālinī evam jīva ākule loke kaḥ asau syāt yaḥ tu ahiṃsakaḥ sthūla madhyama sūkṣmaiḥ ca sveda vāta mahīruhaiḥ dṛśya rūpaiḥ adṛśyaiḥ ca nānā rūpaiḥ ca bhāmini jīvaiḥ tatam idam sarvam ākāśam pṛthivī tathā matsyāḥ matsyān grasanti iha khagān ca eva khagāḥ tathā sarīsṛpaiḥ ca jīvanti kapota ādyāḥ vihaṃgamāḥ bhūcarāḥ khecarāḥ ca anye kravyādāḥ māṃsa gṛddhinaḥ samṛddhāḥ para māṃsaiḥ tu bhakṣeran ye api ca aparaiḥ sattvaiḥ sattvāni jīvanti śataśas atha sahasraśas a pīḍayitvā na eva anyam jīvāḥ jīvanti sundari jīvasya ekasya māṃsena payasā rudhireṇa vā tṛpyante bahavaḥ jīvāḥ kravyādāḥ māṃsa bhojinaḥ ekaḥ jīva sahasrāṇi sadā khādati mānavaḥ annādi alpa upabhogena dhānya saṃjñāni yāni tu māṃsa dhānyaiḥ sa bījaiḥ ca bhojanam parivarjayet tri rātram pañca rātram vā sapta rātram atha api vā dhānyāni yaḥ na hiṃseta ahiṃsakaḥ parikīrtitaḥ na aśnāti yāvataḥ jīvān tāvat puṇyena yujyate tapyamāne śarīre tu śarīram ca indriyāṇi api vaśe tiṣṭhanti suśroṇi nṛpāṇām iva kiṃkarāḥ niruṇaddhi indriyāṇi eva sa sukhī sa vicakṣaṇaḥ etat mūlam ahiṃsāyāḥ upavāsaḥ prakīrtitaḥ āhāram kurute yaḥ tu bhūmim ākramate ca yaḥ sarve te hiṃsakāḥ devi yathā dharmeṣu dṛśyate yathā iha ahiṃsakaḥ devi tattva jñaḥ jñāyate naraḥ tathā te aham pravakṣyāmi śrūyatām dharma cāriṇi phalāni jīrṇa parṇāni bhasma vā yaḥ api bhakṣayet ālekhyam iva niśceṣṭam tam manye aham ahiṃsakam ārambhāḥ hiṃsayā yuktāḥ dhūmena agniḥ iva āvṛtāḥ yaḥ tu sarvam samutsṛjya dīkṣitaḥ niyataḥ śuciḥ kṛtvā maṇḍala maryādām saṃkalpam kurute naraḥ yāvajjīvam an āśitvā kāla ākāṅkṣī dṛḍha vrataḥ anyathā hi na paśyāmi naraḥ yaḥ syāt ahiṃsakaḥ yatas yatas mahābhāge hiṃsā syāt mahatī tatas nivṛttau madhu māṃsābhyām hiṃsā tu alpatarā bhavet manasā vinivṛttānām dharmasya nicayaḥ mahān manaḥ pūrvaṃgamāḥ dharmāḥ adharmāḥ ca na saṃśayaḥ nigṛhīte bhavet svargaḥ visṛṣṭe narakaḥ dhruvaḥ āyuḥ kṣayam gate adya eṣām mṛte tu praharāmi aham vidhinā nihataḥ pūrvam nimittam sa tu ghātakaḥ jīvāḥ purākṛtena eva tiryagyoni sarīsṛpāḥ nānā yoniṣu jāyante sva karma pariveṣṭitāḥ nānāvidha vicitra aṅgāḥ nānā vīrya parākramāḥ nānā bhūmi pradeśeṣu nānā āhārāḥ ca jantavaḥ jāyamānasya jīvasya mṛtyuḥ pūrvam prajāyate sukham vā yadi vā duḥkham yathā pūrvam kṛtam tathā na atikrāntum hi śakyam syāt nideśaḥ pūrva karmaṇaḥ samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu kālaḥ kālam nirīkṣate gata āyuṣaḥ hi ākṣipati jīvam sarvasya dehinaḥ araṇye sva gṛhe vā api rātrau vā ahani vā tathā maheśvaraḥ śrūyatām kāraṇam devi yathā hi duratikramaḥ āyuḥ kṣayeṇa upahatāḥ samāgamya varānane kīṭāḥ pataṃgāḥ bahavaḥ sthūlāḥ sūkṣmāḥ ca madhyamāḥ bahūnām mṛga yūthānām nānā vana niṣeviṇām sūnā artham devi baddhānām kṣīṇa āyuḥ yaḥ nibadhyate yathā pakṣi gaṇāḥ kṣipram vistīrṇa ākāśa gāminaḥ kṣīṇa āyuṣaḥ nibadhyante śaktāḥ api palāyitum yathā vāri carāḥ mīnāḥ bahavaḥ bahu jātayaḥ āyuḥ kṣayeṇa upahatāḥ nipadyante sarīsṛpāḥ kṛṣatām karṣakāṇām ca nā asti buddhiḥ vihiṃsane atha eṣām lāṅgala agra ādyaiḥ hanyante jantavaḥ akṣayāḥ pāda agreṇa eva ca ekena yām hiṃsām kurute naraḥ mātaṃgaḥ api na tām kuryāt krūraḥ janma śataiḥ api kṛṣāmi iti matiḥ tasya na asti cintā vihiṃsane vidhinā vihitaḥ pūrvam paścāt prāṇī vipadyate gata āyuṣā na śakyam hi muhūrtam api jīvitum tathā tābhiḥ ca martavyam bhoktavyāḥ ca eva tāḥ tathā gavām vai hanyamānānām rudhira prabhavā nadī carmaṇvatī iti vikhyātā khura śṛṅga asthi durgamā rudhiram tām nadīm prāpya toyam bhavati śobhane tatra abhiṣekam kurvanti kṛta japyāḥ kṛta āhnikāḥ dvijāḥ deva gaṇāḥ ca api lokapālāḥ maheśvarāḥ vidhinā pūrva dṛṣṭena tat māṃsam upakalpitam sa māṃsam ca sadā hi annam śataśas atha sahasraśas bhuñjānānām dvijātīnām astam eti divākaraḥ paṭhyamāneṣu mantreṣu yathānyāyam yathāvidhi tāḥ ca svargam gatāḥ gāvaḥ rantidevaḥ ca pārthivaḥ bhogānām saṃvibhāgena vastra ābharaṇa bhūṣaṇaiḥ sahāya kāle saṃprāpte saṃgrāme śastram uddharet vyūḍha anīke yathā śastram senayoḥ ubhayoḥ api hasti aśva ratha saṃpūrṇe padāti bala saṃkule śakti tomara kunta asi śūla paṭṭasa dhāribhiḥ kūṭamudgara cāpa iṣu bhusuṇḍhī juṣṭa muṣṭibhiḥ bhindipāla gadā cakra prāsa karpaṇa dhāribhiḥ yuddha śauṇḍaiḥ pragarjadbhiḥ vṛṣeṣu vṛṣabhaiḥ iva śaṅkha duṃdubhi nādena nānā tūrya raveṇa ca haya heṣita śabdena kuñjarāṇām ca bṛṃhitaiḥ yodhānām siṃhanādaiḥ ca ghaṇṭānām śiñjita svanaiḥ diśaḥ ca vidiśaḥ ca eva samantāt badhirīkṛtāḥ grīṣma anteṣu vigarjadbhiḥ nabhasi iva balāhakaiḥ kapilābhiḥ iva ākāśe chādyamāne samantataḥ pravṛtte śastra saṃpāte yodhānām tatra senayoḥ palāśa aśoka puṣpāṇām jaṃgamāḥ iva rāśayaḥ śobhamānāḥ raṇe śūrāḥ āhvayantaḥ parasparam hanyamāneṣu abhighnatsu śūreṣu raṇa saṃkaṭe pṛṣṭham dattvā atha ye tatra nāyakasya nara adhamāḥ te duṣkṛtam prapadyante nāyakasya akhilam narāḥ
