yat ca asti sukṛtam teṣām yujyate tena nāyakaḥ saṃgrāmeṣu na yudhyante bhṛtāḥ ca eva anurūpatas narakam yānti te ghoram bhartṛ piṇḍa apahāriṇaḥ āviṣṭaḥ ca eva sattvena nirghṛṇaḥ jāyate naraḥ yaḥ tu svam nāyakam rakṣan ati ghore raṇa aṅgaṇe tāpayan ari sainyāni siṃhaḥ mṛga gaṇān iva ādityaḥ iva madhyāhne durnirīkṣyaḥ raṇa ajire yajate sa tu pūta ātmā saṃgrāmeṇa mahā kratum varma kṛṣṇājinam tasya daṇḍa kāṣṭham dhanuḥ smṛtam rathaḥ vedī dhvajaḥ yūpaḥ kuśāḥ ca ratha raśmayaḥ mānaḥ darpaḥ tu ahaṃkāraḥ trayaḥ tretāgnayaḥ smṛtāḥ sruc bhāṇḍam ca api yat kim cit yajña upakaraṇāni ca āyudhāni asya tat sarvam samidhaḥ sāyakāḥ smṛtāḥ sveda srāvaḥ ca gātrebhyaḥ kṣaudram tasya yaśasvinaḥ puroḍāśāḥ nṛ śīrṣāṇi rudhiram ca āhutiḥ smṛtam tūṇaḥ ca eva caruḥ jñeyaḥ vasoḥ dhārā vasā smṛtā kravyādāḥ bhūta saṃghāḥ ca tasmin yajñe dvijātayaḥ teṣām bhakṣa anna pānāni hatā nṛ gaja vājinaḥ bhuñjate te yathākāmam yathā yajñe kim icchake nihatānām tu yodhānām vastra ābharaṇa bhūṣaṇam hiraṇyam ca suvarṇam ca yat vai yajñasya dakṣiṇā yathā nadī sahasrāṇi praviṣṭāni mahā udadhim praviṣṭā rāja dharmeṇa ācārya vinayaḥ tathā veda uktāḥ ca eva ye dharmāḥ pāṣaṇḍeṣu ca kīrtitāḥ tathā eva mānavāḥ dharmāḥ dharmāḥ ca anye tathā apare deśa jāti kulānām ca grāma dharmāḥ tathā eva ca ye dharmāḥ pārvatīyeṣu ye dharmāḥ pattana ādiṣu teṣām pūrva pravṛttānām kartavyam parirakṣaṇam prajāḥ pālayate yatra dharmeṇa vasudhādhipaḥ ṣaṭkarma niratāḥ viprāḥ pūjyante pitṛ devatāḥ na eva tasmin anāvṛṣṭiḥ na rogāḥ na api upadravāḥ dharma śīlāḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ svadharma nirate nṛpe eṣṭavyaḥ satatam devi yukta ācāraḥ narādhipaḥ chidra jñaḥ ca eva śatrūṇām apramattaḥ pratāpavān śūdrāḥ pṛthivyām bahavaḥ rājñām bahu vināśakāḥ tasmāt pramādam suśroṇi na kuryāt paṇḍitaḥ nṛpaḥ teṣu mitreṣu tyakteṣu tathā martyeṣu hastiṣu yaḥ tu rāṣṭra hita arthāya go brāhmaṇa kṛte tathā bandi grāhāya mitra arthe prāṇān tyajati dustyajān sarva kāma dughām dhenum dharaṇīm loka dhāriṇīm samudra antām varārohe sa śaila vana kānanām dadyāt devi dvijātibhyaḥ vasu pūrṇām vasuṃdharām na tad samam varārohe prāṇatyāgī viśiṣyate yajñaiḥ tasya kim āścaryam prāṇatyāgaḥ su duṣkaraḥ tasmāt sarveṣu yajñeṣu śastra yajñaḥ viśiṣyate maheśvaraḥ evam saṃgrāma yajñāḥ te yathātattvam udāhṛtāḥ mṛga yātrām tu vakṣyāmi śṛṇu tām dharma cāriṇi mṛgān hatvā mahīpālaḥ yathā pāpaiḥ na lipyate nirmānuṣām imām sarve mṛgāḥ icchanti medinīm bhakṣayanti ca sasyāni śāsitavyāḥ nṛpeṇa te duṣṭānām śāsanam dharmaḥ śiṣṭānām paripālanam kartavyam bhūmipālena nityam kāryeṣu ca ārjavam svargam mṛgāḥ ca gacchanti svayam nṛpatinā hatāḥ yathā gāvaḥ hi a gopālāḥ tathā rāṣṭram anāyakam tasmāt aṃśāḥ tu devānām gandharva uraga rakṣasām rājye niyuktāḥ rāṣṭreṣu prajā pālana kāraṇāt aśiṣṭa śāsane ca eva śiṣṭānām paripālane teṣām caryām pravakṣyāmi śrūyatām anupūrvaśas yathā pracaratām teṣām pārthivānām yaśasvini rāṣṭram dharmaḥ dhanam ca eva yaśaḥ kīrtiḥ ca vardhate nṛpāṇām pūrvam eva ayam dharmaḥ dharma bhṛtām vare sabhā prapāta ṭākāni devatāyatanāni ca brāhmaṇa āvasathāḥ ca eva kartavyāḥ nṛpa sattamaiḥ brāhmaṇāḥ na avamantavyāḥ bhasma channāḥ iva agnayaḥ kulam utsādayeyuḥ te krodha āviṣṭāḥ dvijātayaḥ tathā krodha agninā viprāḥ daheyuḥ pṛthivīm imām na hi vipreṣu kruddheṣu rājyam bhuñjanti bhūmipāḥ paribhūya dvijān mohāt vātāpi nahuṣa ādayaḥ sa bandhu mitrāḥ naṣṭāḥ te dagdhāḥ brāhmaṇa manyubhiḥ śarīram ca api śakrasya kṛtam bhaga nirantaram prasādam kārayām āsuḥ praṇāma stuti vandanaiḥ indrasya vṛṣaṇau bhūtvā kṣipram vai śleṣam āgatau evam vipreṣu kruddheṣu devarājaḥ śatakratuḥ aśaktaḥ śāsitum rājyam kim punar mānuṣaḥ bhuvi krodha āviṣṭaḥ dahet vipraḥ śuṣka indhanam iva analaḥ adevān api devān sa kuryāt devān adevatāḥ tasmāt na utpādayet manyum manyu praharaṇāḥ dvijāḥ mahatsu api aparādheṣu śāsanam na arhati dvijaḥ dṛśyate triṣu lokeṣu brāhmaṇānām anindite krodhāḥ ca vipulāḥ ghorāḥ prasādāḥ ca api anuttamāḥ tasmāt nā utpādayet krodham nityam pūjyāḥ dvijātayaḥ kruddheṣu yaḥ vai vipreṣu rājyam bhuṅkte narādhipaḥ saṃprahāsaḥ ca bhṛtyeṣu na kartavyaḥ narādhipaiḥ laghu tvam ca eva prāpnoti ājñā ca asya nivartate bhṛtyānām saṃprahāsena pārthivaḥ paribhūyate tasmāt bhṛtyeṣu nṛpatiḥ saṃprahāsam vivarjayet sagotreṣu viśeṣeṇa sarva upāyaiḥ na viśvaset viśvāsāt bhayam utpannam hanyāt vṛkṣam iva aśaniḥ pramādāt dhanyate rājā lobhena ca vaśīkṛtaḥ bhaya ārtānām paritrātā dīna anugraha kārakaḥ tasmāt kṛtya viśeṣa jñaḥ nityam rāṣṭra hite rataḥ alubdhaḥ nyāya vādī ca ṣaḍbhāgam ca upajīvati kārya akārya viśeṣa jñaḥ sarvam dharmeṇa paśyati sva rāṣṭreṣu dayām kuryāt akāryam na pravartate aparādha anurūpeṇa duṣṭam daṇḍena śāsayet na adharmaḥ vidyate tatra yatra rājā kṣamā anvitaḥ aśiṣṭa śāsanam dharmaḥ śiṣṭānām paripālanam avadhyāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ gāvaḥ dūtaḥ ca eva pitā tathā vidyām grāhayate yaḥ ca ye ca pūrva upakāriṇaḥ dharaṇīm gām hiraṇyam ca siddha annam ca tilān ghṛtam evam carati yaḥ nityam rājā rāṣṭra hite rataḥ tasya rāṣṭram dhanam dharmaḥ yaśaḥ kīrtiḥ ca vardhate ṣaḍbhāgam upayuñjan yaḥ prajāḥ rājā na rakṣati nirudyogaḥ nṛpaḥ yaḥ ca para rāṣṭra nighātane sva rāṣṭram niṣpratāpasya para cakreṇa hanyate tat pāpam sakalam rājā hatarāṣṭraḥ prapadyate mātulam bhāgineyam vā mātaram śvaśuram gurum svasya rāṣṭrasya rakṣā artham yudhyamānaḥ tu yaḥ hataḥ saṃgrāme para cakreṇa śrūyatām tasya yā gatiḥ śakra lokam itas yāti saṃgrāme nihataḥ nṛpaḥ yāvantaḥ lomakūpāḥ syuḥ tasya gātreṣu sundari tāvat varṣa sahasrāṇi śakra loke mahīyate rājā vā rājamātraḥ vā bhūyas bhavati vīryavān vyavahāraḥ ca cāraḥ ca satatam satya saṃdha tā apramādaḥ pramodaḥ ca vyavasāye api a caṇḍa tā bharaṇam ca eva bhṛtyānām vāhanānām ca poṣaṇam yodhānām ca eva satkāraḥ kṛte karmaṇi amogha tā maheśvaraḥ yūpe nibaddhāḥ mantraiḥ ca yathānyāyam yathāvidhi mantra āhuti vipūtāḥ te svargam yānti yaśasvini tarpitāḥ yajña bhāgeṣu teṣām māṃsaiḥ varānane agnayaḥ tridaśāḥ ca eva lokapālāḥ saha īśvarāḥ teṣu tuṣṭeṣu jāyeta tasya yajñasya yat phalam sa patnīkaḥ sa putraḥ sa sa pitṛ bhrātṛbhiḥ saha umā etat te sarvam ākhyātam bhūyas śrotum kim icchasi bhagavan sarva bhūta īśa śūlapāṇe mahā dyute śrotum icchāmi aham vṛttam sarveṣām gṛhamedhinām kīdṛśam caritam teṣām trivarga sahitam prabho pratyāyatiḥ katham teṣām jīvana artham udāhṛtam vartamānāḥ katham sarve prāpnuvanti uttamām gatim maheśvaraḥ nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā asi bhāmini prāyaśas loka sat vṛttam iṣyate gṛha vāsinām teṣām saṃrakṣaṇa arthāya rājānaḥ saṃsmṛtāḥ bhuvi sarveṣām atha martyānām vṛttim sāmānyatas śṛṇu vidyā vārttā ca sevā ca kāru tvam nāṭya tā tathā iti ete jīvana arthāya martyānām vihitāḥ priye api janma phalam tāvat mānuṣāṇām viśeṣataḥ vihitam tat sva vṛttena tat me nigadataḥ śṛṇu karma kṣetram hi mānuṣyam bhoga duḥkha yutāḥ pare sarveṣām prāṇinām tasmāt mānuṣye vṛttam iṣyate vidyā yogaḥ tu sarveṣām pūrvam eva vidhīyate kārya akāryam vijānāti vidyayā devi nā anyathā vidyayā sphīyate jñānam jñānāt tattva vidarśanam dṛṣṭa tattvaḥ vinīta ātmā sarva arthasya ca bhājanam śakyam vidyā vinītena loke saṃjīvanam sukham ātmānam vidyayā tasmāt pūrvam kṛtvā tu bhājanam bhāvayitvā tathā ātmānam pūjanīyaḥ satām api kula anuvṛttam vṛttam vā pūrvam eva samāśrayet iṣyate gṛha vāsāya dārakarma yathāpuram yadi ced vidyayā ca eva vṛttim kāṅkṣet atha ātmanaḥ rāja vidyām tu vāde api loka vidyām atha api vā tīrthataḥ ca api gṛhṇīyāt śuśrūṣā ādi guṇaiḥ yutaḥ granthataḥ ca arthataḥ ca eva dṛḍhīkuryāt prayatnataḥ evam vidyā phalam devi prāpnuyāt na anyathā naraḥ yadi ced vārttayā vṛttim kāṅkṣeta vidhi pūrvakam kṣetre jala upapanne ca tat yogyām kṛṣim ācaret vāṇijyam vā yathākālam kuryāt tad deśa yogataḥ paśu saṃjīvakaḥ ca eva deśe gāḥ poṣayet dhruvam bahu prakārāḥ bahavaḥ paśavaḥ tasya sādhakāḥ yaḥ kaḥ cit sevayā vṛttim kāṅkṣeta matimān naraḥ yata ātmā śravaṇīyānām bhavet vai saṃprayojakaḥ buddhyā vā karma yogāt vā yodhanāt vā samāśrayet yathā yathā sa tuṣyeta tathā saṃtoṣayeta tam anujīvi guṇa upetaḥ kuryāt ātma artham āśritaḥ vipriyam na ācaret tasya eṣā sevā samāsatas kāru karma ca nāṭyam ca prāyaśas nīca yoniṣu sarveṣām pūrvam ārambhān cintayet naya pūrvakam ātma śaktim upāyān ca deśa kālau ca śaktitaḥ kāraṇāni pravāsam ca prakṣepam ca phala udayam evamādīni saṃcintya dṛṣṭvā daiva anukūla tām vṛttim evam samāsādya tām sadā paripālayet pālayan vardhayan bhuñjan tām prāpya na vināśayet kṣīyate giri saṃkāśam aśnataḥ hi anapekṣayā dharmāya arthāya kāmāya āpad praśamanāya ca caturṣu api vibhāgeṣu vidhānam śṛṇu śobhane yajña artham ca anna dāna artham dīna anugraha kāraṇāt deva brāhmaṇa pūjā artham pitṛ pūjā artham eva ca kula artham saṃnivāsa artham kriyā nityaiḥ ca dhārmikaiḥ evamādiṣu ca anyeṣu dharma artham saṃtyajet dhanam aiśvarya sthāna lābha artham rāja vāllabhya kāraṇāt āvāhe ca vivāhe ca putrāṇām vṛtti kāraṇāt evamādiṣu ca anyeṣu artha artham vibhajet dhanam anubandham phalam hetum dṛṣṭvā vittam parityajet na adhanāḥ prāpnuvanti arthān narāḥ yatna śataiḥ api tasmāt dhanam rakṣitavyam dātavyam ca vidhānataḥ śarīra poṣaṇa arthāya āhārasya viśeṣaṇe naṭa gāndharva saṃyoge kāma yātrā vihārayoḥ manaḥ priyāṇām saṃyoge prīti dāne tathā eva ca evamādiṣu ca anyeṣu kāma artham visṛjet dhanam vicārya guṇa doṣau tu mātrayā tatra saṃtyajet rājya bhraṃśa vināśa artham durbhikṣa artham ca śobhane mahā vyādhi vimokṣa artham vārddhakasya eva kāraṇāt śatrūṇām pratikārāya sāhasaiḥ ca api amarṣaṇāt prasthāne ca anya deśa artham āpadām vipramokṣaṇe sukham arthavatām loke kṛcchrāṇām vipramokṣaṇam aśanāt indriyāṇi iva sarvam arthāt pravartate nidhānam mātrayā kuryāt anyathā vilayam vrajet evam devi manuṣyāṇām loke tu ājīvanam prati evam yuktasya lokasya laukyam vṛttam punar śṛṇu dhanyam yaśasyam āyuṣyam svargīyam paramam vacaḥ trivarge ca yathā yuktam sarveṣām saṃvidhīyate tathā saṃvartamānāḥ tu lokayoḥ hitam āpnuyuḥ kālya utthānam ca śaucam ca deva brāhmaṇa bhakta tā gurūṇām eva śuśrūṣā brāhmaṇeṣu abhivādanam pratyutthānam ca vṛddhānām deva sthāna praṇāmanam ābhimukhyam puraskṛtya atithīnām ca bhojanam vṛddha upadeśa karaṇam śravaṇam hita pathyayoḥ poṣaṇam bhṛtya vargasya sāntva dāna parigrahaiḥ samyak vṛttam sva dāreṣu doṣāṇām pratiṣedhanam putrāṇām vinayam kuryāt tad tad kārye niyojayet varjanam ca aśubha arthānām śubhānām joṣaṇam tathā kula ucitānām dharmāṇām yathāvat paripālanam varjanam kalaha ādīnām bhaktyā saṃśamanam tathā dīnānām saṃgraham ca eva na ānṛśaṃsya vyapekṣayā abaddha anṛta ghorāṇām vacanānām vivarjanam kula saṃdhāraṇam ca eva pauruṣeṇa eva sarvaśas evamādi śubham sarvam tasya vṛttam iti smṛtam vṛddha sevī bhavet nityam hita artham jñāna kāṅkṣayā na yāceta parān dhīraḥ sva bāhu balam āśrayet sva śarīram sadā rakṣet āhāra ācārayoḥ api hitam pathyam sat āhāram jīrṇam bhuñjīta mātrayā devatā atithi satkāram kṛtvā sarvam yathāvidhi śeṣam bhuñjet śuciḥ bhūtvā na ca bhāṣeta vipriyam pratiśrayam ca pānīyam balim bhikṣām ca sarvaśas gṛhasthaḥ pravahet dadyāt gāḥ ca vatsān ca poṣayet bahis niṣkramaṇam ca eva kuryāt kāraṇataḥ divā viṣamāt na avagāheta sva śaktyā samam ācaret yathā āya vyaya tā loke gṛhasthānām prapūjitā bhayāt vā yadi vā lobhāt na kurvīta kadā cana buddhi pūrvam samālokya dūratas guṇa doṣataḥ ātmā sākṣī bhavet nityam ātmanaḥ tu śubha aśubhe umā manasā karmaṇā vācā na ca kāṅkṣeta pātakam ime tu varṇāḥ catvāraḥ vihitāḥ svid svabhāvataḥ uta aho kriyayā varṇāḥ saṃbhavanti maheśvara maheśvaraḥ svabhāvāt eva vidyante catvāraḥ brāhmaṇa ādayaḥ eka jātyāḥ su duṣprāpam anya varṇa tvam āgatam tasmāt te saṃpravakṣyāmi tat sarvam karma pākataḥ brāhmaṇaḥ tu naraḥ bhūtvā sva jātim anupālayet dṛḍham brāhmaṇa karmāṇi veda uktāni samācaret satya ārjava paraḥ bhūtvā dāna yajña paraḥ tathā tasyām jātyām samuditaḥ jāti dharmān na hāpayet evam saṃvartamānaḥ tu kāladharmam gataḥ punar tad kṣaye brāhmaṇaḥ bhūtvā tathā eva nṛṣu jāyate evam sva karmaṇā martyaḥ sva jātim labhate punar avamatya eva tām jātim ajñāna tamasā āvṛtaḥ śūdra vat vicaret loke śūdra karma abhilāṣayā śūdraiḥ saha caran nityam śauca maṅgala varjitaḥ sa ca api kāladharma sthaḥ yama lokeṣu daṇḍitaḥ yadi jāyeta martyeṣu śūdraḥ eva abhijāyate śūdraḥ eva bhavet devi brāhmaṇaḥ api sva karmaṇā tathā eva śūdraḥ tu aparaḥ śūdra karmāṇi varjayan evam yukta samācāraḥ śūdraḥ api maraṇam gataḥ brāhmaṇānām kule mukhye veda svādhyāya saṃyute evam eva sadā loke śūdraḥ brāhmaṇyam āpnuyāt evam kṣatriya vaiśyāḥ ca jāti dharmeṇa saṃyutāḥ sva karmaṇā eva jāyante viśiṣṭeṣu adhameṣu ca evam jāti viparyāsaḥ pretyabhāve bhavet nṛṇām anyathā tu na śakyante loka saṃsthiti kāraṇāt tasmāt jātim viśiṣṭām tu katham cit prāpya paṇḍitaḥ umā maheśvaraḥ śobhana aśobhanam sarvam adhikāra vaśāt svayam śaṭheṣu śaṭha vat kuryāt āryeṣu ṛju vat ācaret āpatsu na avasīdet ca ghorān saṃkrāmayet parān sāmnā eva sarva kāryāṇi kartum pūrvam samārabhet anartha adharma śokān tu yathā na prāpnuyāt svayam etat vṛttam samāsādya gṛham āśritya mānavāḥ nirābādhāḥ nirudvegāḥ prāpnuvanti uttamām gatim etat janma phalam nityam sarveṣām gṛha vāsinām etat sarvam mayā proktam kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi umā mānuṣeṣu eva ye ke cit āḍhyāḥ kleśa vivarjitāḥ bhuñjānāḥ vividhān bhogān dṛśyante nirupadravāḥ apare kleśa saṃyuktāḥ daridrāḥ bhoga varjitāḥ maheśvaraḥ nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā asi bhāmini śṛṇu tat sarvam akhilam mānuṣāṇām hitam vacaḥ ādi sarge purā brahmā sama tvena asṛjat prajāḥ tadā tasmāt samāḥ sarve babhūvuḥ sarvatas narāḥ teṣām hānim tatas dṛṣṭvā tulyānām eva bhāmini brahmāṇam te samājagmuḥ tad kāraṇa nivedakāḥ kartum nā arhasi deveśa pakṣapātam tvam īdṛśam yūyam mā kārṣṭa me roṣam sva kṛtam smarata prajāḥ yuṣmābhiḥ eva yuṣmākam grathitam hi śubha aśubham yādṛśam kurute karma tādṛśam phalam aśnute evam saṃbodhitāḥ tena lokakartrā svayaṃbhuvā evam vijñāta tattvāḥ te dāna dharma parāyaṇāḥ śubhāni vidhivat kṛtvā kāladharma gatāḥ punar apare dharma kāryebhyaḥ nivṛttāḥ tu śubha īkṣaṇe kadaryāḥ niranukrośāḥ prāyeṇa ātma parāyaṇāḥ tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā daridrāḥ kleśa saṃyuktāḥ bhavanti eva na saṃśayaḥ mānuṣeṣu eva ye ke cit dhana dhānya samanvitāḥ maheśvaraḥ tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane phalāni tāni saṃprāpya bhuñjate na kadā cana umā maheśvaraḥ mānuṣāḥ saṃpradṛśyante tat me śaṃsitum arhasi ānṛśaṃsya parāḥ ye tu dharma kāmāḥ ca durgatāḥ para upakāram kurvanti dīna anugraha kāraṇāt pratidadyuḥ para dhanam naṣṭam vā anyaiḥ hṛtam dhanam kāladharma vaśam prāptāḥ punarjanmani te narāḥ dharma dāna upadeśam vā kartavyam iti niścayaḥ umā mānuṣāḥ trividhāḥ deva dṛśyante satatam vibho āsīnāḥ eva bhuñjante sthāna aiśvarya parigrahaiḥ maheśvaraḥ nyāyataḥ tvam mahābhāge śrotu kāmā asi bhāmini ye loke mānuṣāḥ devi dāna dharma parāyaṇāḥ pātrāṇi vidhivat jñātvā dūratas hi anumānataḥ dānāni ca iṅgitaiḥ eva taiḥ a vijñātam eva vā punarjanmani deveśi tādṛśāḥ śobhanāḥ narāḥ āsīnāḥ eva bhuñjante bhogān sukṛta bhoginaḥ yathā yathā te dīna tvāt punar dāsyanti yācitāḥ tāvat kālam tathā devi punarjanmani te narāḥ yatnataḥ śrama saṃyuktāḥ punar tāni āpnuvanti uta umā maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu tat tvam samāhitā dharma kāryam ciram kālam vismṛtya dhana saṃyutāḥ prāṇa anta kāle saṃprāpte vyādhibhiḥ ca nipīḍitāḥ ārabhante punar dharmam dātum dānāni vā narāḥ labhante pūrva dattānām phalāni śubha lakṣaṇe umā maheśvaraḥ vyādhi yoga parikliṣṭāḥ ye nirāśāḥ sva jīvite ārabhante tadā kartum dānāni śubha lakṣaṇe umā rūpa yuktāḥ pradṛśyante śubha aṅgā priya darśanāḥ maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu tat tvam samāhitā ye purā manujāḥ devi lajjā yuktāḥ priyaṃvadāḥ śaktāḥ su madhurāḥ nityam bhūtvā ca eva svabhāvataḥ a māṃsa bhojinaḥ ca eva sadā prāṇi dayā yutāḥ pratikarma pradāḥ vā api vastra dāḥ dharma kāraṇāt bhūmi śuddhi karāḥ vā api kāraṇāt agni pūjakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani te narāḥ umā rūpeṇa spṛhaṇīyāḥ tu bhavanti eva na saṃśayaḥ virūpāḥ ca pradṛśyante mānuṣeṣu eva ke cana maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam rūpa yogāt purā martyāḥ darpa ahaṃkāra saṃyutāḥ virūpa hāsakāḥ ca eva stuti nindā ādibhiḥ bhṛśam para upatāpinaḥ ca eva māṃsa ādāḥ ca tathā eva ca abhyasūyā parāḥ ca eva aśuddhāḥ ca tathā eva ca evam yukta samācārāḥ yama loke su daṇḍitāḥ katham cit prāpya mānuṣyam tatra te rūpa varjitāḥ virūpāḥ saṃbhavanti eva nā asti tatra vicāraṇā umā rūpa bhoga vihīnāḥ ca dṛśyante pramadā priyāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi saumya śīlāḥ priyaṃvadāḥ sva dāraiḥ eva saṃtuṣṭāḥ dāreṣu sama vṛttayaḥ na tu pratyādiśanti eva strī doṣān guhya saṃśritān anna pānīya dāḥ kāle nṛṇām svādu pradāḥ ca ye evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane mānuṣāḥ te bhavanti eva satatam subhagāḥ bhṛśam umā durbhagāḥ saṃpradṛśyante āḍhyāḥ bhoga yutāḥ api maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu sarvam samāhitā ye purā manujāḥ devi sva dāreṣu anapekṣayā yathā iṣṭa vṛttayaḥ ca eva nirlajjāḥ vīta saṃbhramāḥ pareṣām vipriya karāḥ vāc manaḥ kāya karmabhiḥ nirāśrayāḥ nirannādyāḥ strīṇām hṛdaya kopanāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani te narāḥ durbhagāḥ saṃbhavanti eva strīṇām hṛdaya vipriyāḥ umā nā asti teṣām rati sukham sva dāreṣu api kim cana jñāna vijñāna saṃpannāḥ buddhimantaḥ vicakṣaṇāḥ durgatāḥ tu pradṛśyante yatamānāḥ yathāvidhi maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam nirāśrayāḥ nirannādyāḥ bhṛśam ātma parāyaṇāḥ umā mūrkhāḥ loke pradṛśyante dṛḍham mūḍhāḥ vicetasaḥ jñāna vijñāna rahitāḥ samṛddhāḥ ca samantataḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi bāliśāḥ api sarvatas samācaranti dānāni dīna anugraha kāraṇāt paṇḍitaḥ apaṇḍitaḥ vā api bhuṅkte dāna phalam naraḥ umā medhāvinaḥ śruti dharāḥ bhavanti viśada akṣarāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi guru śuśrūṣakāḥ bhṛśam jñāna artham te tu saṃgṛhya tīrthataḥ vidhi pūrvakam a ślāghamānāḥ jñānena praśāntāḥ yata vācakāḥ tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā medhāvinaḥ śruti dharāḥ bhavanti viśada akṣarāḥ bahiṣkṛtāḥ pradṛśyante śruta vijñāna buddhibhiḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi jñāna darpa samanvitāḥ ślāghamānāḥ ca tat prāpya jñāna ahaṃkāra mohitāḥ tapanti ye parān nityam jñāna ādhikyena darpitāḥ jñānāt asūyām kurvanti na sahanti eva ca aparān tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane mānuṣyam su ciram prāpya tatra bodha vivarjitāḥ umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit sarva kalyāṇa saṃyutāḥ vyādhi hīnāḥ nirābādhāḥ rūpa ārogya balaiḥ yutāḥ dhana dhānyena saṃpannāḥ prāsādaiḥ yāna vāhanaiḥ maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu sarvam samāhitā ye purā manujāḥ devi āḍhyāḥ vā itare api vā śruta vṛtta samāyuktāḥ dāna kāmāḥ śruta priyāḥ para iṅgita parāḥ nityam dātavyam iti niścitāḥ satya saṃdhāḥ kṣamā śīlāḥ lobha moha vivarjitāḥ dātāraḥ pātrataḥ dānam vrataiḥ niyama saṃyutāḥ sva duḥkham iva saṃsmṛtya para duḥkham mahātmanaḥ saumya śīlāḥ śubha ācārāḥ deva brāhmaṇa pūjakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane mānuṣeṣu api ye jātāḥ tādṛśāḥ saṃbhavanti te yādṛśāḥ tu tvayā proktāḥ sarve kalyāṇa saṃyutāḥ rūpam dravyam balam ca āyuḥ bhoga aiśvaryam kulam śrutam iti etat sarva sādguṇyam dānāt bhavati na anyathā umā atha ke cit pradṛśyante mānuṣeṣu eva mānuṣāḥ durgatāḥ kleśa bhūyiṣṭhāḥ dāna bhoga vivarjitāḥ bhayaiḥ tribhiḥ sadā juṣṭāḥ vyādhi kṣudh bhaya pīḍitāḥ duṣkalatra abhibhūtāḥ ca satatam vighna darśakāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi āsuram bhāvam āśritāḥ krodha lobha ratāḥ nityam nirannādyāḥ ca niṣkriyāḥ nāstikāḥ ca eva dhūrtāḥ ca mūrkhāḥ ca ātma parāyaṇāḥ para upatāpinaḥ devi prāyaśas prāṇi nirdayāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane katham cit prāpya mānuṣyam tatra te duḥkha pīḍitāḥ śubha aśubham kṛtam karma sukha duḥkha phala udayam umā jāti andhāḥ ca eva dṛśyante jātāḥ vā naṣṭa cakṣuṣaḥ maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam ye purā kāma rāgeṇa para veśmasu lolupāḥ para striyaḥ abhivīkṣante duṣṭena eva sva cakṣuṣā lakṣaṇa jñāḥ ca rūpeṣu ayathāvat pradarśakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ kāladharma gatāḥ tu te daṇḍitāḥ yama daṇḍena niraya sthāḥ ciram priye yadi ced mānuṣam janma labheran tādṛśāḥ narāḥ svabhāvataḥ vā jātāḥ vai andhāḥ eva bhavanti te akṣi roga yutā vā api nā asti tatra vicāraṇā umā mukha roga yutāḥ ke cit kliśyante satatam narāḥ ādi prabhṛti martyāḥ vā jātāḥ vā api atha kāraṇāt maheśvaraḥ hanta te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā kuvaktāraḥ tu ye devi jihvayā kaṭukam bhṛśam asatyam paruṣam ghoram gurūn prati parān prati jihvā bādhām tathā anyeṣām kurvate kopa kāraṇāt prāyaśas anṛta bhūyiṣṭhāḥ narāḥ kārya vaśena vā teṣām jihvā pradeśa sthāḥ vyādhayaḥ saṃbhavanti te ku śrotāraḥ tu ye ca artham pareṣām karṇa nāśakāḥ umā anye mukha āśritāḥ doṣāḥ sarve ca ātma kṛtam phalam pīḍyante satatam deva mānuṣeṣu eva ke cana kukṣi pakṣa āśritaiḥ doṣaiḥ vyādhibhiḥ ca udara āśritaiḥ tīkṣṇa śūlaiḥ ca pīḍyante narāḥ duḥkha pariplutāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi kāma krodha yutāḥ bhṛśam ātma artham eva ca āhāram bhuñjate nirapekṣakāḥ abhakṣya āhāra dāḥ ca eva viśvastānām viṣa pradāḥ abhakṣya bhakṣya dāḥ ca eva śauca maṅgala varjitāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane katham cit prāpya mānuṣyam tatra te vyādhi pīḍitāḥ taiḥ taiḥ bahuvidha ākāraiḥ vyādhibhiḥ duḥkha pīḍitāḥ umā dṛśyante satatam deva vyādhibhiḥ mehana āśritaiḥ pīḍyamānāḥ tathā martyāḥ aśmarī śarkarā ādibhiḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi para dāra pradharṣakāḥ kāma doṣeṇa ye dhūrtāḥ kanyāsu vidhavāsu ca balātkāreṇa gacchanti rūpa darpa dhana anvitāḥ tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ punarjanmani śobhane yadi ced mānuṣam janma labheran te tathāvidhāḥ umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit dṛśyante śoṣiṇaḥ kṛśāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi māṃsa lubdhāḥ su lolupāḥ ātma artham svādu gṛddhāḥ ca para bhoga upatāpinaḥ abhyasūyā parāḥ ca eva para bhogeṣu ye narāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane śoṣa vyādhi yutāḥ tatra narāḥ dhamani saṃtatāḥ umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit kliśyante kuṣṭha rogiṇaḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi pareṣām rūpa nāśakāḥ āghāta vadha bandhaiḥ ca vṛthā daṇḍena mohitāḥ iṣṭa nāśa karāḥ ye tu apathya āhāra dāḥ narāḥ cikitsakāḥ vā duṣṭāḥ ca lobha moha samanvitāḥ nirdayāḥ prāṇi hiṃsāyām mala dāḥ citta nāśakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane yadi vai mānuṣam janma labheran te su duḥkhitāḥ atra te kleśa saṃyuktāḥ kuṣṭha roga samanvitāḥ ke cit tvac doṣa saṃyuktāḥ vraṇa kuṣṭhaiḥ ca saṃyutāḥ śvitra kuṣṭha yutāḥ vā api bahudhā kuṣṭha saṃyutāḥ umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cid aṅga hīnāḥ ca paṅgavaḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi lobha moha samāvṛtāḥ prāṇinām prāṇa hiṃsā artham aṅga hīnam prakurvate śastreṇa utkṛtya vā devi prāṇinām ceṣṭana aśakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā svabhāvataḥ vā jātāḥ vā paṅgavaḥ tu bhavanti te bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cid granthibhiḥ piṭakaiḥ tathā maheśvaraḥ kliśyamānāḥ pradṛśyante tat me śaṃsitum arhasi ye purā manujāḥ devi granthibheda karāḥ nṛṇām muṣṭi prahāra paruṣāḥ nṛśaṃsāḥ pāpa kāriṇaḥ pāṭakāḥ todakāḥ ca eva śūlatunnāḥ tathā eva ca evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā granthibhiḥ piṭakaiḥ ca eva kliśyante bhṛśa duḥkhitāḥ bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit pāda roga samanvitāḥ maheśvaraḥ manujāḥ devatā sthānam sva pādaiḥ bhraṃśayanti uta jānubhiḥ pārṣṇibhiḥ ca eva prāṇi hiṃsām prakurvate evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit dṛśyante bahavaḥ bhuvi rogaiḥ bahuvidhaiḥ deva kliśyamānāḥ su duḥkhitāḥ asamastaiḥ samastaiḥ ca āḍhyāḥ vā durgatāḥ tathā maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam ye purā manujāḥ devi āsuram bhāvam āśritāḥ svavaśāḥ kopana parāḥ guru vidveṣiṇaḥ tathā pareṣām duḥkha janakāḥ manaḥ vāc kāya karmabhiḥ chindan bhindan tudan eva nityam prāṇiṣu nirdayāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane yadi vai mānuṣam janma labheran te tathāvidhāḥ ke cit ślīpada saṃyuktāḥ ke cid kāsa samanvitāḥ jvara atisāra tṛṣṇābhiḥ pīḍyamānāḥ tathā apare vāta gulmaiḥ ca bahubhiḥ śopha udara samanvitāḥ pāda rogaiḥ ca vividhaiḥ vraṇa kuṣṭha bhagaṃdaraiḥ āḍhyāḥ vā durgatāḥ vā api dṛśyante vyādhi pīḍitāḥ umā hrasvāṅgāḥ ca eva vakrāṅgāḥ kubjāḥ vāmanakāḥ tathā apare mānuṣāḥ deva dṛśyante kuṇi bāhavaḥ maheśvaraḥ dhānya mānān vikurvanti kraya vikraya kāraṇāt tulā doṣam tathā devi dhṛta māneṣu nityaśas argha apakaraṇam ca eva sarveṣām kraya vikraye aṅga doṣa karāḥ ye tu pareṣām kopa kāraṇāt māṃsa ādāḥ ca eva ye mūrkhāḥ ayathāvat vṛthā sadā evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane umā hrasva aṅgāḥ vāmanāḥ ca eva kubjāḥ ca eva bhavanti te bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit dṛśyante mānuṣeṣu vai unmattāḥ ca piśācāḥ ca paryaṭantaḥ yatas tatas maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi darpa ahaṃkāra saṃyutāḥ vṛddhān gurūn ca ye mūrkhāḥ vṛthā eva apahasanti ca śauṇḍāḥ vidagdhāḥ śāstreṣu tathā eva anṛta vādinaḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane unmattāḥ ca piśācāḥ ca bhavanti eva na saṃśayaḥ umā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit nirapatyāḥ su duḥkhitāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi sarva prāṇiṣu nirdayāḥ ghnanti bālān ca bhuñjante mṛgāṇām pakṣiṇām api guru vidveṣiṇaḥ ca eva para putra abhyasūyakāḥ pitṛ pūjām na kurvanti yathā uktam ca aṣṭaka ādibhiḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane mānuṣyam su cirāt prāpya nirapatyāḥ bhavanti te umā putra śoka yutā vā api nā asti tatra vicāraṇā bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit pradṛśyante su duḥkhitāḥ udvega vāsa niratāḥ sa udvegāḥ ca yatas tatas nitya śoka samāviṣṭāḥ durgatāḥ ca tathā eva ca maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ nityam utkocana parāyaṇāḥ ṛṇaiḥ vṛddhim ca ye kṛtvā daridrebhyaḥ yatheṣṭataḥ ṛṇa artham abhigacchanti satatam vṛddhi rūpakāḥ udvijante hi tān dṛṣṭvā dhārakāḥ tu atha kāraṇāt ye śvabhiḥ krīḍamānāḥ ca trāsayanti vane mṛgān prāṇi hiṃsām tathā devi kurvanti ca yatas tatas yeṣām gṛheṣu vai śvānaḥ trāsayanti vṛthā narān evam yukta samācārāḥ kāladharma gatāḥ punar pīḍitāḥ yama daṇḍena niraya sthāḥ ciram priye katham cit prāpya mānuṣyam tatra te duḥkha saṃyutāḥ ku deśe duḥkha bhūyiṣṭhe vyāghāta śata saṃkule umā jāyante tatra śocantaḥ sa udvegāḥ ca yatas tatas bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit aiśvarya sthāna saṃyutāḥ mleccha bhūmiṣu dṛśyante mleccha aiśvarya samanvitāḥ maheśvaraḥ apātrebhyaḥ ca ye dānam śauca maṅgala varjitāḥ dadati eva ca ye mūrkhāḥ ślāghayā avajñayā api vā evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane ku deśe mleccha bhūyiṣṭhe durgame vana saṃkaṭe umā klībāḥ napuṃsakāḥ ca eva dṛśyante paṇḍakāḥ tathā nīca karma ratāḥ nīcāḥ nīca saukhyāḥ tathā bhuvi maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam ye purā manujāḥ bhūtvā ghora karma ratāḥ tathā puṃstva upaghātinaḥ ca eva narāṇām kopa kāraṇāt ye dhūrtāḥ strīṣu gacchanti ayathāvat yatheṣṭataḥ kāma vighna karāḥ ye tu dveṣa paiśunya kāraṇāt evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane daṇḍitāḥ yama daṇḍena niraya sthāḥ ciram priye yadi ced mānuṣam janma labheran te tathāvidhāḥ klībāḥ varṣavarāḥ ca eva paṇḍakāḥ ca bhavanti te nīca karma ratāḥ loke nirlajjāḥ vīta saṃbhramāḥ strīṇām api tathā devi yathā puṃsām tu karma jam umā bhagavan devadeveśa śūlapāṇe vṛṣadhvaja puṃścalyaḥ iti yāḥ strīṣu nīca vṛtta ratāḥ smṛtāḥ maheśvaraḥ yāḥ purā pramadāḥ devi buddhi moha samanvitāḥ kāma rāga samāyuktāḥ patīn apacaranti vai pratikūla parāḥ yāḥ tu patīn prati yathā tathā śaucam lajjām tu vismṛtya yatheṣṭa paricārakāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ yama loke su daṇḍitāḥ yadi ced mānuṣam janma labheran tāḥ tathāvidhāḥ bahu sādhāraṇāḥ evam puṃścalyaḥ ca bhavanti tāḥ pauṃścalyam yat tu tat vṛttam strīṇām kaṣṭatamam smṛtam tatas prabhṛti tāḥ devi patanti eva na saṃśayaḥ umā śocanti ced tu tat vṛttam manasā hitam āpnuyuḥ dṛśyante mānuṣe loke sarva kalyāṇa varjitāḥ maheśvaraḥ kuṭumbam tatra vai patyuḥ nāśayanti vṛthā tathā viṣa dāḥ ca agni dāḥ ca eva patīn prati su nirdayāḥ anyāsām hi patīn yānti sva pati dveṣa kāraṇāt evam yukta samācārāḥ yama loke su daṇḍitāḥ niraya sthāḥ ciram kālam katham cit prāpya mānuṣam umā liṅginyaḥ saṃpradṛśyante pāṣaṇḍam dharmam āśritāḥ maheśvaraḥ yāḥ purā bhāva doṣeṇa lobha moha samanvitāḥ para dravya parāḥ lobhāt pareṣām dravya hārakāḥ abhyasūyā parāḥ yāḥ tu sapatnīnām pradūṣakāḥ īrṣyā parāḥ kopanāḥ ca bandhūnām viphalāḥ sadā evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani tāḥ priye apalakṣaṇa saṃpannāḥ pāṣaṇḍam dharmam āśritāḥ umā striyaḥ pravrāja śīlāḥ ca bhavanti eva na saṃśayaḥ bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cid kāru vṛttim samāśritāḥ pradṛśyante manuṣyeṣu nīca karma ratāḥ janāḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi stabdhāḥ māna yutāḥ bhṛśam darpa ahaṃkāra saṃyuktāḥ kevala ātma parāyaṇāḥ tādṛśāḥ manujāḥ devi punarjanmani śobhane kāravaḥ naṭa gandharvāḥ saṃbhavanti yathā tathā nāpitāḥ bandinaḥ ca eva tathā vaitālikāḥ priye evaṃbhūtāḥ tu adhas vṛttim jīvanti āśritya mānavāḥ para prasādana karāḥ te paraiḥ kṛta vetanāḥ umā dāsa bhūtāḥ pradṛśyante sarva karmakarāḥ bhṛśam āghāta bhartsana sahāḥ pīḍyamānāḥ ca sarvaśas maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam ye purā manujāḥ devi pareṣām vitta hārakāḥ ṛṇa vṛddhi karam kuryāt nyāsadattam tathā eva ca nikṣepa kāraṇāt dattam pareṣām dravya hāriṇaḥ pramādāt vismṛtam naṣṭam pareṣām dhana hārakāḥ tādṛśāḥ maraṇam prāptāḥ daṇḍitāḥ yama śāsanaiḥ dāsa bhūtāḥ bhavanti eva janma prabhṛti mānavāḥ teṣām karmāṇi kurvanti yeṣām te dhana hārakāḥ ā samāpteḥ sva pāpasya kurvanti iti viniścayaḥ paśu bhūtāḥ tathā ca anye bhavanti dhana hārakāḥ tat tathā kṣīyate karma teṣām pūrva aparādha jam kim tu mokṣa vidhiḥ teṣām sarvathā tad prasādanam mokṣa kāmī yathānyāyam kurvan karmāṇi sarvaśas bhartuḥ prasādam ākāṅkṣet āghātān sarvadā sahan prīti pūrvam tu yaḥ bhartrā muktaḥ muktaḥ sva pāpataḥ tathābhūtān karmakarān sadā saṃpoṣayet patiḥ yathārham kārayet karma daṇḍam kāraṇataḥ kṣipet vṛddhān bālān tathā kṣīṇān pālayan dharmam āpnuyāt umā bhagavat mānuṣeṣu eva mānuṣāḥ sama darśanāḥ caṇḍālāḥ iva dṛśyante sparśa mātreṇa dūṣakāḥ nīca karmakarāḥ deva sarveṣām mala hārakāḥ durgatāḥ kleśa bhūyiṣṭhāḥ virūpāḥ duṣṭa cetasaḥ maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi tvam ekāgra manāḥ śṛṇu ye purā manujāḥ ghorāḥ atimāna yutāḥ bhṛśam ātma saṃbhāvanā yuktāḥ stabdhāḥ darpa samanvitāḥ praṇāmam na tu kurvanti gurūṇām api pāpakāḥ ye svadharma arpaṇam kāryam atimānāt na kurvate parān saṃnāmayanti eva ājñayā ātmani ye balāt pānapāḥ sarva bhakṣāḥ ca paruṣāḥ kaṭukāḥ narāḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ daṇḍitāḥ yama śāsanaiḥ katham cit prāpya mānuṣyam caṇḍālāḥ saṃbhavanti te nīca karma ratāḥ ca eva sarveṣām mala hārakāḥ pareṣām vandana parāḥ te bhavanti avamānitāḥ virūpāḥ pāpa yoni sthāḥ sparśanāt api varjitāḥ kuvṛttim upajīvanti bhūtvā te rajaka ādayaḥ purā ati māna doṣāt tu bhuñjate sva kṛtam phalam tān api avasthā kṛpaṇān caṇḍālān api buddhimān umā caṇḍālāḥ api tām jātim śocantaḥ śuddhim āpnuyuḥ bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit āśā pāśa śataiḥ vṛtāḥ pareṣām dvāri tiṣṭhanti pratiṣiddhāḥ praveśane draṣṭum jñāpayitum ca eva na labhante ca yatnataḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi aiśvarya sthāna saṃyutāḥ dvārāṇi na dadati eva dveṣa lobha ādibhiḥ vṛtāḥ avasthā moha saṃyuktāḥ sva artha mātra parāyaṇāḥ lubdhāḥ bhoga yutāḥ vā api sarveṣām niṣphalāḥ bhṛśam api śaktāḥ na kuryuḥ ye para anugraha kāraṇāt evam yukta samācārāḥ punarjanmani śobhane yadi ced mānuṣam janma labheran te tathāvidhāḥ durgatāḥ duravasthāḥ ca karma vyākṣepa saṃyutāḥ rājñām vā rājamātrāṇām dvāri tiṣṭhanti vāritāḥ umā praveṣṭum api te dvāri bahis tiṣṭhanti kāṅkṣayā bhagavat mānuṣe loke mānuṣeṣu bahuṣu api sahasā naṣṭa sarvasvāḥ bhraṣṭa kośa parigrahāḥ dṛśyante mānuṣāḥ ke cid rāja cora udaka ādibhiḥ maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi āsuram bhāvam āśritāḥ pareṣām vṛtti nāśam tu kurvate dveṣa lobhataḥ utkocana parāḥ ca eva piśunāḥ ca tathāvidhāḥ para dravya harāḥ ghorāḥ cauryāt vā anyena karmaṇā nirdayāḥ niranukrośāḥ pareṣām vṛtti nāśakāḥ nāstika anṛta bhūyiṣṭhāḥ para dravya apahāriṇaḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ daṇḍitāḥ yama śāsanaiḥ niraya sthāḥ ciram kālam tatra duḥkha samanvitāḥ yadi ced mānuṣam janma labheran te tathāvidhāḥ kaṣṭam ca prāpnuvanti eva kāraṇa akāraṇāt api umā nāśam vināśam dravyāṇām upaghātān ca sarvaśas bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cid bāndhavaiḥ sahasā pṛthak kāraṇa akāraṇāt eva sahasā prāṇa nāśanam śastreṇa vā anyathā vā api prāpnuvanti vadham narāḥ maheśvaraḥ āsurāḥ prāyaśas mūrkhāḥ prāṇi hiṃsā priyāḥ bhṛśam nirdayāḥ prāṇi hiṃsāyām tathā prāṇa vighātakāḥ viśvasta ghātakāḥ ca eva tathā supta vighātakāḥ prāyaśas anṛta bhūyiṣṭhāḥ nāstikāḥ māṃsa bhojinaḥ evam yukta samācārāḥ prāṇi dharma gatāḥ punar daṇḍitāḥ yama daṇḍena niraya sthāḥ ciram priye yadi ced mānuṣam janma labheran te tathāvidhāḥ āḍhyāḥ vā durgatāḥ vā api bhuñjate sva kṛtam phalam suptāḥ mattāḥ ca viśvastāḥ tathā te prāpnuvanti uta prāṇa ābādha kṛtam duḥkham bāndhavaiḥ sahasā pṛthak umā putra dāra vināśam vā śastreṇa anyena vā vadham bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cid rājabhiḥ nīti kovidaiḥ daṇḍyante mānuṣe loke mānuṣāḥ satatam bhuvi maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi manujān ca itarān ca vā kliṣṭa ghātena nighnanti prāṇān prāṇiṣu nirdayāḥ asurāḥ ghora karmāṇaḥ krūra daṇḍa vadha priyāḥ ye daṇḍayanti a daṇḍyān tu rājānaḥ kopa mohitāḥ hiṃsā vihārāḥ puruṣāḥ māṃsa ādāḥ nāstikāḥ śubhe ke cit strī puruṣa ghnāḥ ca guru ghnāḥ ca tathā priye evam yukta samācārāḥ prāṇi dharma gatāḥ punar daṇḍitāḥ yama daṇḍena niraya sthāḥ ciram priye pūrva deha kṛtam karma bhuñjate tat iha prajāḥ eṣā vyavasthitiḥ devi mānuṣeṣu eva dṛśyate na ṛṣīṇām na devānām amara tvāt tapaḥ balāt umā na tathā mānuṣāṇām syāt antardhāya bhavet hi tam kimartham mānuṣāḥ loke daṇḍyante pṛthivīśvaraiḥ kṛta aparādham uddiśya hantā hartā ayam iti uta putra arthī putra kāma iṣṭyā iha eva labhate sutān tena eva hi śarīreṇa bhuñjānāḥ karmaṇām phalam maheśvaraḥ etat me saṃśaya sthānam tat tvam śaṃsitum arhasi sthāne saṃśayitam devi tat tvam śṛṇu samāhitā teṣām tad pūrvakam karma daṇḍyate yatra rājabhiḥ daihikam duṣkṛtam teṣām hetuḥ bhavati śāsane aparādha apadeśena rājā daṇḍayati prajāḥ ihaloke vyavasthā artham rājabhiḥ daṇḍanam smṛtam udvejana artham śeṣāṇām aparādham tam uddiśan purā kṛta phalam daṇḍaḥ daṇḍyamānasya tat dhruvam umā prāk eva ca mayā proktam tatra niḥsaṃśayā bhava bhagavan bhuvi martyānām daṇḍitānām nareśvaraiḥ daṇḍena eva tu tena iha pāpa nāśaḥ bhavet na vā maheśvaraḥ etat mayā saṃśayitam tat bhavān chettum arhati sthāne saṃśayitam devi śṛṇu tat tvam samāhitā ye nṛpaiḥ daṇḍitāḥ bhūmau aparādha apadeśataḥ a daṇḍitāḥ vā ye mithyā martyāḥ vā daṇḍitāḥ bhuvi tān yamaḥ daṇḍayati eva sa hi veda kṛta akṛtam na atikramet yamam kaḥ cit karma kṛtvā iha mānuṣaḥ rājā yamaḥ ca kurvāte daṇḍa mātram tu śobhane ubhābhyām yama rājabhyām daṇḍitaḥ a daṇḍitaḥ api vā paścāt karma phalam bhuṅkte narake mānuṣeṣu vā umā kimartham duṣkṛtam kṛtvā mānuṣāḥ bhuvi nityaśas punar tad karma nāśāya prāyaścittāni kurvate prāyaścittāni ca anyāni pāpa nāśāya kurvate maheśvaraḥ tasmāt me saṃśayaḥ jātaḥ tvam tam chettum iha arhasi sthāne saṃśayitam devi śṛṇu tat tvam samāhitā saṃśayaḥ hi mahān eṣa pūrveṣām ca manīṣiṇām abhisaṃdhāya vā nityam anyathā vā yadṛcchayā abhisaṃdhi kṛtasya iha nā eva nāśaḥ asti karmaṇaḥ anyathā yat kṛtam pāpam pramādāt vā yadṛcchayā prāyaścitta aśvamedhābhyām śodhanāt tu praṇaśyati loka saṃvyavahāra artham prāyaścitta ādiḥ iṣyate umā bhagavan devadeveśa mānuṣāḥ ca itarāḥ api mriyante bahudhā loke kāraṇa akāraṇāt api maheśvaraḥ ye purā manujāḥ devi kāraṇa akāraṇāt api yathā asubhiḥ viyojyante prāṇinaḥ prāṇi nirdayaiḥ viṣadaḥ tu viṣeṇa eva śastraiḥ śastreṇa ghātakaḥ evam eva yathā loke mānuṣāḥ ghnanti mānuṣān kāraṇaiḥ eva te tena tathā sva prāṇa nāśanam bhūyas tava samāsena kathayiṣyāmi tat śṛṇu yena eva kāraṇena api karma yat tu śubha aśubham prāpnuvanti narāḥ pretya niḥsaṃdeham śubha aśubham na śakyam karma ca ā bhoktum sa deva asura mānuṣaiḥ umā kathitam śrotu kāmāyāḥ kim bhūyas śrotum icchasi prasādāt te mahādeva śrutā me karmaṇām gatiḥ saṃgṛhītam ca tat sarvam tattvataḥ amṛta saṃnibham kartāram sva kṛtam karma nityam tam anudhāvati kṛtasya karmaṇaḥ ca iha na nāśaḥ asti iti niścayaḥ karmaṇām gatim āśritya saṃśayān moktum arhasi maheśvaraḥ umā tat sarvam nirviśaṅkā tvam pṛccha mām śubha lakṣaṇe kṛtvā tat puruṣaḥ karma śubham vā yadi vā itarat karmaṇaḥ sva kṛtasya iha kadā bhuṅkte phalam punar maheśvaraḥ sthāne saṃśayitam devi tat hi guhyatamam śṛṇu tvad priya artham pravakṣyāmi deva guhyam śubha ānane pūrva deha kṛtam karma bhuñjate tat iha prajāḥ iha eva yat kṛtam puṃsām tat paratra phaliṣyati eṣā vyavasthitiḥ devi mānuṣeṣu eva dṛśyate devānām asurāṇām ca amara tvāt tapaḥ balāt ekena eva śarīreṇa bhujyate karmaṇām phalam umā bhagavan bhaga netra ghna mānuṣāṇām viceṣṭitam loke graha kṛtam sarvam matvā karma śubha aśubham sadā eva graha nakṣatram prāyaśas paryupāsate maheśvaraḥ sthāne saṃśayitam devi śṛṇu tattva viniścayam nakṣatrāṇi grahāḥ ca eva śubha aśubha nivedakāḥ mānavānām mahābhāge na tu karmakarāḥ svayam prajānām tu hita arthāya śubha aśubha vidhim prati graha sāmye śubham kuryāt jātyām jātyām purā kṛtam kevalam graha nakṣatram na karoti śubha aśubham umā iti te kathitam sarvam viśaṅkām jahi śobhane bhagavan vividham karma kṛtvā jantuḥ śubha aśubham kim tayoḥ pūrvakataram bhuṅkte janmāntare punar maheśvaraḥ sthāne saṃśayitam devi tat te vakṣyāmi tattvataḥ bhuñjānāḥ ca eva dṛśyante kramaśas bhuvi mānavāḥ ṛddhim hānim sukham duḥkham abhayam bhayam eva ca duḥkhāni anubhavanti āḍhyāḥ daridrāḥ ca sukhāni ca yaugapadyāt ca bhuñjānāḥ dṛśyante loka sākṣikam śubha aśubhānām ādhikyāt karmaṇā tatra sevyate tatra api su mahat kṛtvā pūrvam alpam punar śubhe umā etat te sarvam ākhyātam bhūyas śrotum kim icchasi jātāḥ jātāḥ na tiṣṭhanti tat me śaṃsitum arhasi maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tattvam samāhitā ātma karma kṣayāt deham yathā muñcanti tat śṛṇu śarīra ātma samāhāraḥ jantuḥ iti abhidhīyate tat tu kāla vaśāt loke jīrṇam bhavati śobhane kālaḥ chinatti ca āyūṃṣi dina māsa ṛtu hāyanaiḥ kālaḥ pacati bhūtāni kālena ākulitam jagat kālam na atikrameran hi sa deva asura mānavāḥ yathā ākāśe na tiṣṭheta dravyam kim cit acetanam tathā dhāvati kālaḥ ayam kṣaṇam kim cit na tiṣṭhati evam kālena saṃkrāntam śarīram jarjarīkṛtam akarma yogyam saṃśīrṇam tyaktvā dehī tatas vrajet nityasya anitya saṃtyāgāt loke tat maraṇam viduḥ sa punar jāyate anyatra śarīram navam āviśan umā bhagavan prāṇinām bālāḥ dṛśyante maraṇam gatāḥ ati vṛddhāḥ ca jīvantaḥ dṛśyante cira jīvinaḥ kevalam kāla maraṇam na pramāṇam maheśvara maheśvaraḥ tasmāt me saṃśayam brūhi prāṇinām jīva kāraṇam śṛṇu tad kāraṇam devi nirṇayaḥ tu ekaḥ eva saḥ jīrṇa tva mātram kurute kālaḥ deham na pātayet jīrṇe karmaṇi saṃghātaḥ svayam eva viśīryate pūrva karma pramāṇena jīvitam mṛtyuḥ eva vā yāvat pūrva kṛtam karma tāvat jīvati mānavaḥ tadvat kāla vaśāt bālāḥ mriyante bhoga saṃkṣayāt jīvanti cira vṛddhāḥ ca tathā karma pramāṇataḥ umā maheśvaraḥ alpa āyuṣaḥ narāḥ kena tat me śaṃsitum arhasi śṛṇu tat sarvam akhilam guhyam pathyataram nṛṇām ye narāḥ vṛtta saṃpannāḥ bhavanti cira jīvinaḥ ahiṃsā satya vacanam akrodhaḥ kṣāntiḥ ārjavam gurūṇām nitya śuśrūṣā vṛddhānām api pūjanam śaucam ca akārya saṃtyāgaḥ sadā pathyasya bhojanam evamādi śubham vṛttam narāṇām dīrgha jīvinām tapasā brahmacaryeṇa rasāyana niṣevaṇāt svarge vā mānuṣe vā api ciram tiṣṭhanti dhārmikāḥ apare pāpa karmāṇaḥ prāyaśas anṛta vādinaḥ hiṃsā priyāḥ guru dviṣṭāḥ niṣkriyāḥ śauca varjitāḥ nāstikāḥ ghora karmāṇaḥ satatam māṃsa pānapāḥ pāpa ācārāḥ guru dveṣāḥ kopanāḥ kalaha priyāḥ evam eva aśubha ācārāḥ tiṣṭhanti niraye ciram tasmāt alpa āyuṣaḥ martyāḥ tādṛśāḥ saṃbhavanti te agamya deśa gamanāt apathyānām ca bhojanāt āyuḥ kṣayaḥ bhavet nṝṇām āyuḥ kṣaya karāḥ hi te umā ātmanaḥ jāti saṃbandham brūhi strī puruṣa antare strī prāṇaḥ puruṣa prāṇaḥ ekaḥ sa pṛthak eva vā maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu sarvam samāhitā puṃs tvam strī tvam iti prāṇe sthitiḥ nā asti śubha īkṣaṇe nirvikārī sadā eva ātmā strī tvam puṃs tvam na ca ātmani karma prakāreṇa tathā jātyām jātyām prajāyate akartā ca avikārī ca nityaḥ ca puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ strī śarīra kṛtam karma strī bhāvena upabhujyate puruṣeṇa kṛtam karma puruṣeṇa eva bhujyate śarīram karma nānātvāt dṛśyate viparīta vat kṛtvā tu pauruṣam karma strī pumān api jāyate umā strī bhāva yuj pumān kṛtvā karmaṇā pramadā bhavet maheśvaraḥ śṛṇu bhāmini kartāram ātmā hi na ca karma kṛt śarīram prāṇinām loke yathā pitta kapha anilaiḥ sattvam rajaḥ tamaḥ iti guṇāḥ tu ete śarīriṇaḥ prakāśa ātmakam eteṣām sattvam satatam iṣyate satyam prāṇi dayā śaucam śreyaḥ prītiḥ kṣamā damaḥ anṛtam ca eva pāruṣyam śruti vidveṣa tā bhṛśam hiṃsā para tvam nāstikyam nidrā ālasya bhayāni ca tasmāt guṇa mayaḥ sarvaḥ kārya ārambhaḥ śubha aśubhaḥ sāttvikāḥ puṇya lokeṣu rājasāḥ mānuṣe pade umā tiryagyonau ca narake tiṣṭheyuḥ tāmasāḥ narāḥ maheśvaraḥ svayam praṇaśyate dehī tat me śaṃsitum arhasi tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kalyāṇi kāraṇam karma kṣaye tu saṃprāpte prāṇinām dehadhāriṇām tad nimittam śarīrī tu śarīram prāpta saṃkṣayam umā yathāyogam phalam jantuḥ prāpnoti iti viniścayaḥ pareṣām vipriyam kurvan yathā saṃprāpnuyāt śubham maheśvaraḥ tat api asti mahābhāge abhisaṃdhi phalam nṛṇām hita artham duḥkham anyeṣām kṛtvā aśubham avāpnuyāt daṇḍayan bhartsayan rājā prajāḥ puṇyam avāpnuyāt guruḥ saṃtarjayan śiṣyān bhartā bhṛtya janam svakam unmārga pratipannān ca śāstā dharma phalam labhet yajña artham paśu hiṃsām ca kurvan api na hiṃsakaḥ umā etat kṛtvā tu pāpiṣṭhān doṣān na prāpnuyāt kva cit bhagavan sarva martyānām jñānī bhavati sattamaḥ nāradaḥ kṛtrimam tat svabhāvam vā tat me śaṃsitum arhasi maheśvaraḥ catasraḥ jātayaḥ tatra prajānām kramaśas yathā teṣām udbheda jāḥ vṛkṣāḥ latāḥ vallyaḥ ca vīrudhaḥ daṃśa yūka ādayaḥ ca anye sveda jāḥ kṛmi jātayaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ chidra karṇāḥ ca prāṇinaḥ tu aṇḍa jāḥ smṛtāḥ mṛga vyāla manuṣyān ca viddhi teṣām jarāyu jān evam caturvidhām jātim ātmā saṃśritya tiṣṭhati sparśena eka indriyeṇa ātmā tiṣṭhati udbheda jeṣu vai śarīra sparśa rūpābhyām kleda jeṣu api tiṣṭhati pañcabhiḥ ca indriya dvāraiḥ jīvanti aṇḍa jarāyu jāḥ śīta uṣṇayoḥ tu saṃyogāt jāyante kleda jāḥ priye aṇḍa jāḥ ca api saṃyogāt jāyante kṣetra bījayoḥ śukla śoṇita saṃyogāt saṃbhavanti jarāyu jāḥ jarāyu jānām sarveṣām mānuṣam padam uttamam bhayam ratiḥ ca cittam tat vidyate mṛga pakṣiṣu dharma adharmau ca vijñānam na eva pakṣi mṛga ādiṣu buddhiḥ sattvam dhṛtiḥ lajjā jñānam vijñānam eva ca ūha apohau ca martyeṣu guṇāḥ nānāvidhāḥ smṛtāḥ mānuṣam hi padam tasmāt viśiṣṭam iha lakṣyate mahatā dharma kāryeṇa prāpyate janma mānuṣam atas param tama utpattim śṛṇu devi samāhitā dvividham hi tamaḥ loke śārvaram deha jam tathā jyotirbhiḥ ca agninā loke nāśam gacchati śārvaram tamasaḥ tasya nāśa artham na upāyam adhijagmivān tapaḥ cakāra vipulam loka kartā pitāmahaḥ caturaḥ tu samudbhūtāḥ vedāḥ sa aṅgāḥ saha uttarāḥ tān labdhvā mumude brahmā lokānām hita kāraṇāt kārya akāryam idam ca iti vācya avācyam idam tu iti paśubhiḥ nirviśeṣam tu ceṣṭante mānuṣāḥ tu iti yajña ādīnām samārambhaḥ śrutena eva vidhīyate prīti yuktāḥ punar devāḥ mānuṣāṇām phalanti uta jñānāt viśiṣṭam jantūnām nā asti lokatraye api ca sahajam tat pradhānam syāt aparam kṛtrimam śrutam saṃpragṛhya śrutam sarvam kṛtakṛtyāḥ bhavanti uta upari iva ca martyānām deva vat saṃprakāśate kāmam krodham bhayam darpam ajñānam ca eva buddhi jam tat śrutam nudati kṣipram yathā vāyuḥ balāhakān alpa mātram kṛtaḥ dharmaḥ bhavet jñānavatām mahān parāvara jñaḥ bhūtānām jñānavān tattva vid bhavet umā evam śruta phalam sarvam kathitam te śubha īkṣaṇe bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit jāti smaraṇa saṃyutāḥ maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi kāraṇam śṛṇu śobhane ye mṛtāḥ sahasā martyāḥ jāyante sahasā punar teṣām paurāṇikaḥ abhyāsaḥ kaṃ cit kālam sa tiṣṭhati tasmāt jātismarāḥ loke jāyante bodha saṃyutāḥ teṣām vivardhatām saṃjñā svapna vat sā praṇaśyati umā para lokasya ca astitve mūḍhānām kāraṇam ca tat bhagavat mānuṣāḥ ke cit mṛtāḥ bhūtvā hi saṃprati nivartamānāḥ dṛśyante deheṣu eva punar narāḥ maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi kāraṇam śṛṇu śobhane prāṇaiḥ viyojyamānānām bahu tvāt prāṇinām kṣaye tathā eva nāma sāmānyāt yamadūtāḥ nṛṇām bhuvi vahanti te kva cit mohāt anyān te iti yāmikāḥ nirvikāram hi tat sarvam yamaḥ veda kṛta akṛtam tasmāt saṃyaminīm prāpya yamena ete vimokṣitāḥ umā bhagavan supta mātrāṇām martyānām svapna darśanam maheśvaraḥ suptānām tu punar ceṣṭā svapnaḥ iti abhidhīyate anāgatam atikrāntam paśyate saṃcarat manaḥ nimittam ca bhavet tasmāt prāṇinām svapna darśanam umā evam te kathitam sarvam bhūyas śrotum kim icchasi apare ceṣṭayā ca iti dṛṣṭvā pratyakṣataḥ kriyām tat tvam vada mahādeva śrotum kautūhalam hi me maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tat tvam samāhitā lakṣyate dvividham karma mānuṣāṇām śubha ānane purākṛtam tayoḥ ekam aihikam ca param tathā aihikam dṛṣṭa karaṇam tat mānuṣam iti smṛtam mānuṣam ca kriyā mātram daivāt saṃbhavate phalam evam tat ubhayam sarvam mānuṣam vidyate nṛṣu kṛṣau tu dṛśyate karma karṣaṇam vapanam tathā ropaṇam ca eva lavanam yat ca anyat pauruṣam smṛtam kāle vṛṣṭiḥ suvāpaḥ ca prarohaḥ paktiḥ eva ca daivāt asiddhiḥ ca bhavet duṣkṛtam ca asti pauruṣe umā katham ātmā sadā garbham saṃviśet karma kāraṇāt maheśvaraḥ śṛṇu bhāmini tat guhyam guhyānām paramam priye adṛśyaḥ hi sa devānām śaighryāt saukṣmyāt nirāśrayāt atimātra ati māyānām ātma māyā sadā iṣyate saḥ ayam caturvidhām jātim saṃviśati ātma māyayā maithunam śoṇitam bījam daivam ca api atra kāraṇam bīja śoṇita saṃyogaḥ yadā saṃbhavate śubhe tad ātmā viśate garbham evam aṇḍa jarāyu je śīta uṣṇayoḥ tu saṃyoge kleda jeṣu avatiṣṭhati bīja bhūmi jalānām tu yogāt udbheda jeṣu ca evam saṃyoga kāle tu ātmā garbha tvam eyivān kalalāt jāyate piṇḍam piṇḍāt peśī arbudam bhavet evam strī puruṣa saṃyogam pauruṣam viddhi bhāmini pañcabhūta sthitiḥ ca eva jyotiṣām ayanam tathā tādṛśam kāraṇam daivam śubham vā yadi vā itarat yādṛśam ca ātmanā śakyam tat pauruṣam iti smṛtam tayoḥ samāhitam karma śīta uṣṇam yugapad tathā pauruṣam tu tayoḥ pūrvam ārabdhavyam vijānatā ātmanā nā eva śakyam hi na tathā kīrtim āpnuyāt khananāt mathanāt loke jala agni prāpaṇam yathā tathā puruṣakāre tu daiva saṃpad samāhitā asurāḥ rākṣasāḥ ca eva manyante loka nāyakāḥ na paśyante ca te pāpāḥ kevalam māṃsa bhakṣakāḥ pracchāditam hi tat sarvam guhya kāmāḥ hi devatāḥ ādi kāle prajāḥ sarvāḥ kṛtvā karma śubha aśubham bhuñjate paśyamānāḥ te vṛttāntam lokayoḥ dvayoḥ yathā eva ātma kṛtam vidyuḥ deśa antara gatāḥ narāḥ acireṇa eva kālena svargaḥ saṃpūritaḥ tadā devānām api saṃbādham dṛṣṭvā brahmā api acintayat saṃcarante katham devāḥ mānuṣāḥ praviśanti hi kāma krodhau ca tad kāle mānuṣeṣu nyapātayat purākṛtasya avijñānāt kāma krodha abhipīḍanāt na etat asti iti manvānāḥ vikārān cakrire punar akārya ādīn mahā doṣān ārabhanta ātma kāraṇāt vismṛtya dharma kāryāṇi para loka bhayam tathā lokānām ca eva vedānām kṣayāya eva tadā priye narakāḥ pūritāḥ ca āsan prāṇibhiḥ pāpa kāribhiḥ punar eva tu tat dṛṣṭvā lokakartā pitāmahaḥ acintayat tam eva artham lokānām hita kāraṇāt cintayitvā tadā brahmā jñānena tapasā priye umā akarot jñāna dṛśyam tu para lokam na cakṣuṣā bhagavat mṛta mātraḥ tu yaḥ ayam jātaḥ iti smṛtaḥ garbha ādau eva saṃviṣṭaḥ ātmā iti bhagavat matam maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tat tvam samāhitā sūtikāyāḥ hita artham ca mohana artham ca dehinām māyā sā yāmikā nāma yat janma maraṇa antare umā svabhāvāt jāyate sarvam yathā vṛkṣa phalam tathā tapaḥ dāna ādi yat karma tatra ātmā dṛśyate vṛthā parokṣa vacanam śrutvā na pratyakṣasya darśanāt tat sarvam na asti vā asti iti saṃśaya sthāḥ tathā apare nāradaḥ praśnam etam tu pṛcchantyām rudrāṇyām pariṣad tadā maheśvaraḥ kautūhala yutā śrotum samāhita manāḥ bhavat na etat asti mahābhāge yat vadanti iha nāstikāḥ etat eva abhiśastānām śruti vidveṣiṇām matam sarvam artham śrutam dṛṣṭam yat prāk uktam mayā tava tadā prabhṛti martyānām śrutam āśritya paṇḍitāḥ kāmān saṃchindya parighān dhṛtyā vai parama asinā abhiyānti eva te svargam paśyantaḥ karmaṇām phalam buddhiḥ śraddhā ca śuśrūṣā kāraṇāni hita eṣiṇām anye karaṇa hīna tvāt nāstikyam bhāvam āśritāḥ śruta vidveṣiṇaḥ mūrkhāḥ nāstikāḥ dṛḍha niścayāḥ niṣkriyāḥ ca nirannādyāḥ patanti eva adhamām gatim eṣā brahma kṛtā māyā durvijñeyā sura asuraiḥ kim punar mānuṣaiḥ loke jñātu kāmaiḥ sva karmabhiḥ kevalam śraddhayā devi śruti mātra niviṣṭayā badhira andha vat eva atra vartitavyam hita eṣiṇā umā kīdṛśāḥ yamadaṇḍāḥ te kīdṛśāḥ paricārakāḥ katham mṛtāḥ te gacchanti prāṇinaḥ yama sādanam kīdṛśam bhavanam tasya katham daṇḍayati prajāḥ maheśvaraḥ śṛṇu kalyāṇi tat sarvam yat tat devi manaḥ priyam dakṣiṇasyām diśi śubhe yamasya sadanam mahat vicitram ramaṇīyam ca nānā bhāva samanvitam pitṛbhiḥ preta saṃghaiḥ ca yamadūtaiḥ ca saṃtatam prāṇi saṃghaiḥ su bahubhiḥ karma vaśyaiḥ tu pūritam māyayā satatam vetti prāṇinām yat śubha aśubham māyayā saṃharan tatra prāṇi saṃghān yatas tatas tasya māyā mayāḥ pāśāḥ na vedyante sura asuraiḥ evam hi śāsataḥ tasya yamasya paricārakāḥ gṛhītvā saṃnayanti eva prāṇinaḥ kṣīṇa karmiṇaḥ karmaṇā prāṇinaḥ loke uttama adhama madhyamāḥ yathārham tān samādāya nayanti yama sādanam dhārmikān uttamān viddhi svargīyāḥ te yathā amarāḥ nṛṣu janma labhante ye karmaṇā madhyamāḥ smṛtāḥ tiryak naraka gantāraḥ hi adhamāḥ te nara adhamāḥ panthānaḥ trividhā dṛṣṭā sarveṣām gati jīvinām ramaṇīyam nirābādham durdarśam iti nāmataḥ ramaṇīyam tu sat mārgam patākā dhvaja saṃkulam dhūpitam sikta saṃmṛṣṭam puṣpa mālā abhisaṃkulam nirābādham yathā loke su praśastam tathā bhavet tṛtīyam tat tu durdarśam durgandhi tamasā āvṛtam paruṣam śarkarā ākīrṇam śva daṃṣṭrā bahulam bhṛśam kṛmi kīṭa samākīrṇam gacchatām ati durgamam saṃnayanti yathā kāle tat me śṛṇu śuci smite uttamān antakāle tu yama dūtāḥ su saṃyatāḥ umā bhagavan tatra ca ātmānam tyakta deham nirāśrayam maheśvaraḥ adṛśyam katham ādāya saṃnayanti yama antikam śṛṇu bhāmini tat sarvam trividham dehadhāraṇam mānuṣam karma vaśyam syāt svargīyam bhoga sādhanam tṛtīyam yātanā vaśyam śarīram māyayā kṛtam śarīraiḥ yātanā vaśyaiḥ jīvam āmucya bhāmini nayanti yāmikāḥ tatra prāṇinaḥ māyayā mṛtān caṇḍāla veṣāḥ tu adhamān gṛhītvā bhartsya tarjanaiḥ ākarṣantaḥ tathā pāśaiḥ durdarśena nayanti tān dharma āsana gatam dakṣam bhrājamānam sva tejasā darśayanti mahābhāge yāmikāḥ tān nivedya te pūjayan daṇḍayan kān cit teṣām śṛṇvan śubha aśubham vyāpṛtaḥ bahu sāhasraiḥ tatra āste satatam yamaḥ gatānām tu yamaḥ teṣām uttamān abhipūjayā abhisaṃgamya vidhivat pṛṣṭvā svāgata kauśalam prastutya sat kathām teṣām lokam saṃdiśate yamaḥ yamena evam anujñātāḥ yānti paścāt triviṣṭapam madhyamānām yamaḥ teṣām śrutvā karma yathātatham jāyantām mānuṣeṣu eva iti saṃdiśate ca tān adhamān pāpa saṃyuktān yamaḥ na avekṣate āgatān yamasya puruṣāḥ ghorāḥ caṇḍāla sama darśanāḥ yātanāḥ yāpayanti etān lokapālasya śāsanāt krośataḥ pātayanti etān mithas garteṣu avāṅmukhān saṃghātinyaḥ śilāḥ ca eṣām patanti śirasi priye ayaḥ mukhāḥ kāka balāḥ bhakṣayanti su dāruṇāḥ tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭrāḥ tathā śvānaḥ kān cit tatra daśanti vai tatra vaitaraṇī nāma nadī grāha samākulā duṣpraveśā ca ghorā ca mūtra śoṇita vāhinī tasyām saṃmajjayanti etān tṛṣitān pāyayanti tān āropayanti vai kān cit tatra kaṇṭaka śalmalīm aṅgāreṣu ca dahyante tathā duṣkṛta kāriṇaḥ pāṭyante taru vat śastraiḥ pīḍyante locana ādiṣu bhidyante atha aṅgaśas śūlaiḥ tudyante sūkṣma sūcibhiḥ evam tvayā kṛtaḥ doṣaḥ tad artham daṇḍanam tu idam vācā evam ghoṣayanti sma daṇḍyamānān samantataḥ evam te yātanāḥ prāpya śarīraiḥ yātanā vaśaiḥ evam bahuvidhāḥ daṇḍāḥ bhujyante pāpa kāribhiḥ yātanāḥ ca tathā bhuktvā pacyante narake punar pāpa doṣa kṣaya karā yātanā saṃsmṛtā nṛṇām umā maheśvaraḥ kati te nirayāḥ ghorāḥ kīdṛśāḥ te maheśvara śṛṇu bhāmini tat sarvam pañca ete naraka āhvayāḥ bhūmeḥ adhastāt vihitā ghorā duṣkṛta karmaṇām tāvat pramāṇam vistīrṇam tāmasam pāpa pīḍitam bhṛśam durgandhi paruṣam kṛmibhiḥ dāruṇaiḥ yutam kṛmibhiḥ bhakṣyamāṇāḥ ca visra gandha samāyutāḥ evaṃpramāṇam udvignāḥ yānti tiṣṭhanti tatra te yātanābhyaḥ daśaguṇam narake duḥkham iṣyate bhramanti duḥkha mokṣa artham trātā kaḥ cit na vidyate duḥkhasya antara mātram tu dvāram vā na ca labhyate mahāraurava saṃjñam tu dvitīyam narakam priye tṛtīyam narakam tatra kaṇṭakāvana saṃjñitam tatas dvi guṇitam tam ca pūrvābhyām duḥkha mānayoḥ mahāpātaka saṃyuktāḥ ghorāḥ tasmin viśanti hi etat dvi guṇitam tasmāt yathā aniṣṭa sukham tathā bhuñjate tatra tatra eva duḥkham duṣkṛta kāriṇaḥ pañcakaṣṭam iti khyātam pañcamam narakam priye bhuñjate tatra tatra eva duḥkham duṣkṛta kāriṇaḥ amānuṣa arham tat duḥkham mahābhūtaiḥ tu bhujyate ati ghoram ciram kṛtvā mahābhūtāni yānti tam pañca kaṣṭena hi samam nā asti duḥkham tathāvidham duḥkhasya antam iti prāhuḥ pañca kaṣṭam sadā priye evam te teṣu tiṣṭhanti prāṇinaḥ duḥkha bhāginaḥ krośantaḥ ca rudantaḥ ca vedanā ārtāḥ bhṛśa āturāḥ ādhāvante nivāryante śūla hastaiḥ yatas tatas rujā arditāḥ tṛṣā yuktāḥ prāṇinaḥ pāpa kāriṇaḥ kṛmibhiḥ bhakṣyamāṇāḥ ca vedanā ārtāḥ tṛṣā anvitāḥ saṃsmarantaḥ svakam karma kṛtam ātma aparādha jam śocantaḥ tatra tiṣṭhanti yāvat pāpa kṣayam priye evam bhuktvā tu narakam mucyante pāpa saṃkṣayāt umā maheśvaraḥ śatam sahasram varṣāṇām ādim kṛtvā hi jantavaḥ umā tiṣṭhanti naraka āvāsāḥ pralaya antam iti sthitiḥ maheśvaraḥ raurave śata sāhasram varṣāṇām iti saṃsthitiḥ mānuṣa ghnāḥ kṛtaghnāḥ ca tathā eva anṛta vādinaḥ jāra corāḥ ca nārī ca sarvatas ca apacāriṇī utkocakāḥ ca piśunāḥ varṇa saṃbheda kārakāḥ tādṛśāḥ paripacyante raurave narake narāḥ deśa ghnāḥ ca eva rāja ghnāḥ pitṛ mātṛ vighātinaḥ rājā ca anyāyataḥ śāstā sākṣi tve anṛta vādinaḥ bahu ghnāḥ ca kula ghnāḥ ca bhrūṇa ghnāḥ guru ghātinaḥ viśvasta ghātakāḥ ca evam ātma ghnāḥ kopa kāraṇāt dvitīye dviguṇam kālam pacyante tādṛśāḥ narāḥ mahāpātaka yuktāḥ tu tṛtīye duḥkham āpnuyuḥ etāvat mānuṣa saham param anyeṣu lakṣyate yakṣāḥ vidyādharāḥ ca eva kādraveyāḥ ca kiṃnarāḥ gandharvāḥ bhūta saṃghāḥ ca teṣām pāpa yutāḥ bhṛśam sahantaḥ tādṛśam ghoram pañca kaṣṭe ca yādṛśam tādṛgvidhasya duḥkhasya ayogyān viddhi mānuṣān evam te narakān bhuktvā tatra kṣapita kalmaṣāḥ narakebhyaḥ vimuktāḥ ca jāyante kṛmi jātiṣu udbheda jeṣu vā ke cid tatra api kṣīṇa kalmaṣāḥ umā mṛga pakṣiṣu tat bhuktvā labhante mānuṣam padam nānā jātiṣu kena eva jāyante pāpa kāriṇaḥ maheśvaraḥ sarvathā ātmā karma vaśāt nānā jātiṣu dṛśyate yaḥ ca māṃsa priyaḥ nityam kaṅka gṛdhrān sa saṃspṛśet abhakṣya bhakṣaṇaḥ martyaḥ kāka jātiṣu saṃspṛśet ātma ghnaḥ yaḥ naraḥ kopāt preta jātiṣu tiṣṭhati paiśunyāt parivādāt ca kukkuṭa tvam samaśnuyāt nāstikaḥ ca eva yaḥ mūrkhaḥ mṛga jātim sa gacchati hiṃsā vihāraḥ tu naraḥ kṛmi kīṭeṣu jāyate asatyam paruṣam vākyam yaḥ vadet matsya tām vrajet agamyā gamanāt ca eva para dāra abhimarśanāt mūṣika tvam vrajet martyaḥ nā asti tatra vicāraṇā kṛtaghnaḥ mitra ghātī ca sṛgāla vṛka jātiṣu guru ghnaḥ pitṛ ghātī ca sthāvareṣu avatiṣṭhati vācikaiḥ pakṣi mṛga tām mānasaiḥ anya jāti tām śarīra jaiḥ karma doṣaiḥ yāti sthāvara tām naraḥ evamādi aśubham kṛtvā narāḥ nirayam āśritāḥ tān tān bhāvān prapadyante sva kṛtasya eva kāraṇāt evam jātiṣu nirdagdhāḥ prāṇinaḥ pāpa kāriṇaḥ bahu kṛtvas ati saṃtaptam loham śucitamam yathā bahu duḥkha abhisaṃtaptaḥ tathā ātmā śudhyate malāt umā bhagavan devadeveśa śūlapāṇe vṛṣadhvaja śrutam me paramam guhyam prasādāt te vara prada śrotum bhūyas aham icchāmi prajānām hita kāraṇāt śubha aśubham iti proktam karma sarvam samāsatas tat me vistaraśaḥ brūhi śubha aśubha vidhim prati aśubham kīdṛśam karma prāṇinaḥ adhas nipātayet maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi tat sarvam śṛṇu śobhane sukṛtam duṣkṛtam ca iti dvividhaḥ karma vistaraḥ manaḥ pūrvam tu vāc karma vartate vāc mayam tatas jāyate vai kriyā yogam evam ceṣṭā kramam priye abhidrohaḥ abhyasūyā ca para arthe cakṣuṣā spṛhā śubha aśubhānām martyānām vartanam viparītataḥ dharma kārye yat aśraddhā pāpa karmaṇi harṣaṇam asatyam parivādaḥ ca pāpam etat tu vāc mayam cauryam pareṣām dravyāṇām haraṇam nāśanam tathā abhakṣya bhakṣaṇam ca eva vyasaneṣu abhiṣaṅga tā darpāt stambha abhimānāt ca pareṣām upatāpanam akāryāṇām ca karaṇam aśaucam pānam eva ca dauḥśīlyam kṣudra saṃparkaḥ sāhāyyam pāpa karmaṇi adharmyam ayaśasyam ca kāryam tasya niṣevaṇam evamādi aśubham ca anyat śarīram pāpam ucyate mānasāt vāc mayam pāpam viśiṣṭam iti vakṣyate vāc mayāt api vai pāpam śārīram gaṇyate bahu dvividham tat kṛtam pāpam kartāram narakam nayet umā maheśvaraḥ yaḥ naraḥ anaparādhī san ātma prāṇasya rakṣaṇāt śatrum udyata śastram vā pūrvam tena hataḥ api vā cora āṭavika saṃtrastāḥ tad pratīkāra ceṣṭayā grāma artham bhartṛ piṇḍa artham dīna anugraha kāraṇāt akāryam vā api abhakṣyam vā kṛtvā pāpaiḥ na lipyate anṛtam dharma yuktam ca dākṣiṇyāt strīṣu bhāṣitam ātma prāṇa bhayāt uktam mokṣa artham ca api adhārmikāt vidhiḥ eṣa gṛhasthānām prāyeṇa eva upadiśyate avācyam vā api akāryam vā deśa kāla vaśena tu umā evam devi vijānīhi na asti tatra vicāraṇā bhagavan pāna doṣān ca peya apeya tva kāraṇam maheśvaraḥ śucayaḥ subhaga ācārāḥ sarve sumanasaḥ priye preṣya abhāvāt manuṣyāṇām karma ārambhaḥ nanāśa ha ubhayoḥ lokayoḥ nāśam dṛṣṭvā karma kṣayāt prabhuḥ ājñāpayat surān devi mohayasva iti mānuṣān tamasaḥ sāram uddhṛtya pānam buddhi praṇāśanam nyapātayat manuṣyeṣu pāpa doṣa āvaham priye tadā prabhṛti tat pītvā mumuhuḥ mānuṣāḥ bhuvi kārya akāryam a jānantaḥ vācya avācyam guṇa aguṇam nṛtyanti muditāḥ ke cid gāyanti ca śubha aśubham nagnāḥ vikṣipya gātrāṇi naṣṭa saṃjñāḥ mṛtāḥ iva evam bahuvidhān pāpān kurvanti hṛta cetasaḥ ye pibanti mahā moham pānam pāpa yutāḥ narāḥ dhṛtim lajjām ca buddhim ca pānam pītam praṇāśayet tasmāt narāḥ saṃbhavanti nirlajjāḥ nirapatrapāḥ buddhi sattvaiḥ parikṣīṇāḥ tejaḥ hīnāḥ mala anvitāḥ pītvā pītvā tṛṣā yuktāḥ pānapāḥ saṃbhavanti ca pāna kāmāḥ pāna kathāḥ pāna kāla abhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ pāna artham karma vaśyāḥ te saṃbhavanti nara adhamāḥ pāna kāmāḥ tṛṣā yogāt buddhi sattva parikṣayāt pānapānām preṣya karāḥ pānapāḥ tu abhavan bhṛśam tadā prabhṛti vai loke nīcaiḥ pāna vaśaiḥ naraiḥ kārayanti ca karmāṇi buddhimantaḥ tu apānapāḥ kāru tvam atha dāsa tvam preṣya tām etya pānapāḥ pānapāḥ tu mada andha tvāt tadā buddhi praṇāśanāt kārya akāryasya ca ajñānāt yathā iṣṭa karaṇāt svayam viśiṣṭaiḥ jñātibhiḥ dveṣyaḥ satatam kali bhāvanaḥ kaṭukam paruṣam ghoram vākyam vadati sarvatas saṃvidam kurute śauṇḍaiḥ na śṛṇoti hitam kva cit evam bahuvidhāḥ doṣāḥ pānape santi śobhane kevalam narakam yānti na asti tatra vicāraṇā tasmāt tat varjitam sadbhiḥ pānam ātma hita eṣibhiḥ yadi pānam na varjeran santaḥ cāritra kāraṇāt maheśvaraḥ vidhānam su kṛtasya api bhūyas śṛṇu śuci smite tathā eva sa upakaraṇam tāvatā sukṛtam viduḥ manaḥ vāc kāya jāḥ doṣāḥ śṛṇu me varjanāt śubham tathā dharmam avāpnoti doṣa tyāgaḥ hi duṣkaraḥ doṣa sākalya saṃtyāgāt muniḥ bhavati mānavaḥ ātmanā sthāpanā mātrāt labhyate sukṛtam param aho nṛśaṃsāḥ pacyante mānuṣāḥ su alpabuddhayaḥ ye tādṛśam na budhyante ātma adhīnam ca nirvyayam duṣkṛta tyāga mātreṇa padam ūrdhvam hi labhyate pāpa bhīru tva mātreṇa doṣāṇām parivarjanāt śrutāt ca vṛddha saṃyogāt indriyāṇām ca nigrahāt saṃtoṣāt ca dhṛteḥ ca eva śakyate doṣa varjanam īśvarāṇām prabhavatām daridrāṇām ca vai nṛṇām saphalaḥ doṣa saṃtyāgaḥ dānāt api śubhāt api su kṛtam yāmikam proktam pathyam nirupasādhanam sukha abhisaṃdhiḥ lokānām satyam śaucam atha ārjavam vrata upavāsaḥ prītiḥ ca brahmacaryam damaḥ śamaḥ evamādi śubham karma su kṛtam niyama āśritam śṛṇu teṣām viśeṣān ca kīrtayiṣyāmi bhāmini satyam svargasya sopānam pārāvārasya nauḥ iva na asti satyāt param dānam na asti satyāt param tapaḥ yathā śrutam yathā dṛṣṭam ātmanā yat yathā kṛtam tathā tasya avikāreṇa vacanam satya lakṣaṇam yat chalena abhisaṃyuktam satya rūpam mṛṣā eva tat nityam eva pravaktavyam pārāvaryam vijānatā dīrgha āyuḥ ca bhavet satyāt kula saṃtāna pālakaḥ umā maheśvaraḥ katham saṃdhārayan martyaḥ vratam śubham avāpnuyāt pūrvam uktam tu yat pāpam manaḥ vāc kāya karmabhiḥ maṅgalam śubha karmāṇi vratena eva samācaret naraḥ dharmam avāpnoti śubha saṃjoṣaṇam vratam tyājyam vā yadi vā joṣyam avratena vṛthā caran śuddha kāyaḥ naraḥ bhūtvā snātvā tīrthe yathāvidhi pañca bhūtāni candra arkau saṃdhye dharmam yamam pitṝn vratamāḥ maraṇāt vā api kāla chedena vā caret śāka ādiṣu vratam kuryāt tathā puṣpa phala ādiṣu evam anyeṣu bahuṣu vratam kāryam hita eṣiṇā vrata bhaṅgaḥ yathā na syāt rakṣitavyam tathā budhaiḥ auṣadha artham yat ajñānāt gurūṇām vacanāt api anugraha artham bandhūnām vrata bhaṅgaḥ na duṣyate vrata apavarga kāleṣu deva brāhmaṇa pūjanam umā nareṇa tu yathāśakti kāryam siddhim tathā āpnuyāt maheśvaraḥ mānasam su kṛtam yat tat śaucam ābhyantaram smṛtam sat āhāra viśuddhiḥ ca kāya prakṣālanam ca yat bāhyam śaucam bhavet etat tathā eva ācamana ādi ca mṛd ca eva śuddha deśa sthā go śakṛt mūtram eva ca dravyāṇi gandha yuktāni yāni puṣṭi karāṇi ca etaiḥ saṃmārjayet kāyam ambhasā ca punar punar prāyaśas tādṛśe majjet anyathā ca api varjayet tris ācamanakam śreṣṭham niṣphenaiḥ nirmalaiḥ jalaiḥ tathā viṣ mūtrayoḥ śuddhiḥ adbhiḥ bahu mṛdā bhavet tadā eva jala saṃśuddhiḥ yat saṃśuddham tu saṃspṛśet śakṛtā bhūmi śuddhiḥ syāt lohānām bhasmanā smṛtā takṣaṇam gharṣaṇam ca eva dāravāṇām viśodhanam dahanam mṛd mayānām ca martyānām kṛcchra dhāraṇam śeṣāṇām devi sarveṣām ātapena jalena ca brāhmaṇānām ca vākyena sadā saṃśodhanam bhavet aduṣṭam adbhiḥ nirṇiktam yat ca vācā praśasyate evam āpadi saṃśuddhiḥ evam śaucam vidhīyate umā maheśvaraḥ kṛmi kīṭa malaiḥ hīnam saṃbhṛtam śuddha bhājane evaṃvidham sat āhāram deva brāhmaṇa satkṛtam grāmyāt āraṇyakaiḥ siddham śuddham iti avadhāraya yajña śeṣam haviḥ śeṣam pitṛ śeṣam ca nirmalam umā maheśvaraḥ māṃsasya bhakṣaṇe doṣaḥ yaḥ ca asya a bhakṣaṇe guṇaḥ iṣṭam dattam adhītam ca kratavaḥ ca sa dakṣiṇāḥ a māṃsa bhakṣaṇasya ete kalām na arhanti ṣoḍaśīm ātma artham yaḥ para prāṇān hiṃsyāt svādu phala īpsayā vyāla gṛdhra sṛgālaiḥ ca rākṣasaiḥ ca samaḥ tu saḥ yaḥ vṛthā nitya māṃsa āśī sa pumān adhamaḥ bhavet tatas kaṣṭataram na asti svayam āhṛtya bhakṣaṇāt yaḥ tu sarvāṇi māṃsāni yāvajjīvam na bhakṣayet sa svarge vipulam sthānam labhate na atra saṃśayaḥ yaḥ tu varṣa śatam pūrṇam tapyate paramam tapaḥ yaḥ ca api varjayet māṃsam samam etat na vā samam tasmāt prāṇi dayā kāryā yathā ātmani tathā pare sarve yajñāḥ na tat kuryuḥ sarve vedāḥ ca bhāmini yat māṃsa rasam āsvādya punar māṃsāni varjayet iti evam munayaḥ prāhuḥ māṃsasya a bhakṣaṇe guṇān evam bahuguṇam devi nṛṇām māṃsa vivarjanam na śaknuyāt yadā jīvan tyaktum māṃsam katham cana tripuṇya māsa mātram vā varjanīyam viśeṣataḥ varjanīyam tathā māṃsam paratra hitam icchatā aśaktaḥ kāraṇāt martyaḥ bhoktum icchet vidhim śṛṇu sūnāyām ca gata prāṇam krītvā nyāyena bhāmini brāhmaṇa atithi pūjā artham bhoktavyam hitam icchatā bhaiṣajya kāraṇāt vyādhau khādan pāpaiḥ na lipyate pitṛ śeṣam tathā eva aśnan māṃsam na aśubham ṛcchati umā maheśvaraḥ guru pūjā katham deva kriyate dharma kāṅkṣibhiḥ guru pūjām pravakṣyāmi yathāvat tava śobhane kṛtajñānām paraḥ dharmaḥ iti veda anuśāsanam tasmāt sva guravaḥ pūjyāḥ te hi pūrva upakāriṇaḥ gurūṇām ca garīyāṃsaḥ trayaḥ lokeṣu pūjitāḥ upādhyāyaḥ pitā mātā saṃpūjyāḥ te viśeṣataḥ ye pitṛ bhrātaraḥ jyeṣṭhāḥ ye ca tasya anujāḥ tathā pituḥ pitā ca sarve te pūjanīyāḥ yathā pitā mātuḥ yā bhaginī jyeṣṭhā mātuḥ yā ca yavīyasī mātāmahī ca dhātrī ca sarvāḥ tāḥ mātaraḥ smṛtāḥ ṛtvij guruḥ pitā ca iti guravaḥ parikīrtitāḥ jyeṣṭhaḥ bhrātā narendraḥ ca mātulaḥ śvaśuraḥ tathā bhaya trātā ca bhartā ca guravaḥ te prakīrtitāḥ iti eṣa kathitaḥ svargyaḥ gurūṇām sarva saṃgrahaḥ anuvṛttim ca pūjām ca teṣām api nibodha me yena prīṇāti ca pitā tena prītaḥ prajāpatiḥ yena prīṇāti ced mātā prītāḥ syuḥ deva mātaraḥ yena prīṇāti upādhyāyaḥ brahmā tena abhipūjitaḥ na brūyāt vipriyam teṣām aniṣṭe na pravartayet vigṛhya na vadet teṣām samīpe spardhayā kva cit veda anuśāsana samam guru śāsanam iṣyate kalahān ca vivādān ca gurubhiḥ saha varjayet kaitavam parihāsān ca manyu kāma āśrayāḥ kathāḥ gurūṇām yaḥ an ahaṃvādī karoti ājñām atandritaḥ asūyām apavādān ca gurūṇām parivarjayet teṣām priya hita anveṣī bhūtvā paricaret sadā yat kuryāt puruṣasya iha guru pūjā sadā kṛtā anuvṛtteḥ vinā dharmaḥ nā asti sarva āśrameṣu api tasmāt samādṛtaḥ kāle guru vṛttim samācaret upādhyāyam pitaram mātaram ca teṣām pāpam bhrūṇahatyā viśiṣṭam umā maheśvaraḥ śarīra mala kārśya artham indriya ucchoṣaṇāya ca bahūnām uparodham tu na kuryāt ātma kāraṇāt jīva upaghātam ca tathā dhānyam saṃjīvyam iṣyate tasmāt puṇyam labhet martyaḥ svayam āhāra karśanāt umā bhuñjan na vrata ghātī syāt brāhmaṇān anumānya ca maheśvaraḥ tat aham te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi samāhitā brahmacaryam param śaucam brahmacaryam param tapaḥ saṃsparśāt atha saṃyogāt pañcadhā rakṣitam param nitya saṃrakṣaṇam tasya naiṣṭhikānām vidhīyate tat iṣyate gṛhasthānām kālam uddiśya kāraṇam umā tīrtha caryā katham deva kriyate dharma kāṅkṣibhiḥ maheśvaraḥ kāni tīrthāni lokeśa tat me śaṃsitum arhasi pāvana artham ca śauca artham brahmaṇā nirmitāḥ purā yāḥ tu loke mahā nadyaḥ tāḥ sarvāḥ tīrtha saṃjñitāḥ tāsām prāk srotasaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ saṃgamaḥ ca parasparam tāsām sāgara saṃyogaḥ variṣṭhaḥ ca iti vidyate tāsām ubhayatas kūlam yatra tatra mahā ṛṣibhiḥ samudraḥ ca mahā tīrtham pāvanam paramam śubham tasya kūla gatāḥ tīrthāḥ mahadbhiḥ ca samāplutāḥ srotasām parvatānām ca joṣitānām mahā ṛṣibhiḥ api kūpa taṭākam vā munibhiḥ sevitam priye evam tīrtha udbhavam viddhi tasya snāna vidhim śṛṇu tad manāḥ vrata bhūyiṣṭhaḥ gatvā tīrtha abhikāṅkṣayā upavāsa trayam kuryāt ekam vā niyama anvitaḥ puṇya māsa yute kāle paurṇamāsyām yathāvidhi bahis eva śuciḥ bhūtvā tat tīrtham tad manāḥ āviśet tris āplutya jala abhyāśe dattvā brāhmaṇa dakṣiṇām etat vidhānam sarveṣām tīrtham tīrtham prati priye samīpa tīrtha snānāt tu dūra tīrtham su pūjitam ādi prabhṛti śuddhasya tīrtha snānam śubham bhavet tapaḥ rtham pāpa nāśa artham śauca artham tīrtha gāhanam evam puṇyeṣu māseṣu tīrtha snānam śubham bhavet maheśvaraḥ dānāni deva pūjām ca pitṛ pūjām tathā eva ca anyāni dharma kāryāṇi sukṛtam sa upasādhanam tat sarvam śṛṇu kalyāṇi prajānām hita kāmyayā umā maheśvaraḥ loke bhūta mayam dravyam sarva sādhāraṇam tathā dātā pratigrahītā ca deyam sa upakramam tathā deśa kālau ca yat tu etat dānam ṣaḍguṇam ucyate teṣām saṃpad viśeṣān ca kīrtyamānān nibodha me ādi prabhṛti yaḥ śuddhaḥ manaḥ vāc kāya karmabhiḥ śraddhāvān āstikaḥ ca eva evam dātā praśasyate pañcayajña paraḥ nityam nirvikāra śarīravān etān pātra guṇān viddhi tādṛś pātram praśasyate evam pātreṣu nānātvam iṣyate puruṣam prati jāraḥ ca uraḥ ca ṣaṇḍaḥ ca hiṃsraḥ samayabhedakaḥ loka vighna karāḥ ca anye varjitāḥ sarvaśas priye para upaghātāt yat dravyam cauryāt vā labhyate nṛbhiḥ adharmāt atha mohāt vā bahūnām uparodhanāt tādṛśena kṛtam dharmam niṣphalam viddhi bhāmini tasmāt nyāya āgatena eva dātavyam śubham icchatā upakramam imam viddhi dātṝṇām paramam hitam dātā dānam tathā dadyāt yathā tuṣyeta tena saḥ eṣa dāna vidhiḥ śreṣṭhaḥ samāhūya tu madhyamaḥ pūrvam ca pātra tām jñātvā samāhūya nivedya ca śauca arcana samāyuktam dātavyam śraddhayā priye yācitṝṇām tu paramam ābhimukhya puraskṛtam saṃmāna pūrvam saṃgṛhya dātavyam deśa kālayoḥ pātrāṇi saṃparīkṣya eva dātā vai dāna mātrayoḥ ati śaktyā param dānam yathāśakti tu madhyamam tṛtīyam ca aparam dānam na anurūpam iva ātmanaḥ yathā saṃbhāvitam pūrvam dātavyam tat tathā eva ca puṇya kṣetreṣu yat dattam puṇya kāleṣu vā tathā umā yaḥ ca puṇyatamaḥ deśaḥ tathā kālaḥ ca śaṃsa me maheśvaraḥ giriḥ varaḥ ca tīrthāni deśa bhāgena pūjitāḥ śuklapakṣaḥ ca pakṣāṇām paurṇamāsī ca parvasu pitṛ daivata nakṣatram nirmalāḥ divasāḥ tathā pratigrahītuḥ yaḥ kālaḥ manasā kīrtitaḥ śubhe evamādiṣu kāleṣu dattam dānam mahat bhavet dātā deyam ca pātram ca upakrama yutā kriyā deśaḥ kālaḥ ca iti eṣām saṃpad śuddhiḥ prakīrtitā bhūtvā anantam nayet svargam dātāram doṣa varjitam su mahat vā api yat dānam guṇaiḥ ebhiḥ vinākṛtam umā evam guṇa yutam dānam dattam tu aphalatām vrajet maheśvaraḥ tat api asti mahābhāge narāṇām bhava doṣataḥ kṛtvā tu dharmam vidhivat paścāttāpam karoti ced ślāghayā vā yadi brūyāt vṛthā saṃsadi yat kṛtam dharma kāryam kṛtam yat ca satatam phala kāṅkṣayā evam kṛtam vā dattam vā paratra viphalam bhavet ete doṣāḥ vivarjyāḥ ca dātṛbhiḥ puṇya kāṅkṣibhiḥ sanātanam idam vṛttam sadbhiḥ ācaritam tathā anugrahaḥ pareṣām tu gṛhasthānām ṛṇam hi tat iti evam manaḥ āveśya dātavyam satatam budhaiḥ umā maheśvaraḥ tāni aham śrotum icchāmi tat me śaṃsitum arhasi annam pratiśrayaḥ dīpaḥ pānīyam tṛṇam indhanam snehaḥ gandhaḥ ca bhaiṣajyam tilāḥ ca lavaṇam tathā sāmānyam sarva varṇānām dānam śṛṇu samāhitā annam prāṇāḥ manuṣyāṇām anna daḥ prāṇa daḥ bhavet tasmāt annam viśeṣeṇa dātum icchanti mānavāḥ brāhmaṇāya abhirūpāya yaḥ dadyāt annam īpsitam nidadhāti nidhim śreṣṭham saḥ anantam pāralaukikam śrāntam adhva pariśrāntam atithim gṛham āgatam kṛtvā tu pāpakam karma yaḥ dadyāt annam arthinām brāhmaṇānām viśeṣeṇa saḥ apahanti svakam tamaḥ pitaraḥ tasya nandanti su vṛṣṭyā karṣakāḥ iva api caṇḍāla śūdrāṇām anna dānāt na garhyate kalatram pīḍayitvā api poṣayet atithīn sadā anna dānāt ca ye lokāḥ tān pravakṣyāmi anindite bhavanāni prakāśante divi teṣām mahātmanām vaiḍūrya arciḥ prakāśāni rukma rūpya nibhāni ca nānā saṃsthāna rūpāṇi nānā ratna mayāni ca candra maṇḍala śubhrāṇi kiṅkiṇī jālavanti ca taruṇa āditya varṇāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca yathā iṣṭa bhakṣya bhojyāni śayana āsanavanti ca sarva kāma phalāḥ ca atra vṛkṣāḥ bhavana saṃsthitāḥ vāpyaḥ bahvyaḥ ca kūpāḥ ca dīrghikāḥ ca sahasraśas arujāni viśokāni nityāni vividhāni ca bhavanāni viviktāni prāṇa dānām triviṣṭape tatra te su ciram kālam vihṛtya apsarasām gaṇaiḥ jāyante mānuṣe loke sarva kalyāṇa saṃyutāḥ bala saṃhanana upetāḥ nīrogāḥ cira jīvinaḥ kulīnāḥ matimantaḥ ca bhavanti anna pradāḥ narāḥ tasmāt annam viśeṣeṇa dātavyam bhūtim icchatā tasmāt tat varṇayiṣyāmi yathāvat anupūrvaśas api pāpam kṛtam krūram dattam rukmam praṇāśayet agniḥ hi devatāḥ sarvāḥ suvarṇam ca agniḥ ucyate tasmāt suvarṇa dānena dattāḥ syuḥ sarva devatāḥ tasmāt suvarṇa dātāraḥ sarvān kāmān avāpnuyuḥ ādityasya hutāśasya lokān nānāvidhān śubhān kāñcanam saṃpradāya āśu praviśanti na saṃśayaḥ alaṃkāra kṛtam ca api kevalāt praviśiṣyate sauvarṇaiḥ brāhmaṇān kāle taiḥ alaṃkṛtya bhojayet dyutim medhām vapuḥ kīrtim punar jāte labhet dhruvam tasmāt sva śaktyā dātavyam kāñcanam bhuvi mānavaiḥ atas ūrdhvam pravakṣyāmi gavām dānam anindite lokān sisṛkṣuṇā pūrvam gāvaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayaṃbhuvā vṛtti artham sarva bhūtānām tasmāt tāḥ mātaraḥ smṛtāḥ loka jyeṣṭhā loka vṛttyām pravṛttā mayi āyattāḥ soma viṣyanda bhūtāḥ saumyāḥ puṇyāḥ kāma dāḥ prāṇa dāḥ ca tasmāt pūjyāḥ puṇya kāmaiḥ manuṣyaiḥ dhenum hi dattvā nibhṛtām arogām kalyāṇa vatsām ca payasvinīm ca tāvat samāḥ svarga phalāni bhuṅkte prayacchate yaḥ kapilām sa celām kāṃsya upadohām kanaka agra śṛṅgīm putrān ca pautrān ca kulam ca sarvam antar jātāḥ krītakāḥ dyūta labdhāḥ prāṇa krītāḥ sa udakāḥ ca ojasā vā kṛcchra utsṛṣṭāḥ poṣaṇa artha āgatāḥ ca dvāraiḥ etaiḥ tāḥ pralabdhāḥ pradadyāt kṛśāya bahu putrāya śrotriyāya āhitāgnaye pradāya nīrujām dhenum lokān prāpnoti anuttamān nṛśaṃsasya kṛtaghnasya lubdhasya anṛta vādinaḥ su vastrām vastra saṃdānām soma loke mahīyate samāna vatsām yaḥ dhenum kṛṣṇām dadyāt payasvinīm su vṛttām vastra saṃdānām lokān prāpnoti apām pateḥ samāna vatsām yaḥ dhenum dadyāt gaurīm payasvinīm su vṛttām vastra saṃdānām agni loke mahīyate hiraṇya varṇām piṅga akṣīm sa vatsām kāṃsya dohanām pradāya vastrasaṃdānām yāti kaubera sadma saḥ vāyu reṇu savarṇām tu sa vatsām kāṃsya dohanām pradāya vastra saṃdānām vāyu loke mahīyate yuvānam balinam śyāmam śatena saha yūthapam gavendram brāhmaṇa indrāya bhūri śṛṅgam alaṃkṛtam ṛṣabham ye prayacchanti śrotriyāṇām mahātmanām aiśvaryam abhijāyante jāyamānāḥ punar punar gavām mūtra purīṣāṇi na udvijeta kadā cana na ca āsām māṃsam aśnīyāt goṣu bhaktaḥ sadā bhavet grāsa muṣṭim para gave dadyāt saṃvatsaram śuciḥ a kṛtvā svayam āhāram vratam tat sārvakāmikam gāvaḥ pavitram paramam goṣu lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ katham cit na avamantavyāḥ gāvaḥ lokasya mātaraḥ tasmāt eva gavām dānam viśiṣṭam iti kathyate goṣu pūjā ca bhaktiḥ ca narasya āyuṣyam āvahet atas param pravakṣyāmi bhūmi dānam mahā phalam bhūmi dāna samam dānam na kila asti iti niścayaḥ gṛha yuj kṣetra yuj vā api bhūmi bhāgaḥ pradīyate sukha bhogyam nirākrośam vāstu pūrvam prakalpya ca grahītāram alaṃkṛtya vastra puṣpa anulepanaiḥ sa bhṛtyam sa parīvāram bhojayitvā yatheṣṭataḥ yaḥ dadyāt dakṣiṇā kāle tris adbhiḥ gṛhyatām iti evam bhūmyām pradattāyām śraddhayā vīta matsaraiḥ yāvat tiṣṭhati sā bhūmiḥ tāvat tasya phalam viduḥ bhūmi daḥ svargam āruhya ramate śāśvatīḥ samāḥ acalā hi akṣayā bhūmiḥ sarvān kāmān dudhukṣati yat yat ca kurute pāpam puruṣaḥ vṛtti karśitaḥ suvarṇa rajatam vastram maṇi muktā vasūni ca sarvam etat mahā prājñe bhūmi dāne pratiṣṭhitam bhartuḥ niḥśreyase yuktāḥ tyaktātmānaḥ raṇe hatāḥ dadyāt sa kūpa śaraṇām vā api sā bhavet sarva kāma dā niṣpanna sasyām pṛthivīm yaḥ dadāti dvijanmanām brāhmaṇam vṛtta saṃpannam āhitāgnim śuci vratam grāhayitvā nijām bhūmim na yāti yama sādanam tathā bhūmi kṛtam dānam sasye sasye vivardhate yathā bījāni rohanti prakīrṇāni mahī tale tathā kāmāḥ prarohanti bhūmi dāna guṇa arjitāḥ pitaraḥ pitṛ loka sthāḥ devatāḥ ca divi sthitāḥ dīrgha āyuḥ tvam aroga tvam sphītām ca śriyam uttamām atas param pravakṣyāmi kanyā dānam yathāvidhi kanyā deyā tathā devi pareṣām ātmanaḥ api vā kanyām śuddha vrata ācārām kula rūpa samanvitām yasmai ditsati pātrāya tena api bhṛśa kāmi tām kārayitvā gṛham pūrvam dāsī dāsa paricchadaiḥ gṛha upakaraṇam ca eva śuddha dhānyena saṃyutam dāra arthine tad arhāya kanyām tām samalaṃkṛtām sa vivāham yathānyāyam prayacchet agni sākṣikam vṛtti āyatim tathā kṛtvā sat gṛhe tau niveśayet evam kṛtvā vadhū dānam tasya dānasya gauravāt punar jātau ca saubhāgyam kula vṛddhim tathā āpnuyāt vidyā dānam tathā devi pātra bhūtāya vai dadat pretyabhāve labhet martyaḥ medhām vṛddhim dhṛtim smṛtim anurūpāya śiṣyāya svām vidyām yaḥ prayacchati evam te kathitāni eva mahā dānāni bhāmini tvad priya artham mahādevi bhūyas śrotum kim icchasi umā maheśvaraḥ tila kalpa vidhim devi tvam me śṛṇu samāhitā samṛddhaiḥ a samṛddhaiḥ vā tilāḥ deyāḥ viśeṣataḥ tilāḥ pavitrāḥ pāpa ghnāḥ su puṇyāḥ iti saṃsmṛtāḥ nyāyatas tu tilān śuddhān saṃbhṛtya atha sva śaktitaḥ tila rāśim punar kuryāt parvata ābham sa ratnakam mahāntam yadi vā stokam nānā dravya samāyutam suvarṇa rajatābhyām ca maṇi muktā pravālakaiḥ alaṃkṛtya yathāyogam sa patākam sa vedikam sa bhūṣaṇam sa vastram ca śayana āsana saṃyutam prāyaśas kaumudī māse paurṇamāsyām viśeṣataḥ svayam kṛtvā upavāsam ca vṛtta śauca samanvitaḥ dadyāt pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tila rāśim sa dakṣiṇam ekasya vā bahūnām vā dātavyam bhūtim icchatā kevalam vā tilaiḥ eva bhūmau kṛtvā gavākṛtim sa ratnakam sa vastram ca puṃsā go dāna kāṅkṣiṇā śarāvān tila saṃpūrṇān sa hiraṇyān sa campakān nānā dāna phalam bhūyas śṛṇu devi samāhitā balam āyuṣyam ārogyam anna dānāt labhet naraḥ pānīya daḥ tu saubhāgyam rasa jñānam labhet naraḥ vastra dānāt vapuḥ śobhām alaṃkāram labhet naraḥ dīpa daḥ buddhi vaimalyam dyutim śobhām labhet punar rāja pīḍā vimokṣam tat chatra daḥ labhate phalam dāsī dāsa pradānāt tu bhavet karmānta bhāj naraḥ dāsī dāsam ca vividham labhet pretya guṇa anvitam yānāni vāhanam ca eva tad arhāya dadan naraḥ pāda roga parikleśāt muktaḥ ca utsāhavān bhavet vicitram ramaṇīyam ca labhate yāna vāhanam setu kūpa taṭākānām kartā tu labhate naraḥ dīrgha āyuḥ tvam ca saubhāgyam tathā pretya āyatīm śubhām yaḥ tu saṃkrama kṛt loke nadīṣu jala tāriṇām mārga kṛt satatam martyaḥ bhavet saṃtānavān punar auṣadhānām pradātā ca satatam kṛpayā anvitaḥ bhavet vyādhi vihīnaḥ ca dīrgha āyuḥ ca viśeṣataḥ anāthān poṣayet yaḥ tu kṛpaṇa andhaka paṅgukān devagoṣṭhān sabhāḥ śālāḥ bhikṣūṇām ca pratiśrayam prāsāda vāsam vividham pakṣa śobhām labhet naraḥ vividham vividha ākāram bhakṣya bhojya guṇa anvitam ramyam sadā eva go vāṭam yaḥ kuryāt labhate naraḥ pretyabhāve śubhām jātim vyādhi mokṣam tathā eva ca umā evam nānāvidham dravyam dātā kartā labhet phalam maheśvaraḥ pretyabhāve śṛṇu phalam dattasya ca kṛtasya ca dānam vā ṣaḍguṇa yutam tad arhāya yathāvidhi yathā vibhavataḥ dānam dātavyam iti mānavaiḥ buddhim āyuṣyam ārogyam kulam bhāgyam tathā āgamam rūpeṇa saptadhā bhūtvā mānuṣyam phalati dhruvam idam dattam idam me syāt iti evam phala kāṅkṣayā umā bhagavan devadeveśa viśiṣṭam yajñam ucyate maheśvaraḥ devatānām ca saṃpūjā yajñeṣu eva samāhitā yajñāḥ vedeṣu adhīnāḥ ca vedāḥ brāhmaṇa saṃyutāḥ yajña artham viddhi tat sṛṣṭam lokānām hita kāmyayā evam vijñāya tad kartā sa dāraḥ satatam dvijaḥ tasmāt vipraiḥ yathāśāstram vidhi dṛṣṭena karmaṇā brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāḥ ca eva yajña arhāḥ prāyaśas smṛtāḥ tathā kṛteṣu yajñeṣu devānām toṣaṇam bhavet tuṣṭeṣu deva saṃgheṣu yajvā yajña phalam labhet devāḥ saṃtoṣitāḥ yajñaiḥ lokān saṃvardhayanti uta na asti yajña samam dānam na asti yajña samaḥ nidhiḥ eṣā yajña kṛtā pūjā laukikīm aparām śṛṇu deva goṣṭhān hi saṃskṛtya ca utsavam yaḥ karoti vai yāgān deva upahārān ca śuciḥ bhūtvā yathāvidhi devān saṃtoṣayitvā sa devi dharmam avāpnuyāt nṛttaiḥ vādyaiḥ ca gāndharvaiḥ anyaiḥ dṛṣṭi vilobhanaiḥ teṣām bhakti kṛtena eva satkāreṇa abhipūjitāḥ tathā eva toṣam saṃyānti devi devāḥ triviṣṭape mānuṣaiḥ aparaiḥ vā api śucibhiḥ tad parāyaṇaiḥ prayataiḥ śuddha gātraiḥ tu śuddha deśe su pūjitāḥ saṃtoṣam yānti vai devāḥ bhaktaiḥ saṃpūjitāḥ tathā umā triviṣṭapa sthāḥ vai devāḥ bhūmau mānuṣa ceṣṭitam maheśvaraḥ prāṇinām tu śarīreṣu antarātmā vyavasthitaḥ ātmānam paramam devam iti viddhi śubha īkṣaṇe ātmā manaḥ vyavasthānāt sarvam vetti śubha aśubham satām manaḥ vyavasthānāt śubham bhavati vai nṛṇām tasmāt deva abhisaṃpūjā pitṛ pūjā tathā eva ca yajñāḥ ca dharma kāryāṇi guru pūjāḥ ca śobhane evam kṛtvā śubha ākāṅkṣī paratra iha ca modate umā sarveṣām pitaraḥ pūjyāḥ sarva saṃpad pradāyinaḥ maheśvaraḥ pitṛ medham pravakṣyāmi yathāvat tad manāḥ śṛṇu deśa kālau vidhānam ca tad kriyāyāḥ śubha aśubham lokeṣu pitaraḥ pūjyāḥ devatānām ca devatāḥ śucayaḥ nirmalāḥ puṇyāḥ dakṣiṇām diśam āśritāḥ yathā vṛṣṭim pratīkṣante bhūmiṣṭhāḥ sarva jantavaḥ pitaraḥ ca tathā loke pitṛ medham śubha īkṣaṇe tasya deśāḥ kurukṣetram gayā gaṅgā sarasvatī prabhāsam puṣkaram ca iti teṣu dattam mahā phalam tīrthāni saritaḥ puṇyāḥ viviktāni vanāni ca nadīnām pulināni iti deśāḥ śrāddhasya pūjitāḥ maghā prauṣṭhapadau māsau śrāddha karmaṇi pūjitau pakṣayoḥ kṛṣṇapakṣaḥ ca pūrvapakṣāt praśasyate amāvāsyām trayodaśyām navamyām pratipatsu ca tithiṣu etāsu tuṣyanti dattena iha pitāmahāḥ pūrvāhṇe śuklapakṣe vā rātrau janma dineṣu vā yugmeṣu ahaḥsu ca śrāddham na ca kurvīta paṇḍitaḥ eṣa kālaḥ mayā proktaḥ pitṛ medhasya pūjitaḥ yasmin vā brāhmaṇam pātram paśyet kālaḥ sa ca smṛtaḥ a pāṅkteyāḥ dvijāḥ varjyāḥ grāhyāḥ te paṅkti pāvanāḥ pūjayet yadi pāpiṣṭhān śrāddheṣu narakam vrajet vṛtta śruta kula upetān sa kalatrān guṇa anvitān tad arhān śrotriyān viddhi brāhmaṇān ayujaḥ śubhe tatra śrāddha kriyām paścāt ārabheta yathāvidhi trīṇi śrāddhe pavitrāṇi dauhitraḥ kutapaḥ tilāḥ trīṇi ca atra praśaṃsanti śaucam akrodham a tvarām nīla śākam gajacchāyā pavitram śrāddha karmasu tilān avakiret tatra nānā varṇān samantataḥ aśuddham pitṛ yajñeṣu tilaiḥ śudhyati śobhane nīla kāṣāya vastram ca bhinna karṇam nava vraṇam hīna aṅgam aśucim vā api varjayet tatra dūratas kukkuṭān ca varāhān ca nagnam klībam rajasvalām āyasam trapu sīsam ca śrāddha karmaṇi varjayet māṃsaiḥ prīṇanti pitaraḥ mudga māṣa yavaiḥ iha śaśa raurava māṃsena ṣaṣ māsam tṛptiḥ iṣyate vārdhrāṇasasya māṃsena tṛptiḥ dvādaśa vārṣikī ānantyāya bhavet dattam khaḍga māṃsam pitṛ kṣaye pāyasam sa tilam kṣaudram khaḍga māṃsena saṃmitam mahā śaphariṇaḥ matsyāḥ chāgaḥ vā sarva lohitaḥ kālaśākakam iti eva tad ānantyam prakīrtitam sa apūpam sa āmiṣam snigdham āhāram upakalpayet upakalpya tad āhāram brāhmaṇān arcayet tatas śmaśrukarma śiraḥsnātān samāropya āsanam kramāt su gandha mālya ābharaṇaiḥ sragbhiḥ etān vibhūṣayet alaṃkṛta upaviṣṭān tān piṇḍa āvāpam nivedayet tatas prastīrya darbhāṇām prastaram dakṣiṇā mukhaḥ samīpe tu agnīṣomābhyām pitṛbhyaḥ juhuyāt tathā tatas darbheṣu piṇḍān trīn nivapet dakṣiṇā mukham apasavyam apāṅguṣṭham nāmadheya puraskṛtam etena vidhinā dattam pitṝṇām akṣayam bhavet tatas viprān yathākāmam pūjayet niyataḥ śuciḥ sa dakṣiṇam sa saṃbhāram yathā tuṣyanti te dvijāḥ tatas nirvapaṇe vṛtte tān piṇḍān tad antaram patnīm vā madhyamam piṇḍam putra kāmaḥ hi prāśayet tṛptān utthāpya tān viprān anna śeṣam nivedayet tad śeṣam bahubhiḥ paścāt sa bhṛtyaḥ bhakṣayet naraḥ eṣa proktaḥ samāsena pitṛ yajñaḥ sanātanaḥ ahani ahani vā kuryāt māse māse atha vā punar dīrgha āyuḥ ca bhavet svasthaḥ pitṛ medhena mānavaḥ śrāddhāni puṣṭi kāmāḥ ye prakurvanti ca paṇḍitāḥ teṣām puṣṭim prajāḥ ca eva dāsyanti pitaraḥ sadā dhanyam yaśasyam āyuṣyam svargyam śatru vināśanam umā kula saṃdhāraṇam ca iti śrāddham āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ nānā jātiṣu jāyante śīghram karma vaśāt punar pitṝṇām katamaḥ deśaḥ piṇḍān aśnanti te katham anne datte mṛtānām tu katham āpyāyanam bhavet nāradaḥ evam mayā saṃśayitam bhagavan chettum arhasi etat viruddham rudrāṇyām pṛcchantyām pariṣad bhṛśam maheśvaraḥ babhūva sarvā muditā śrotum paramakam hi tat sthāne saṃśayitam devi śṛṇu kalyāṇi tattvataḥ guhyānām paramam guhyam khilānām paramam khilam yathā deva gaṇāḥ devi tathā pitṛ gaṇāḥ priye dakṣiṇasyām diśi śubhe sarve pitṛ gaṇāḥ sthitāḥ pretān uddiśya yā pūjā kriyate mānuṣaiḥ iha tena tuṣyanti pitaraḥ na pretāḥ pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ uttarasyām yathā devāḥ ramante yajña karmabhiḥ dakṣiṇasyām tathā te ca tuṣyanti vividhaiḥ makhaiḥ āpyāyitāḥ ca te śrāddhaiḥ punar āpyāyayanti tān an iṣṭvā ca pitṝn pūrvam yaḥ kriyām prakaroti ced rakṣāṃsi ca piśācāḥ ca phalam bhokṣyanti tasya tat kavyena saṃtatiḥ dṛṣṭā havye bhūtiḥ pṛthagvidhā umā maheśvaraḥ pramāṇam phala mānānām tat me śaṃsitum arhasi
